Sentence: a vājasyemām prasavaḥ śiśriye divam imā ca viśvā bhuvanāni samrāṭ \\

Sentence: b aditsantam dāpayati prajānan sa no rayiṁ sarvavīram ni yacchatu svāhā \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a vājasya nu prasava ābabhūvemā ca viśvā bhuvanāni sarvataḥ \\

Sentence: b sanemi rājā pariyāti vidvān prajām puṣṭim vardhayamāno asme svāhā \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a ādityān viṣṇuṁ sūryam brahmāṇam ca bṛhaspatiṁ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a aryamaṇam bṛhaspatim indram dānāya codaya \\

Sentence: b vācam viṣṇuṁ sarasvatīṁ savitāram ca vājinaṁ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a agne acchāvadeha naḥ prati naḥ sumanā bhava \\

Sentence: b pra no yaccha sahasrajittvaṁ hi dhanadā asi svāhā \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a pra no yacchatv aryamā pra pūṣā pra bṛhaspatiḥ \\

Sentence: b pra vāg devī dadātu naḥ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a devasya tvā savituḥ prasavṝ śvinor bāhubhyām pūṣṇo hastābhyām \\

Sentence: b sarasvatyai vāco yantur yantriye dadhāmi bṛhaspateṣ ṭvā sāmrājyenābhi ṣiñcāmy asau \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a agnir ekākṣareṇa prāṇam ud ajayat tam uj jeṣam \\

Sentence: b aśvinau dvyakṣareṇa dvipado manuṣyān ud ajayatām tān uj jeṣam \\

Sentence: c viṣṇus tryakṣareṇa trīṁl lokān ud ajayat tān uj jeṣam \\

Sentence: d somaś caturakṣareṇa catuṣpadaḥ paśūn ud ajayat tān uj jeṣam \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a pūṣā pañcākṣareṇa pañca diśa ud ajayat tā uj jeṣam \\

Sentence: b savitā ṣaḍakṣareṇa ṣaḍ ṛtūn ud ajayat tān uj jeṣam \\

Sentence: c marutaḥ saptākṣareṇa sapta grāmyān paśūn ud ajayaṁs tān uj jeṣam \\

Sentence: d bṛhaspatir aṣṭākṣareṇa gāyatrīm ud ajayat tām uj jeṣam \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a mitro navākṣareṇa trivṛtaṁ stomam ud ajayat tam uj jeṣam \\

Sentence: b varuṇo daśākṣareṇa virājam ud ajayat tām uj jeṣam \\

Sentence: c indra ekādaśākṣareṇa triṣṭubham ud ajayat tām uj jeṣam \\

Sentence: d viśve devā dvādaśākṣareṇa jagatīm ud ajayaṁs tām uj jeṣam \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a vasavas trayodaśākṣareṇa trayodaśaṁ stomam ud ajayaṁs tam uj jeṣam \\

Sentence: b rudrāś caturdaśākṣareṇa caturdaśaṁ stomam ud ajayaṁs tam uj jeṣam \\

Sentence: c ādityāḥ pañcadaśākṣareṇa pañcadaśaṁ stomam ud ajayaṁs tam uj jeṣam \\

Sentence: d aditiḥ ṣoḍaśākṣareṇa ṣoḍaśaṁ stomam ud ajayat tam uj jeṣam \\

Sentence: e prajāpatiḥ saptadaśākṣareṇa saptadaśaṁ stomam ud ajayat tam uj jeṣam \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a eṣa te nirṛte bhāgas tam juṣasva svāhā \\

Sentence: b agninetrebhyo devebhyaḥ puraḥsadbhyaḥ svāhā \\

Sentence: c yamanetrebhyo devebhyo dakṣiṇāsadbhyaḥ svāhā \\

Sentence: d viśvadevanetrebhyo devebhyo paścātsadbhyaḥ svāhā \\

Sentence: e mitrāvaruṇanetrebhyo vā marunnetrebhyo vā devebhya uttarāsadbhyaḥ svāhā \\

Sentence: f somanetrebhyo devebhyo uparisadbhyo duvasvadbhyaḥ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a ye devā agninetrāḥ puraḥsadas tebhyaḥ svāhā \\

Sentence: b ye devā yamanetrā dakṣiṇāsadas tebhyaḥ svāhā \\

Sentence: c ye devā viśvadevanetrāḥ paścātsadas tebhyaḥ svāhā \\

Sentence: d ye devā mitrāvaruṇanetrā vā marunnetrā vottarāsadas tebhyaḥ svāhā \\

Sentence: e ye devāḥ somanetrā uparisado duvasvantas tebhyaḥ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a agne sahasva pṛtanā abhimātīr apāsya \\

Sentence: b dusṭaras tarann arātīr varco dhā yajñavāhasi \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a devasya tvā savituḥ prasavṝ śvinor bāhubhyām pūṣṇo hastābhyām \\

Sentence: b upāṁśor vīryṝṇa juhomi hataṁ rakṣaḥ svāhā \\

Sentence: c rakṣasām tvā badhāya \\

Sentence: d abadhiṣma rakṣo badhiṣmāmum asau hataḥ \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a savitā tvā savānāṁ suvatām agnir gṛhapatīnāṁ somo vanaspatīnām \\

Sentence: b bṛhaspatir vāca indro jyaiṣṭhyāya rudraḥ paśubhyo mitraḥ satyo varuṇo dharmapatīnām \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a imam devā asupatnaṁ suvadhvam mahate kṣatrāya mahate jyaiṣṭhyāya mahate jānarājyāyendrasyendriyāya \\

Sentence: b imam amuṣya putram amuṣyai putram asyai viśa eṣa vo mī rājā somo smākam brāhmaṇānāṁ rājā \\\\

Sentence: c vṛṣaseno si rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram me dehi svāhā \\

Sentence: d vṛṣaseno si rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram amuṣmai dehi \\\\

Sentence: j apām garbho si rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram amuṣmai dehi \\\\

Sentence: d sūryavarcasa stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram amuṣmai datta \\

Sentence: e māndā stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram me datta svāhā \\

Sentence: f māndā stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram amuṣmai datta \\

Sentence: g vrajakṣita stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram me datta svāhā \\

Sentence: h vrajakṣita stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram amuṣmai datta \\

Sentence: i vāśā stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram me datta svāhā \\

Sentence: j vāśā stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram amuṣmai datta \\

Sentence: k śaviṣṭhā stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram me datta svāhā \\

Sentence: l śaviṣṭhā stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram amuṣmai datta \\

Sentence: m śakvarī stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram me datta svāhā \\

Sentence: n śakvarī stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram amuṣmai datta \\

Sentence: o janabhṛta stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram me datta svāhā \\

Sentence: p janabhṛta stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram amuṣmai datta \\

Sentence: q viśvabhṛta stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram me datta svāhā \\

Sentence: r viśvabhṛta stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram amuṣmai datta \\

Sentence: s āpaḥ svarāja stha rāṣṭradā rāṣṭram amumai datta \\

Sentence: t madhumatīr madhumatībhiḥ pṛcyantām mahi kṣatram kṣatriyāya vanvānāḥ \\

Sentence: u anādhṛṣṭāḥ sīdata sahaujaso mahi kṣatram kṣatriyāya dadhatīḥ \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a somasya tviṣir asi taveva me tviṣir bhūyāt \\

Sentence: b agnaye svāhā \\

Sentence: c somāya svāhā \\

Sentence: d savitre svāhā \\

Sentence: e sarasvatyai svāhā \\

Sentence: f pūṣṇe svāhā \\

Sentence: g bṛhaspataye svāhā \\

Sentence: h indrāya svāhā \\

Sentence: i ghoṣāya svāhā \\

Sentence: j ślokāya svāhā \\

Sentence: k aṁśāya svāhā \\

Sentence: l bhagāya svāhā \\

Sentence: m aryamṇe svāhā \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a pavitre stho vaiṣṇavyau \\

Sentence: b savitur vaḥ prasava ut punāmy acchidreṇa pavitreṇa sūryasya raśmibhiḥ \\

Sentence: c anibhṛṣṭam asi vāco bandhus tapojāḥ somasya dātram asi svāhā rājasvaḥ \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a sadhamādo dyumninīr āpa etā anādhṛṣṭā apasyo vasānāḥ \\

Sentence: b pastyāsu cakre varuṇaḥ sadhastham apāṁ śiśur mātṛtamāsv antaḥ \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a kṣatrasyolvam asi \\

Sentence: b kṣatrasya jarāyv asi \\

Sentence: c kṣatrasya yonir asi \\

Sentence: d kṣatrasya nābhir asi \\

Sentence: e indrasya vātraghnam \\

Sentence: f mitrasyāsi varuṇasyāsi \\

Sentence: g tvayāyam vṛtram badhet \\

Sentence: h dṛvāsi \\

Sentence: i rujāsi \\

Sentence: j kṣumāsi \\

Sentence: k pātainam prāñcam \\

Sentence: l pātainam pratyañcam \\

Sentence: m pātainam tiryañcam digbhyaḥ pāta \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a āvirmayāḥ \\

Sentence: b āvitto agnir gṛhapatiḥ \\

Sentence: c āvitta indro vṛddhaśravāḥ \\

Sentence: d āvittau mitrāvaruṇau dhṛtavratau \\

Sentence: e āvittaḥ pūṣā viśvavedāḥ \\

Sentence: f āvitte dyāvāpṛthivī viśvaśambhuvau \\

Sentence: g āvittāditir uruśarmā \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a aveṣṭā dandaśūkāḥ \\

Sentence: b prācīm ā roha gāyatrī tvāvatu rathantaraṁ sāma trivṛt stomo vasanta ṛtur brahma draviṇam \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a dakṣiṇām ā roha triṣṭup tvāvatu bṛhat sāma pañcadaśa stomo grīṣma ṛtuḥ kṣatram draviṇam \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a pratīcīm ā roha jagatī tvāvatu vairūpaṁ sāma saptadaśa stomo varṣā ṛtuḥ viḍ draviṇam \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a udīcīm ā rohānuṣṭup tvāvatu vairājaṁ sāmaikaviṁśa stomaḥ śarad ṛtuḥ phalam draviṇam \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a ūrdhvām ā roha paṅktis tvāvatu śākvararaivate sāmanī triṇavatrayastriṁśau stomau hemantaśiśirāv ṛtū varco draviṇam \\

Sentence: b pratyastam namuceḥ śiraḥ \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a somasya tviṣir asi taveva me tviṣir bhūyāt \\

Sentence: b mṛtyoḥ pāhi \\

Sentence: c ojo si saho sy amṛtam asi \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a hiraṇyarūpā uṣaso viroka ubhāv indrā udithaḥ sūryaś ca \\

Sentence: b ā rohatam varuṇa mitra gartam tataś cakṣāthām aditim ditim ca \\

Sentence: c mitro si varuṇo si \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a somasya tvā dyumnenābhiṣiñcāmy agner bhrājasā sūryasya varcasendrasyendriyeṇa \\

Sentence: b kṣatrāṇām kṣatrapatir edhy ati didyūn pāhi \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a imam devā asupatnaṁ suvadhvam mahate kṣatrāya mahate jyaiṣṭhyāya mahate jānarājyāyendrasyendriyāya \\

Sentence: b imam amuṣya putram amuṣyai putram asyai viśa eṣa vo mī rājā somo smākam brāhmaṇānāṁ rājā \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a pra parvatasya vṛṣabhasya pṛṣṭhān nāvaś caranti svasica iyānāḥ \\

Sentence: b tā āvavṛtrann adharāg udaktā ahim budhnyam anu rīyamāṇāḥ \\

Sentence: c viṣṇor vikarmaṇam asi \\

Sentence: d viṣṇor vikrāntam asi \\

Sentence: e viṣṇoḥ krāntam asi \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a prajāpate na tvad etāny anyo viśvā rūpāṇi pari tā babhūva \\

Sentence: b yatkāmās te juhumas tan no astu \\

Sentence: c ayam amuṣya pitāsāv asya pitā \\

Sentence: d vayaṁ syāma patayo rayīṇāṁ svāhā \\

Sentence: e rudra yat te krivi param nāma tasmin hutam asy ameṣṭam asi svāhā \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a indrasya vajro si \\

Sentence: b mitrāvaruṇayos tvā praśāstroḥ praśiṣā yunajmi \\

Sentence: c avyathāyai tvā svadhāyai tvāriṣṭo arjunaḥ \\

Sentence: d marutām prasavena jaya \\

Sentence: e āpāma manasā \\

Sentence: f sam indriyeṇa \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a mā ta indra te vayam turāṣāḍ ayuktāso abrahmatā vidasāma \\

Sentence: b tiṣṭhā ratham adhi yam vajrahastā raśmīn deva yuvase svaśvān \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a agnaye gṛhapataye svāhā \\

Sentence: b somāya vanaspataye svāhā \\

Sentence: c marutām ojase svāhā \\

Sentence: d indrasyendriyāya svāhā \\

Sentence: e pṛthivi mātar mā mā hiṁsīr mo aham tvām \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a haṁsaḥ śuciṣad vasur antarikṣasad dhotā vediṣad atithir duroṇasat \\

Sentence: b nṛṣad varasad ṛtasad vyomasad abjā gojā ṛtajā adrijā ṛtam bṛhat \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a iyad asyāyur asyāyur mayi dhehi yuṅṅ asi varco si varco mayi dhehi \\

Sentence: b ūrg asy ūrjam mayi dhehi \\

Sentence: c indrasya vām vīryakṛto bāhū abhyupāva harāmi \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a syonāsi suṣadāsi \\

Sentence: b kṣatrasya yonir asi \\

Sentence: c syonām ā sīda suṣadām ā sīda kṣatrasya yonim ā sīda \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a ni ṣasāda ghṛtavrato varuṇaḥ pastyāsv ā \\

Sentence: b sāmrājyāya sukratuḥ \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a abhibhūr asy etās te pañca diśaḥ kalpantām \\

Sentence: b brahmaṁs tvam brahmāsi savitāsi satyaprasavaḥ \\

Sentence: c varuṇo si satyaujāḥ \\

Sentence: d indro si viśaujāḥ \\

Sentence: e rudro si suśevaḥ \\

Sentence: f bahukāra śreyaskara bhūyaskara \\

Sentence: g indrasya vajro si tena me radhya \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a agniḥ pṛthur dharmaṇas patir juṣāṇo agniḥ pṛthur dharmaṇas patir ājyasya vetu svāhā \\

Sentence: b svāhākṛtāḥ sūryasya raśmibir yatadhvaṁ sajātānām madhyameṣṭhyāya \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a savitrā prasavitrā sarasvatyā vācā tvaṣṭrā rūpaiḥ pūṣṇā paśubhir indreṇāsme bṛhaspatinā brahmaṇā varuṇenaujasāgninā tejasā somena rājñā viṣṇunā daśamyā devatayā prasūtaḥ pra sarpāmi \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a aśvibhyām pacyasva \\

Sentence: b sarasvatyai pacyasva \\

Sentence: c indrāya sutrāmṇe pacyasva \\

Sentence: d vāyuḥ pūtaḥ pavitreṇa pratyaṅk somo atisrutaḥ \\

Sentence: e indrasya yujyaḥ sakhā \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a kuvid aṅga yavamanto vayam cid yathā dānty anupūrvam viyūya \\

Sentence: b ihehaiṣām kṛṇuhi bhojanāni ye barhiṣo namaūktim yajanti \\

Sentence: c upayāmagṛhīto sy aśvibhyām tvā sarasvatyai tvendrāya tvā sutrāmṇe \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a vayaṁ surāmam aśvinā namucāv āsure sacā \\

Sentence: b vipipānā śubhas patī indram karmasv āvatam \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a putram iva pitarāv aśvinobhendrāvathuḥ kāvyair daṁsanābhiḥ \\

Sentence: b yat surāmam vyapibaḥ śacībhiḥ sarasvatī tvā maghavann abhiṣṇak \\\\

## Paragraph: 11

Verse: 1

Sentence: a yuñjānaḥ prathamam manas tatvāya savitā dhiyam \\

Sentence: b agner jyotir nicāyya pṛthivyā adhy ābharat \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a yuktena manasā vayam devasya savituḥ save \\

Sentence: b svargyāya śaktyā \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a yuktvāya savitā devānt svaryato dhiyā divam \\

Sentence: b bṛhaj jyotiḥ kariṣyataḥ savitā pra suvāti tān \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a yuñjate mana uta yuñjate dhiyo viprā viprasya bṛhato vipaścitaḥ \\

Sentence: b vi hotrā dadhe vayunāvid eka in mahī devasya savituḥ pariṣṭutiḥ \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a yuje vām brahma pūrvyam namobhir vi śloka etu pathyṝva sūreḥ \\

Sentence: b śṛṇvantu viśve amṛtasya putrā ā ye dhāmāni divyāni tasthuḥ \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a yasya prayāṇam anv anya id yayur devā devasya mahimānam ojasā \\

Sentence: b yaḥ pārthivāni vimame sa etaśo rajāṁsi devaḥ savitā mahitvanā \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a deva savitaḥ pra suva yajñam pra suva yajñapatim bhagāya \\

Sentence: b divyo gandharvaḥ ketapūḥ ketam naḥ punātu vācas patir vācam naḥ svadatu \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a imam no deva savitar yajñam pra ṇaya devāvyaṁ sakhividaṁ satrājitam dhanajitaṁ svarjitam \\

Sentence: b ṛcā stomaṁ samardhaya gāyatreṇa rathantaram bṛhad gāyatravartani svāhā \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a devasya tvā savituḥ prasavṝ śvinor bāhubhyām pūṣṇo hastābhyām \\

Sentence: b ā dade gāyatreṇa chandasāṅgirasvat pṛthivyāḥ sadhasthād agnim purīṣyam aṅgirasvad ā bhara traiṣṭubhena chandasāṅgirasvat \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a abhrir asi nāry asi tvayā vayam agniṁ śakema khanituṁ sadhastha ā jāgatena chandasāṅgirasvat \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a hasta ādhāya savitā bibhrad abhriṁ hiraṇyayīm \\

Sentence: b agner jyotir nicāyya pṛthivyā adhy ā bhara \\

Sentence: c ānuṣṭubhena chandasāṅgirasvat \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a pratūrtam vājinn ā drava variṣṭhām anu samvatam \\

Sentence: b divi te janma paramam antarikṣe tava nābhiḥ pṛthivyām adhi yonir it \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a yuñjāthāṁ rāsabham yuvam asmin yāme vṛṣaṇvasū \\

Sentence: b agnim bharantam asmayum \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a yoge-yoge tavastaram vāje-vāje havāmahe \\

Sentence: b sakhāya indram ūtaye \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a pratūrvann ehy avakrāmann aśasto rudrasya gāṇapatyam mayobhūr ehi \\

Sentence: b urv antarikṣam vīhi \\

Sentence: c svastigavyūtir abhayāni kṛṇvan pūṣṇā sayujā saha \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a pṛthivyāḥ sadhasthād agnim purīṣyam aṅgirasvad ā bhara \\

Sentence: b agnim purīṣyam aṅgirasvad acchemaḥ \\

Sentence: c agnim purīṣyam aṅgirasvad bhariṣyāmaḥ \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a anv agnir uṣasām agram akhyad anv ahāni prathamo jātavedāḥ \\

Sentence: b anu sūryasya purutrā ca raśmīn anu dyāvāpṛthivī ā tatantha \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a āgatya vājy adhvānaṁ sarvā mṛdho vidhūnute \\

Sentence: b agniṁ sadhasthe mahati cakṣuṣā ni cikīṣate \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a ākramya vājin pṛthivīm agnim iccha rucā tvam \\

Sentence: b bhūmyā vṛktvāya no brūhi yataḥ khanema tam vayam \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a dyaus te pṛṣṭham pṛthivī sadhastham ātmāntarikṣaṁ samudro yoniḥ \\

Sentence: b vikhyāya cakṣuṣā tvam abhi tiṣṭha pṛtanyataḥ \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a ut krāma mahate saubhagāyāsmād āsthānād draviṇodā vājin \\

Sentence: b vayaṁ syāma sumatau pṛthivyā agnim khananta upasthe asyāḥ \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a ud akramīd draviṇodā vājy arvākaḥ su lokaṁ sukṛtam pṛthivyām \\

Sentence: b tataḥ khanema supratīkam agniṁ svo ruhāṇā adhi nākam uttamam \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a ā tvā jigharmi manasā ghṛtena pratikṣiyantam bhuvanāni viśvā \\

Sentence: b pṛthum tiraścā vayasā bṛhantam vyaciṣṭham annai rabhasam dṛśānam \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a ā viśvataḥ pratyañcam jigharmy arakṣasā manasā taj juṣeta \\

Sentence: b maryaśrī spṛhayadvarṇo agnir nābhimṛśe tanvā jarbhurāṇaḥ \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a pari vājapatiḥ kavir agnir havyāny akramīt \\

Sentence: b dadhad ratnāni dāśuṣe \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a pari tvāgne puram vayam vipraṁ sahasya dhīmahi \\

Sentence: b dhṛṣadvarṇam dive-dive hantāram bhaṅgurāvatām \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a tvam agne dyubhis tvam āśuśukṣaṇis tvam adbhyas tvam aśmanas pari \\

Sentence: b tvam vanebhyas tvam oṣadhībhyas tvam nṛṇām nṛpate jāyase śuciḥ \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a devasya tvā savituḥ prasavṝ śvinor bāhubhyām pūṣṇo hastābhyām \\

Sentence: b pṛthivyāḥ sadhasthād agnim purīṣyam aṅgirasvat khanāmi \\

Sentence: c jyotiṣmantam tvāgne supratīkam ajasreṇa bhānunā dīdyatam \\

Sentence: d śivam prajābhyo hiṁsantam pṛthivyā sadhasthād agnim purīṣyam aṅgirasvat khanāmaḥ \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a apām pṛṣṭham asi yonir agneḥ samudram abhitaḥ pinvamānam \\

Sentence: b vardhamāno mahāṁ ā ca puṣkare divo mātrayā varimṇā prathasva \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a śarma ca stho varma ca stho cchidre bahule ubhe \\

Sentence: b vyacasvatī sam vasāthām bhṛtam agnim purīṣyam \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a samvasāthāṁ svarvidā samīcī urasā tmanā \\

Sentence: b agnim antar bhariṣyantī jyotiṣmantam ajasram it \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a purīṣyo si viśvabharā atharvā tvā prathamo nir amanthad agne \\

Sentence: b tvām agne puṣkarād adhy atharvā nir amanthata \\

Sentence: c mūrdhno viśvasya vāghataḥ \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a tam u tvā dadhyaṅṅ ṛṣiḥ putra īdhe atharvaṇaḥ \\

Sentence: b vṛtrahaṇam puramdaram \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a tam u tvā pāthyo vṛṣā samīdhe dasyuhantamam \\

Sentence: b dhanamjayaṁ raṇe-raṇe \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a sīda hotaḥ sva u loke cikitvān sādayā yajñaṁ sukṛtasya yonau \\

Sentence: b devāvīr devān haviṣā yajāsy agne bṛhad yajamāne vayo dhāḥ \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a ni hotā hotṛṣadane vidānas tveṣo dīdivāṁ asadat sudakṣaḥ \\

Sentence: b adabdhavratapramatir vasiṣṭhaḥ sahasrambharaḥ śucijihvo agniḥ \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a saṁ sīdasva mahāṁ asi śocasva devavītamaḥ \\

Sentence: b vi dhūmam agne aruṣam miyedhya sṛja praśasta darśatam \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a apo devīr upasṛja madhumatīr ayakṣmāya prajābhyaḥ \\

Sentence: b tāsām āsthānād uj jihatām oṣadhayaḥ supippalāḥ \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a sam te vāyur mātariśvā dadhātūttānāyā hṛdayam yad vikastam \\

Sentence: b yo devānām carasi prāṇathena kasmai deva vaṣaḍ astu tubhyam \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a sujāto jyotiṣā saha śarma varūtham āsadat svaḥ \\

Sentence: b vāso agne viśvarūpaṁ samvyayasva vibhāvaso \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a ud u tiṣṭha svadhvarāvā no devyā dhiyā \\

Sentence: b dṛśe ca bhāsā bṛhatā śuśukvanir āgne yāhi suśastibhiḥ \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a ūrdhva ū ṣu ṇa ūtaye tiṣṭhā devo na savitā \\

Sentence: b ūrdhvo vājasya sanitā yad añjibhir vāghadbhir vihvayāmahe \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a sa jāto garbho asi rodasyor agne cārur vibhṛta oṣadhīṣu \\

Sentence: b citraḥ śiśuḥ pari tamāṁsy aktūn pra mātṛbhyo adhi kanikradad gāḥ \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a sthiro bhava vīḍv aṅga āśur bhava vājy arvan \\

Sentence: b pṛthur bhava suṣadas tvam agneḥ purīṣavāhaṇaḥ \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a śivo bhava prajābhyo mānuṣībhyas tvam aṅgiraḥ \\

Sentence: b mā dyāvāpṛthivī abhi śocīr māntarikṣam mā vanaspatīn \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a praitu vājī kanikradan nānadad rāsabhaḥ patvā \\

Sentence: b bharann agnim purīṣyam mā pādy āyuṣaḥ purā \\

Sentence: c vṛṣāgnim vṛṣaṇam bharann apām garbhaṁ samudriyam \\

Sentence: d agna āyāhi vītaye \\\\

Verse: 47

Sentence: a ṛtaṁ satyam ṛtaṁ satyam \\

Sentence: b agnim purīṣyam aṅgirasvad bharāmaḥ \\

Sentence: c oṣadhayaḥ prati modadhvam agnim etaṁ śivam āyantam abhy atra yuṣmāḥ \\

Sentence: d vyasyan viśvā anirā amīvā niṣīdan no apa durmatim jahi \\\\

Verse: 48

Sentence: a oṣadhayaḥ prati gṛbhṇīta puṣpavatīḥ supippalāḥ \\

Sentence: b ayam vo garbha ṛtviyaḥ pratnaṁ sadhastham āsadat \\\\

Verse: 49

Sentence: a vi pājasā pṛthunā śośucāno bādhasva dviṣo rakṣaso amīvāḥ \\

Sentence: b suśarmaṇo bṛhataḥ śarmaṇi syām agner ahaṁ suhavasya praṇītau \\\\

Verse: 50

Sentence: a āpo hi ṣṭhā mayobhuvas tā na ūrje dadhātana \\

Sentence: b mahe raṇāya cakṣase \\\\

Verse: 51

Sentence: a yo vaḥ śivatamo rasas tasya bhājayateha naḥ \\

Sentence: b uśatīr iva mātaraḥ \\\\

Verse: 52

Sentence: a tasmā aram gamāma vo yasya kṣayāya jinvatha \\

Sentence: b āpo janayathā ca naḥ \\\\

Verse: 53

Sentence: a mitraḥ saṁsṛjya pṛthivīm bhūmim ca jyotiṣā saha \\

Sentence: b sujātam jātavedasam ayakṣmāya tvā saṁ sṛjāmi prajābhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 54

Sentence: a rudrāḥ saṁsṛjya pṛthivīm bṛhaj jyotiḥ sam īdhire \\

Sentence: b teṣām bhānur ajasra ic chukro deveṣu rocate \\\\

Verse: 55

Sentence: a saṁsṛṣṭā vasubhī rudrair dhīraiḥ karmaṇyām mṛdam \\

Sentence: b hastābhyām mṛdvīm kṛtvā sinīvālī kṛṇotu tām \\\\

Verse: 56

Sentence: a sinīvālī sukapardā sukurīrā svaupaśā \\

Sentence: b sā tubhyam adite mahyokhām dadhātu hastayoḥ \\\\

Verse: 57

Sentence: a ukhām kṛṇotu śaktyā bāhubhyām aditir dhiyā \\

Sentence: b mātā putram yathopasthe sāgnim bibhartu garbha ā \\

Sentence: c makhasya śiro si \\\\

Verse: 58

Sentence: a vasavas tvā kṛṇvantu gāyatreṇa chandasāṅgirasvad dhruvāsi pṛthivy asi \\

Sentence: b dhārayā mayi prajāṁ rāyaspoṣam gaupatyaṁ suvīryaṁ sajātān yajamānāya \\

Sentence: c rudrās tvā kṛṇvantu traiṣṭubhena chandasāṅgirasvad dhruvāsy antarikṣam asi \\

Sentence: d dhārayā mayi prajāṁ rāyaspoṣam gaupatyaṁ suvīryaṁ sajātān yajamānāya \\

Sentence: e ādityās tvā kṛṇvantu jāgatena chandasāṅgirasvad dhruvāsi dyaur asi \\

Sentence: f dhārayā mayi prajāṁ rāyaspoṣam gaupatyaṁ suvīryaṁ sajātān yajamānāya \\

Sentence: g viśve tvā devā vaiśvānarāḥ kṛṇvantv ānuṣṭubhena chandasāṅgirasvad dhruvāsi diśo si \\

Sentence: h dhārayā mayi prajāṁ rāyaspoṣam gaupatyaṁ suvīryaṁ sajātān yajamānāya \\\\

Verse: 59

Sentence: a adityai rāmnāsi \\

Sentence: b aditiṣ ṭe bilam gṛbhṇātu \\

Sentence: c kṛtvāya sā mahīm ukhām mṛnmayīm yonim agnaye \\

Sentence: d putrebhyaḥ prāyacchad aditiḥ śrapayān iti \\\\

Verse: 60

Sentence: a vasavas tvā dhūpayantu gāyatreṇa chandasāṅgirasvat \\

Sentence: b rudrās tvā dhūpayantu traiṣṭubhena chandasāṅgirasvat \\

Sentence: c ādityās tvā dhūpayantu jāgatena chandasāṅgirasvat \\

Sentence: d viśve tvā devā vaiśvānarā dhūpayantv ānuṣṭubhena chandasāṅgirasvat \\

Sentence: e indras tvā dhūpayatu \\

Sentence: f varuṇas tvā dhūpayatu \\

Sentence: g viṣṇu tvā dhūpayatu \\\\

Verse: 61

Sentence: a aditiṣ ṭvā devī viśvadevyāvatī pṛthivyāḥ sadhasthe aṅgirasvat khanatv avaṭa \\

Sentence: b devānām tvā patnīr devīr viśvadevyāvatīḥ pṛthivyāḥ sadhasthe aṅgirasvad dadhatūkhe \\

Sentence: c dhiṣaṇās tvā devīr viśvadevyāvatīḥ pṛthivyāḥ sadhasthe aṅgirasvad abhīndhatām ukhe \\

Sentence: d varūtrīṣ ṭvā devīr viśvadevyāvatīḥ pṛthivyāḥ sadhasthe aṅgirasvac chrapayantūkhe \\

Sentence: e gnās tvā devīr viśvadevyāvatīḥ pṛthivyāḥ sadhasthe aṅgirasvat pacantūkhe \\

Sentence: f janayas tvācchinnapatrā devīr viśvadevyāvatīḥ pṛthivyāḥ sadhasthe aṅgirasvat pacantūkhe \\\\

Verse: 62

Sentence: a mitrasya carṣaṇīdhṛto vo devasya sānasi \\

Sentence: b dyumnam citraśravastamam \\\\

Verse: 63

Sentence: a devas tvā savitodvapatu supāṇiḥ svaṅguriḥ subāhur uta śaktyā \\

Sentence: b avyathamānā pṛthivyām āśā diśa ā pṛṇa \\\\

Verse: 64

Sentence: a utthāya bṛhatī bhavod u tiṣṭha dhrutvā tvam \\

Sentence: b mitraitām ta ukhām pari dadāmy abhittyā eṣā mā bhedi \\\\

Verse: 65

Sentence: a vasavas tvā chṛndantu gāyatreṇa chandasāṅgirasvat \\

Sentence: b rudrās tvā chṛndantu traiṣṭubhena chandasāṅgirasvat \\

Sentence: c ādityās tvā chṛndantu jāgatena chandasāṅgirasvat \\

Sentence: d viśve tvā devā vaiśvānarā ā chṛndantv ānuṣṭubhena chandasāṅgirasvat \\\\

Verse: 66

Sentence: a ākūtim agnim prayujaṁ svāhā \\

Sentence: b mano medhām agnim prayujaṁ svāhā \\

Sentence: c cittam vijñātam agnim prayujaṁ svāhā \\

Sentence: d vāco vidhṛtim agnim prayujaṁ svāhā \\

Sentence: e prajāpataye manave svāhā \\

Sentence: f agnaye vaiśvānarāya svāhā \\\\

Verse: 67

Sentence: a viśvo devasya netur marto vurīta sakhyam \\

Sentence: b viśvo rāya iṣudhyati dyumnam vṛṇīta puṣyase svāhā \\\\

Verse: 68

Sentence: a mā su bhitthā mā su riṣo mba dhṛṣṇu vīrayasva su \\

Sentence: b agniś cedam kariṣyathaḥ \\\\

Verse: 69

Sentence: a dṛṁhasva devi pṛthivi svastaya āsurī māyā svadhayā kṛtāsi \\

Sentence: b juṣṭam devebhya idam astu havyam ariṣṭā tvam ud ihi yajñe asmin \\\\

Verse: 70

Sentence: a drvanna sarpirāsutiḥ pratno hotā vareṇyaḥ \\

Sentence: b sahasas putro adbhutaḥ \\\\

Verse: 71

Sentence: a parasyā adhi samvato varāṁ abhy ātara \\

Sentence: b yatrāham asmi tāṁ ava \\\\

Verse: 72

Sentence: a paramasyāḥ parāvato rohid aśva ihā gahi \\

Sentence: b purīṣyaḥ purupriyo gne tvam tarā mṛdhaḥ \\\\

Verse: 73

Sentence: a yad agne kāni kāni cid ā te dārūṇi dadhmasi \\

Sentence: b sarvam tad astu te ghṛtam taj juṣasva yaviṣṭhya \\\\

Verse: 74

Sentence: a yad atty upajihvikā yad vamro atisarpati \\

Sentence: b sarvam tad astu te ghṛtam taj juṣasva yaviṣṭhya \\\\

Verse: 75

Sentence: a ahar-ahar aprayāvam bharanto śvāyeva tiṣṭhate ghāsam asmai \\

Sentence: b rāyaspoṣeṇa sam iṣā madanto gne mā te prativeśā riṣāma \\\\

Verse: 76

Sentence: a nābhā pṛthivyāḥ samidhāne agnau rāyaspoṣāya bṛhate havāmahe \\

Sentence: b irammadam bṛhadukthyam yajatram jetāram agnim pṛtanāsu sāsahim \\\\

Verse: 77

Sentence: a yāḥ senā abhītvarīr āvyādhinīr ugaṇā uta \\

Sentence: b ye stenā ye ca taskarās tāṁs te agne pi dadhāmy āsyṝ \\\\

Verse: 78

Sentence: a daṁṣṭrābhyām malimlūn jambhyais taskarāṁ uta \\

Sentence: b hanubhyāṁ stenān bhagavas tāṁs tvam khāda sukhāditān \\\\

Verse: 79

Sentence: a ye janeṣu malimlava stenāsas taskarā vane \\

Sentence: b ye kakṣeṣv aghāyavas tāṁs te dadhāmi jambhayoḥ \\\\

Verse: 80

Sentence: a yo asmabhyam arātīyād yaś ca no dveṣate janaḥ \\

Sentence: b nindād yo asmān dhipsāc ca sarvam tam bhasmasā kuru \\\\

Verse: 81

Sentence: a saṁśitam me brahma saṁśitam vīryam balam \\

Sentence: b saṁśitam kṣatram jiṣṇu yasyāham asmi purohitaḥ \\\\

Verse: 82

Sentence: a ud eṣām bāhū atiram ud varco atho balam \\

Sentence: b kṣiṇomi brahmaṇāmitrān un nayāmi svāṁ aham \\\\

Verse: 83

Sentence: a annapate nnasya no dehy anamīvasya śuṣmiṇaḥ \\

Sentence: b pra-pra dātāram tāriṣa ūrjam no dhehi dvipade catuṣpade \\\\

## Paragraph: 12

Verse: 1

Sentence: a dṛśāno rukma urvyā vy adyaud durmarṣam āyuḥ śriye rucānaḥ \\

Sentence: b agnir amṛto abhavad vayobhir yad enam dyaur janayat suretāḥ \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a naktoṣāsā samanasā virūpe dhāpayete śiśum ekaṁ samīcī \\

Sentence: b dyāvākṣāmā rukmo antar vi bhāti devā agnim dhārayan draviṇodāḥ \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a viśvā rūpāṇi prati muñcate kaviḥ prāsāvīd bhadram dvipade catuṣpade \\

Sentence: b vi nākam akhyat savitā vareṇyo nu prayāṇam uṣaso vi rājati \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a suparṇo si garutmāṁs trivṛt te śiro gāyatram cakṣur bṛhadrathantare pakṣau \\

Sentence: b stoma ātmā chandāṁsy aṅgāni yajūṁṣi nāma \\

Sentence: c sāma te tanūr vāmadevyam yajñāyajñiyam puccham dhiṣṇyāḥ śaphāḥ \\

Sentence: d suparṇo si garutmān divam gaccha svaḥ pata \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a viṣṇoḥ kramo si sapatnahā gāyatram chanda ā roha pṛthivīm anu vi kramasva \\

Sentence: b viṣṇoḥ kramo sy abhimātihā traiṣṭubham chanda ā rohāntarikṣam anu vi kramasva \\

Sentence: c viṣṇoḥ kramo sy arātīyato hantā jāgatam chanda ā roha divam anu vi kramasva \\

Sentence: d viṣṇoḥ kramo si śatrūyato hantānuṣṭubham chanda ā roha diśo nu vi kramasva \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a akrandad agni stanayann iva dyauḥ kṣāmā rerihad vīrudhaḥ samañjan \\

Sentence: b sadyo jajñāno vi hīm iddho akhyad ā rodasī bhānunā bhāty antaḥ \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a agne bhyāvartinn abhi mā ni vartasvāyuṣā varcasā prajayā dhanena \\

Sentence: b sanyā medhayā rayyā poṣeṇa \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a agne aṅgiraḥ śatam te santv āvṛtaḥ sahasram ta upāvṛtaḥ \\

Sentence: b adhā poṣasya poṣeṇa punar no naṣṭam ā kṛdhi punar no rayim ā kṛdhi \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a punar ūrjā ni vartasva punar agna iṣāyuṣā \\

Sentence: b punar naḥ pāhy aṁhasaḥ \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a saha rayyā ni vartasvāgne pinvasva dhārayā \\

Sentence: b viśvapsnyā viśvatas pari \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a ā tvāhārṣam antar abhūr dhruvas tiṣṭhāvicācaliḥ \\

Sentence: b viśas tvā sarvā vāñchantu mā tvad rāṣṭram adhi bhraśat \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a ud uttamam varuṇa pāśam asmad avādhamam vi madhyamaṁ śrathāya \\

Sentence: b athā vayam āditya vrate tavānāgaso aditaye syāma \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a agre bṛhann uṣasām ūrdhvo asthān nirjaganvān tamaso jyotiṣāgāt \\

Sentence: b agnir bhānunā ruśatā svaṅga ā jāto viśvā sadmāny aprāḥ \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a haṁsaḥ śuciṣad vasur antarikṣasad dhotā vediṣad atithir dūroṇasat \\

Sentence: b nṛṣad varasad ṛtasad vyomasad abjā gojā ṛtajā adrijā ṛtam \\

Sentence: c bṛhat \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a sīda tvam mātur asyā upasthe viśvāny agne vayunāni vidvān \\

Sentence: b mainām tapasā mārciṣābhiśocīr antar asyāṁ śukrajyotir vi bhāhi \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a antar agne rucā tvam ukhāyāḥ sadane sve \\

Sentence: b tasyās tvaṁ harasā tapan jātavedaḥ śivo bhava \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a śivo bhūtvā mahyam agne atho sīda śivas tvam \\

Sentence: b śivaḥ kṛtvā diśaḥ sarvāḥ svam yonim ihāsadaḥ \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a divas pari prathamam jajñe agnir asmād dvitīyam pari jātavedāḥ \\

Sentence: b tṛtīyam apsu nṛmaṇā ajasram indhāna enam jarate svādhīḥ \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a vidmā te agne tredhā trayāṇi vidmā te dhāma vibhṛtā purutrā \\

Sentence: b vidmā te nāma paramam guhā yad vidmā tam utsam yata ājagantha \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a samudre tvā nṛmaṇā apsv antar nṛcakṣā īdhe divo agna ūdhan \\

Sentence: b tṛtīye tvā rajasi tasthivāṁsam apām upasthe mahiṣā avardhan \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a akrandad agni stanayann iva dyauḥ kṣāmā rerihad vīrudhaḥ samañjan \\

Sentence: b sadyo jajñāno vi hīm iddho akhyad ā rodasī bhānunā bhāty antaḥ \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a śrīṇām udāro dharuṇo rayīṇām manīṣāṇām prārpaṇaḥ somagopāḥ \\

Sentence: b vasuḥ sūnuḥ sahaso apsu rājā vi bhāty agra uṣasām idhānaḥ \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a viśvasya ketur bhuvanasya garbha ā rodasī apṛṇāj jāyamānaḥ \\

Sentence: b vīḍum cid adrim abhinat parāyañ janā yad agnim ayajanta pañca \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a uśik pāvako aratiḥ sumedhā martyeṣv agnir amṛto ni dhāyi \\

Sentence: b iyarti dhūmam aruṣam bharibhrad uc chukreṇa śociṣā dyām inakṣan \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a dṛśāno rukma urvyā vy adyaud durmarṣam āyuḥ śriye rucānaḥ \\

Sentence: b agnir amṛto abhavad vayobhir yad enam dyaur janayat suretāḥ \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a yas te adya kṛṇavad bhadraśoce pūpam deva ghṛtavantam agne \\

Sentence: b pra tam naya prataram vasyo acchābhi sumnam devabhaktam yaviṣṭha \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a ā tam bhaja sauśravaseṣv agna uktha-uktha ā bhaja śasyamāne \\

Sentence: b priyaḥ sūrye priyo agnā bhavāty uj jātena bhinadad uj janitvaiḥ \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a tvām agne yajamānā anu dyūn viśvā vasu dadhire vāryāṇi \\

Sentence: b tvayā saha draviṇam icchamānā vrajam gomantam uśijo vi vavruḥ \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a astāvy agnir narāṁ suśevo vaiśvānara ṛṣibhiḥ somagopāḥ \\

Sentence: b adveṣe dyāvāpṛthivī huvema devā dhatta rayim asme suvīram \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a samidhāgnim duvasyata ghṛtair bodhayatātithim \\

Sentence: b āsmin havyā juhotana \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a ud u tvā viśve devā agne bharantu cittibhiḥ \\

Sentence: b sa no bhava śivas tvaṁ supratīko vibhāvasuḥ \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a pred agne jyotiṣmān yāhi śivebhir arcibhiṣ ṭvam \\

Sentence: b bṛhadbhir bhānubhir bhāsan mā hiṁsīs tanvā prajāḥ \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a akrandad agni stanayann iva dyauḥ kṣāmā rerihad vīrudhaḥ samañjan \\

Sentence: b sadyo jajñāno vi hīm iddho akhyad ā rodasī bhānunā bhāty antaḥ \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a pra-prāyam agnir bharatasya śṛṇve vi yat sūryo na rocate bṛhad bhāḥ \\

Sentence: b abhi yaḥ pūrum pṛtanāsu tasthau dīdāya daivyo atithiḥ śivo naḥ \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a āpo devīḥ prati gṛbhṇīta bhasmaitat syone kṛṇudhvaṁ surabhā loke \\

Sentence: b tasmai namantām janayaḥ supatnīr māteva putram bibhṛtāpsv ṝnat \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a apsv agne sadhiṣ ṭava sauṣadhīr anu rudhyase \\

Sentence: b garbhe san jāyase punaḥ \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a garbho asy oṣadhīnām garbho vanaspatīnām \\

Sentence: b garbho viśvasya bhūtasyāgne garbho apām asi \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a prasadya bhasmanā yonim apaś ca pṛthivīm agne \\

Sentence: b saṁsṛjya mātṛbhiṣ ṭvam jyotiṣmān punar āsadaḥ \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a punar āsadya sadanam apaś ca pṛthivīm agne \\

Sentence: b śeṣe mātur yathopasthe ntar asyāṁ śivatamaḥ \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a punar ūrjā nivartasva punar agna iṣāyuṣā \\

Sentence: b punar naḥ pāhy aṁhasaḥ \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a saha rayyā ni vartasvāgne pinvasva dhārayā \\

Sentence: b viśvapsnyā viśvatas pari \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a bodhā me asya vacaso yaviṣṭha maṁhiṣṭhasya prabhṛtasya svadhāvaḥ \\

Sentence: b pīyati tvo anu tvo gṛṇāti vandāruṣ ṭe tanvam vande agne \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a sa bodhi sūrir maghavā vasupate vasudāvan \\

Sentence: b yuyodhy asmad dveṣāṁsi \\

Sentence: c viśvakarmaṇe svāhā \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a punas tvādityā rudrā vasavaḥ sam indhatām punar brahmāṇo vasunītha yajñaiḥ \\

Sentence: b ghṛtena tvam tanvam vardhayasva satyāḥ santu yajamānasya kāmāḥ \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a apeta vīta vi ca sarpatāto ye tra stha purāṇā ye ca nūtanāḥ \\

Sentence: b adād yamo vasānam pṛthivyā akrann imam pitaro lokam asmai \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a samjñānam asi \\

Sentence: b kāmadharaṇam \\

Sentence: c mayi te kāmadharaṇam bhūyāt \\

Sentence: d agner bhasmāsy agneḥ purīṣam asi \\

Sentence: e cita stha paricita ūrdhvacitaḥ śnayadhvam \\\\

Verse: 47

Sentence: a ayaṁ so agnir yasmint somam indraḥ sutam dadhe jaṭhare vāvaśānaḥ \\

Sentence: b sahasriṇam vājam atyam na saptiṁ sasavānt sant stūyase jātavedaḥ \\\\

Verse: 48

Sentence: a agne yat te divi varcaḥ pṛthivyām yad oṣadhīṣv apsv ā yajatra \\

Sentence: b yenāntarikṣam urv ātatantha tveṣaḥ sa bhānur arṇavo nṛcakṣāḥ \\\\

Verse: 49

Sentence: a agne divo arṇam acchā jigāsy acchā devāṁ ūciṣe dhiṣṇyā ye \\

Sentence: b yā rocane parastāt sūryasya yāś cāvastād upatiṣṭhanta āpaḥ \\\\

Verse: 50

Sentence: a purīṣyāso agnayaḥ prāvaṇebhiḥ sajoṣasaḥ \\

Sentence: b juṣantām yajñam adruho namīvā iṣo mahīḥ \\\\

Verse: 51

Sentence: a iḍām agne purudaṁsaṁ sanim goḥ śaśvattamaṁ havamānāya sādha \\

Sentence: b syān naḥ sūnus tanayo vijāvāgne sā te sumatir bhūtv asme \\\\

Verse: 52

Sentence: a ayam te yonir ṛtviyo yato jāto arocathāḥ \\

Sentence: b tam jānann agna ā rohāthā no vardhayā rayim \\\\

Verse: 53

Sentence: a cid asi tayā devatayāṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda \\

Sentence: b paricid asi tayā devatayāṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda \\\\

Verse: 54

Sentence: a lokam pṛṇa chidram pṛṇātho sīda dhruvā tvam \\

Sentence: b indrāgnī tvā bṛhaspatir asmin yonāv asīṣadan \\\\

Verse: 55

Sentence: a tā asya sūdadohasaḥ somaṁ śrīṇanti pṛśnayaḥ \\

Sentence: b janman devānām viśas triṣv ā rocane divaḥ \\\\

Verse: 56

Sentence: a indram viśvā avīvṛdhant samudravyacasam giraḥ \\

Sentence: b rathītamaṁ rathīnām vājānāṁ satpatim patim \\\\

Verse: 57

Sentence: a samitaṁ sam kalpethāṁ sampriyau rociṣṇū sumanasyamānau \\

Sentence: b iṣam ūrjam abhi samvasānau \\\\

Verse: 58

Sentence: a sam vām manāṁsi sam vratā sam u cittāny ākaram \\

Sentence: b agne purīṣyādhipā bhava tvam na iṣam ūrjam yajamānāya dhehi \\\\

Verse: 59

Sentence: a agne tvam purīṣyo rayimān puṣṭimāṁ asi \\

Sentence: b śivāḥ kṛtvā diśaḥ sarvāḥ yonim ihāsadaḥ \\\\

Verse: 60

Sentence: a bhavatam naḥ samanasau sacetasāv arepasau \\

Sentence: b mā yajñaṁ hiṁsiṣṭam mā yajñapatim jātavedasau śivau bhavatam adya naḥ \\\\

Verse: 61

Sentence: a māteva putram pṛthivī purīṣyam agniṁ sve yonāv abhār ukhā \\

Sentence: b tām viśvair devair ṛtubhiḥ samvidānaḥ prajāpatir viśvakarmā vi muñcatu \\\\

Verse: 62

Sentence: a asunvantam ayajamānam iccha stenasyetyām anv ihi taskarasya \\

Sentence: b anyam asmad iccha sā ta ityā namo devi nirṛte tubhyam astu \\\\

Verse: 63

Sentence: a namaḥ su te nirṛte tigmatejo yasmayam vi cṛtā bandham etam \\

Sentence: b yamena tvam yamyā samvidānottame nāke adhi rohayainam \\\\

Verse: 64

Sentence: a yasyās te ghora āsan juhomy eṣām bandhānām avasarjanāya \\

Sentence: b yām tvā jano bhūmir iti pramandate nirṛtim tvāham pari veda viśvataḥ \\\\

Verse: 65

Sentence: a yam te devī nirṛtir ābabandha pāśam grīvāsv avicṛtyam \\

Sentence: b tam te vi ṣyāmy āyuṣo na madhyād athaitam pitum addhi prasūtaḥ \\

Sentence: c namo bhūtyai yedam cakāra \\\\

Verse: 66

Sentence: a niveśanaḥ samgamano vasunām viśvā rūpābhi caṣṭe śacībhiḥ \\

Sentence: b deva iva savitā satyadharmendro na tasthau samare pathīnām \\\\

Verse: 67

Sentence: a sīrā yuñjanti kavayo yugā vi tanvate pṛthak \\

Sentence: b dhīrā deveṣu sūmnayā \\\\

Verse: 68

Sentence: a yunakta sīrā vi yugā tanudhvam kṛte yonau vapateha bījam \\

Sentence: b girā ca śruṣṭiḥ sabharā asan no nedīya it sṛṇyaḥ pakvam eyāt \\\\

Verse: 69

Sentence: a śunaṁ su phālā vi kṛṣantu bhūmiṁ śunam kīnāśā abhi yantu vāhaiḥ \\

Sentence: b śunāsīrā haviṣā tośamānā supippalā oṣadhīḥ kartanāsme \\\\

Verse: 70

Sentence: a ghṛtena sītā madhunā sam ajyatām viśvair devair anumatā marudbhiḥ \\

Sentence: b ūrjasvatī payasā pinvamānāsmānt sīte payasābhyā vavṛtsva \\\\

Verse: 71

Sentence: a lāṅgalam pavīravat suśevaṁ somapitsaru \\

Sentence: b tad ud vapati gām avim prapharvyam ca pīvarīm prasthāvad rathavāhanam \\\\

Verse: 72

Sentence: a kāmam kāmadughe dhukṣva mitrāya varuṇāya ca \\

Sentence: b indrāyāśvibhyām pūṣṇe prajābhya oṣadhībhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 73

Sentence: a vi mucyadhvam aghnyā devayānā aganma tamasas pāram asya jyotir āpāma \\\\

Verse: 74

Sentence: a sajūr abdo ayavobhiḥ sajūr uṣā aruṇībhiḥ sajoṣasāv aśvinā daṁsobhiḥ sajūḥ sūra etaśena sajūr vaiśvānara iḍayā ghṛtena svāhā \\\\

Verse: 75

Sentence: a yā oṣadhīḥ pūrvā jātā devebhyas triyugam purā \\

Sentence: b manai nu babhrūṇām ahaṁ śatam dhāmāni sapta ca \\\\

Verse: 76

Sentence: a śatam vo amba dhāmāni sahasram uta vo ruhaḥ \\

Sentence: b adhā śatakratvo yūyam imam me agadam kṛta \\\\

Verse: 77

Sentence: a oṣadhīḥ prati modadhvam puṣpavatīḥ prasūvarīḥ \\

Sentence: b aśvā iva sajitvarīr vīrudhaḥ pārayiṣṇvaḥ \\\\

Verse: 78

Sentence: a oṣadhīr iti mātaras tad vo devīr upa bruve \\

Sentence: b saneyam aśvam gām vāsa ātmānam tava pūruṣa \\\\

Verse: 79

Sentence: a aśvatthe vo niṣadanam parṇe vo vasatiṣ kṛtā \\

Sentence: b gobhāja it kilāsatha yat sanavatha pūruṣam \\\\

Verse: 80

Sentence: a yatrauṣadhīḥ samagmata rājānaḥ samitāv iva \\

Sentence: b vipraḥ sa ucyate bhiṣag rakṣohāmīvacātanaḥ \\\\

Verse: 81

Sentence: a aśvāvatīṁ somāvatīm ūrjayantīm udojasam \\

Sentence: b āvitsi sarvā oṣadhīr asmā ariṣṭatātaye \\\\

Verse: 82

Sentence: a uc chuṣmā oṣadhīnām gāvo goṣṭhād iverate \\

Sentence: b dhanaṁ saniṣyantīnām ātmānam tava pūruṣa \\\\

Verse: 83

Sentence: a iṣkṛtir nāma vo mātātho yūyaṁ stha niṣkṛtīḥ \\

Sentence: b sīrāḥ patatriṇī sthana yad āmayati niṣkṛtha \\\\

Verse: 84

Sentence: a ati viśvāḥ pariṣṭhā stena iva vrajam akramuḥ \\

Sentence: b oṣadhīḥ prācucyavur yat kim ca tanvo rapaḥ \\\\

Verse: 85

Sentence: a yad imā vājayann aham oṣadhīr hasta ādadhe \\

Sentence: b ātmā yakṣmasya naśyati purā jīvagṛbho yathā \\\\

Verse: 86

Sentence: a yasyauṣadhīḥ prasarpathāṅgam-aṅgam paruṣ-paruḥ \\

Sentence: b tato yakṣmam vi bādhadhva ugro madhyamaśīr iva \\\\

Verse: 87

Sentence: a sākam yakṣma prapata cāṣeṇa kikidīvinā \\

Sentence: b sākam vātasya dhrājyā sākam naśya nihākayā \\\\

Verse: 88

Sentence: a anyā vo anyām avatv anyānyasyā upāvata \\

Sentence: b tāḥ sarvāḥ samvidānā idam me prāvatā vacaḥ \\\\

Verse: 89

Sentence: a yāḥ phalinīr yā aphalā apuṣpā yāś ca puṣpiṇīḥ \\

Sentence: b bṛhaspatiprasūtās tā no muñcantv aṁhasaḥ \\\\

Verse: 90

Sentence: a muñcantu mā śapathyād atho varuṇyād uta \\

Sentence: b atho yamasya paḍvīśāt sarvasmād devakilviṣāt \\\\

Verse: 91

Sentence: a avapatantīr avadan diva oṣadhayas pari \\

Sentence: b yam jīvam aśnavāmahai na sa riṣyāti pūruṣaḥ \\\\

Verse: 92

Sentence: a yā oṣadhīḥ somarājñīr bahvīḥ śatavicakṣaṇāḥ \\

Sentence: b tāsām asi tvam uttamāram kāmāya śaṁ hṛde \\\\

Verse: 93

Sentence: a yā oṣadhīḥ somarājñīr viṣṭhitāḥ pṛthivīm anu \\

Sentence: b bṛhaspatiprasūtā asyai sam datta vīryam \\\\

Verse: 94

Sentence: a yāś cedam upaśṛṇvanti yāś ca dūram parāgatāḥ \\

Sentence: b sarvāḥ samgatya vīrudho syai sam datta vīryam \\\\

Verse: 95

Sentence: a mā vo riṣat khanitā yasmai cāham khanāmi vaḥ \\

Sentence: b dvipāc catuṣpād asmākaṁ sarvam astv anāturam \\\\

Verse: 96

Sentence: a oṣadhayaḥ sam avadanta somena saha rājñā \\

Sentence: b yasmai kṛṇoti brāhmaṇas taṁ rājan pārayāmasi \\\\

Verse: 97

Sentence: a nāśayitrī balāsasyārśasa upacitām asi \\

Sentence: b atho śatasya yakṣmāṇām pākāror asi nāśanī \\\\

Verse: 98

Sentence: a tvām gandharvā akhanaṁs tvām indras tvām bṛhaspatiḥ \\

Sentence: b tvām oṣadhe somo rājā vidvān yakṣmād amucyata \\\\

Verse: 99

Sentence: a sahasva me arātīḥ sahasva pṛtanāyataḥ \\

Sentence: b sahasva sarvam pāpmānaṁ sahamānāsy oṣadhe \\\\

Verse: 100

Sentence: a dīrghāyus ta oṣadhe khanitā yasmai ca tvā khanāmy aham \\

Sentence: b atho tvam dīrghāyur bhūtvā śatavalśā vi rohatāt \\\\

Verse: 101

Sentence: a tvam uttamāsy oṣadhe tava vṛkṣā upastayaḥ \\

Sentence: b upastir astu so smākam yo asmāṁ abhidāsati \\\\

Verse: 102

Sentence: a mā mā hiṁsīj janitā yaḥ pṛthivyā yo vā divaṁ satyadharmā vyānaṭ \\

Sentence: b yaś cāpaś candrāḥ prathamo jajāna kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema \\\\

Verse: 103

Sentence: a abhyā vartasva pṛthivi yajñena payasā saha \\

Sentence: b vapām te agnir iṣito arohat \\\\

Verse: 104

Sentence: a agne yat te śukram yac candram yat pūtam yac ca yajñiyam \\

Sentence: b tad devebhyo bharāmasi \\\\

Verse: 105

Sentence: a iṣam ūrjam aham ita ādam ṛtasya yonim mahiṣasya dhārām \\

Sentence: b ā mā goṣu viśatvā tanūṣu jahāmi sedim anirām amīvām \\\\

Verse: 106

Sentence: a agne tava śravo vayo mahi bhrājante arcayo vibhāvaso \\

Sentence: b bṛhadbhāno śavasā vājam ukthyam dadhāsi dāśuṣe kave \\\\

Verse: 107

Sentence: a pāvakavarcāḥ śukravarcā anūnavarcā ud iyarṣi bhānunā \\

Sentence: b putro mātarā vicarann upāvasi pṛṇakṣi rodasī ubhe \\\\

Verse: 108

Sentence: a ūrjo napāj jātavedaḥ suśastibhir mandasva dhītibhir hitaḥ \\

Sentence: b tve iṣaḥ sam dadhur bhūrivarpasaś citrotayo vāmajātāḥ \\\\

Verse: 109

Sentence: a irajyann agne prathayasva jantubhir asme rāyo amartya \\

Sentence: b sa darśatasya vapuṣo vi rājasi pṛṇakṣi sānasim kratum \\\\

Verse: 110

Sentence: a iṣkartāram adhvarasya pracetasam kṣayantaṁ rādhaso mahaḥ \\

Sentence: b rātim vāmasya subhagām mahīm iṣam dadhāsi sānasiṁ rayim \\\\

Verse: 111

Sentence: a ṛtāvānam mahiṣam viśvadarśatam agniṁ sumnāya dadhire puro janāḥ \\

Sentence: b śrutkarṇaṁ saprathastamam tvā girā daivyam mānuṣā yugā \\\\

Verse: 112

Sentence: a ā pyāyasva sam etu te viśvataḥ soma vṛṣṇyam \\

Sentence: b bhavā vājasya samgathe \\\\

Verse: 113

Sentence: a sam te payāṁsi sam u yantu vājāḥ sam vṛṣṇyāny abhimātiṣāhaḥ \\

Sentence: b āpyāyamāno amṛtāya soma divi śravāṁsy uttamāni dhiṣva \\\\

Verse: 114

Sentence: a ā pyāyasva madintama soma viśvebhir aṁśubhiḥ \\

Sentence: b bhavā naḥ suśravastamaḥ sakhā vṛdhe \\\\

Verse: 115

Sentence: a ā te vatso mano yamat paramāc cit sadhasthāt \\

Sentence: b agne tvāmkāmayā girā \\\\

Verse: 116

Sentence: a tubhyam tā aṅgirastama viśvāḥ sukṣitayaḥ pṛthak \\

Sentence: b agne kāmāya yemire \\\\

Verse: 117

Sentence: a agniḥ priyeṣu dhāmasu kāmo bhūtasya bhavyasya \\

Sentence: b samrāḍ eko vi rājati \\\\

## Paragraph: 13

Verse: 1

Sentence: a mayi gṛhṇāmy agre agniṁ rāyaspoṣāya suprajāstvāya suvīryāya \\

Sentence: b mām u devatāḥ sacantām \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a apām pṛṣṭham asi yonir agneḥ samudram abhitaḥ pinvamānam \\

Sentence: b vardhamāno mahāṁ ā ca puṣkare divo mātrayā varimṇā prathasva \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a brahma jajñānam prathamam purastād vi sīmataḥ suruco vena āvaḥ \\

Sentence: b sa budhnyā upamā asya viṣṭhāḥ sataś ca yonim asataś ca vi vaḥ \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a hiraṇyagarbhaḥ sam avartatāgre bhūtasya jātaḥ patir eka āsīt \\

Sentence: b sa dādhāra pṛthivīm dyām utemām kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a drapsaś caskanda pṛthivīm anu dyām imam ca yonim anu yaś ca pūrvaḥ \\

Sentence: b samānam yonim anu samcarantam drapsam juhomy anu sapta hotrāḥ \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a namo stu sarpebhyo ye ke ca pṛthivīm anu \\

Sentence: b ye antarikṣe ye divi tebhyaḥ sarpebhyo namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a yā iṣavo yātudhānānām ye vā vanaspatīṁs tu \\

Sentence: b ye vāvaṭeṣu śerate tebhyaḥ sarpebhyo namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a ye vāmī rocane divo ye vā sūryasya raśmiṣu \\

Sentence: b yeṣām apsu sadas kṛtam tebhyaḥ sarpebhyo namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a kṛṇuṣva pājaḥ prasitim na pṛthvīm yāhi rājevāmavāṁ ibhena \\

Sentence: b tṛṣvīm anu prasitim drūṇāno stāsi vidhya rakṣasas tapiṣṭhaiḥ \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a tava bhramāsa āśuyā patanty anu spṛśa dhṛṣatā śośucānaḥ \\

Sentence: b tapūṁṣy agne juhvā pataṅgān asamdito vi sṛja viṣvag ulkāḥ \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a prati spaśo vi sṛja tūrṇitamo bhavā pāyur viśo asyā adabdhaḥ \\

Sentence: b yo no dūre aghaśaṁso yo anty agne mākiṣ ṭe vyathir ā dadharṣīt \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a ud agne tiṣṭha praty ā tanuṣva ny amitrāṁ oṣatāt tigmahete \\

Sentence: b yo no arātiṁ samidhāna cakre nīcā tam dhakṣy atasam na śuṣkam \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a ūrdhvo bhava prati vidhyādhy asmad āviṣ kṛṇuṣva daivyāny agne \\

Sentence: b ava sthirā tanuhi yātujūnām jāmim ajāmim pra mṛṇīhi śatrūn \\

Sentence: c agneṣ ṭvā tejasā sādayāmi \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a agnir mūrdhā divaḥ kakut patiḥ pṛthivyā ayam \\

Sentence: b apāṁ retāṁsi jinvati \\

Sentence: c indrasya tvaujasā sādayāmi \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a bhuvo yajñasya rajasaś ca netā yatrā niyudbhiḥ sacase śivābhiḥ \\

Sentence: b divi mūrdhānam dadhiṣe svarṣām jihvām agne cakriṣe havyavāham \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a dhruvāsi dharuṇāstṛtā viśvakarmaṇā \\

Sentence: b mā tvā samudra ud badhīn mā suparṇo vyathamānā pṛthivīm dṛṁha \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a prajāpatiṣ ṭvā sādayatv apā pṛṣṭhe samudrasyeman \\

Sentence: b vyacasvatīm prathasvatīm prathasva pṛthivy asi \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a bhūr asi bhūmir asy aditir asi viśvadhāyā viśvasya bhuvanasya dhartrī \\

Sentence: b pṛthivīm yaccha pṛthivīm dṛṁha pṛthivīm mā hiṁsīḥ \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a viśvasmai prāṇāyāpānāya vyānāyodānāya pratiṣṭhāyai caritrāya \\

Sentence: b agniṣ ṭvābhi pātu mahyā svastyā chardiṣā śamtamena tayā devatayāṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a kāṇḍāt-kāṇḍāt prarohantī paruṣaḥ-paruṣas pari \\

Sentence: b evā no dūrve pra tanu sahasreṇa śatena ca \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a yā śatena pratanoṣi sahasreṇa virohasi \\

Sentence: b tasyās te devīṣṭake vidhema haviṣā vayam \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a yās te agne sūrye ruco divam ātanvanti raśmibhiḥ \\

Sentence: b tābhir no adya sarvābhī ruce janāya nas kṛdhi \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a yā vo devāḥ sūrye ruco goṣv aśveṣu yā rucaḥ \\

Sentence: b indrāgnī tābhiḥ sarvābhī rucam no dhatta bṛhaspate \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a virāḍ jyotir adhārayat \\

Sentence: b svarāḍ jyotir adhārayat \\

Sentence: c prajāpatiṣ ṭvā sādayatu pṛṣṭhe pṛthivyā jyotiṣmatīm \\

Sentence: d viśvasmai prāṇāyāpānāya vyānāya viśvam jyotir yaccha \\

Sentence: e agniṣ ṭe dhipatis tayā devatayāṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a madhuś ca mādhavaś ca vāsantikāv ṛtū agner antaḥśleṣo si kalpetām dyāvāpṛthivī kalpantām āpa oṣadhayaḥ kalpantām agnayaḥ pṛthaṅ nama jyaiṣṭhyāya savratāḥ \\

Sentence: b ye agnayaḥ samanaso ntarā dyāvāpṛthivī ime vāsantikāv ṛtū abhikalpamānā indram iva devā abhisam viśantu tayā devatayāṅgirasvad dhruve sīdatam \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a aṣāḍhāsi sahamānā sahasvārātīḥ sahasva pṛtanāyataḥ \\

Sentence: b sahasravīryāsi sā mā jinva \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a madhu vātā ṛtāyate madhu kṣaranti sindhavaḥ \\

Sentence: b mādhvīr naḥ santv oṣadhīḥ \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a madhu naktam utoṣaso madhumat pārthivaṁ rajaḥ \\

Sentence: b madhu dyaur astu naḥ pitā \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a madhumān no vanaspatir madhumāṁ astu sūryaḥ \\

Sentence: b mādhvīr gāvo bhavantu naḥ \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a apām gambhant sīda mā tvā sūryo bhi tāpsīn māgnir vaiśvānaraḥ \\

Sentence: b acchinnapatrāḥ prajā anuvīkṣasvānu tvā divyā vṛṣṭiḥ sacatām \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a trīnt samudrānt sam asṛpat svargān apām patir vṛṣabha iṣṭakānām \\

Sentence: b purīṣam vasānaḥ sukṛtasya loke tatra gaccha yatra pūrve paretāḥ \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a mahī dyauḥ pṛthivī ca na imam yajñam mimikṣatām \\

Sentence: b pipṛtām no bharīmabhiḥ \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a viṣṇoḥ karmāṇi paśyata yato vratāni paspaśe \\

Sentence: b indrasya yujyaḥ sakhā \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a dhruvāsi dharuṇeto jajñe prathamam ebhyo yonibhyo adhi jātavedaḥ \\

Sentence: b sa gāyatryā triṣṭubhānuṣṭubhā ca devebhyo havyam vahatu prajānan \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a iṣe rāye ramasva sahase dyumna ūrje apatyāya \\

Sentence: b samrāḍ asi svarāḍ asi sārasvatau tvotsau prāvatām \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a agne yukṣvā hi ye tavāśvāso deva sādhavaḥ \\

Sentence: b aram vahanti manyave \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a yukṣvā hi devahūtamāṁ aśvāṁ agne rathīr iva \\

Sentence: b ni hotā pūrvyaḥ sadaḥ \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a samyak sravanti sarito na dhenā antar hṛdā manasā pūyamānāḥ \\

Sentence: b ghṛtasya dhārā abhi cākaśīmi hiraṇyayo vetaso madhye agneḥ \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a ṛce tvā ruce tvā bhāse tvā jyotiṣe tvā \\

Sentence: b abhūd idam viśvasya bhuvanasya vājinam agner vaiśvānarasya ca \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a agnir jyotiṣā jyotiṣmān rukmo varcasā varcasvān \\

Sentence: b sahasradā asi sahasrāya tvā \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a ādityam garbham payasā sam aṅdhi sahasrasya pratimām viśvarūpam \\

Sentence: b pari vṛṅdhi harasā mābhi maṁsthāḥ śatāyuṣam kṛṇuhi cīyamānaḥ \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a vātasya jūtim varuṇasya nābhim aśvam jajñānaṁ sarirasya madhye \\

Sentence: b śiśum nadīnāṁ harim adribudhnam agne mā hiṁsīḥ parame vyoman \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a ajasram indum aruṣam bhuraṇyum agnim īḍe pūrvacitti namobhiḥ \\

Sentence: b sa parvabhir ṛtuśaḥ kalpamāno gām mā hiṁsīr aditim virājam \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a varūtrīm tvaṣṭur varuṇasya nābhim avim jajñānāṁ rajasaḥ parasmāt \\

Sentence: b mahīṁ sāhasrīm asurasya māyām agne mā hiṁsīḥ parme vyoman \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a yo gnir agner adhiy ajāyata śokāt pṛthivyā uta vā divas pari \\

Sentence: b yena prajā viśvakarmā jajāna tam agne heḍaḥ pari te vṛṇaktu \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a citram devānām ud agād anīkam cakṣur mitrasya varuṇasyāgneḥ \\

Sentence: b āprā dyāvāpṛthivī antarikṣaṁ sūrya ātmā jagatas tasthuṣaś ca \\\\

Verse: 47

Sentence: a imam mā hiṁsīr dvipādam paśuṁ sahasrākṣo medhāya cīyamānaḥ \\

Sentence: b mayum paśum medham agne juṣasva tena cinvānas tanvo ni ṣīda \\

Sentence: c mayum te śug ṛcchatu yam dviṣmas tam te śug ṛcchatu \\\\

Verse: 48

Sentence: a imam mā hiṁsīr ekaśapham paśum kanikradam vājinam vājineṣu \\

Sentence: b gauram āraṇyam anu te diśāmi tena cinvānas tanvo ni ṣīda \\

Sentence: c gauram te śug ṛcchatu yam dviṣmas tam te śug ṛcchatu \\\\

Verse: 49

Sentence: a imaṁ sāhasraṁ śatadhāram utsam vyacyamānaṁ sarirasya madhye \\

Sentence: b ghṛtam duhānām aditim janāyāgne mā hiṁsīḥ parame vyoman \\

Sentence: c gavayam āraṇyam anu te diśāmi tena cinvānas tanvo ni ṣīda \\

Sentence: d gavayam te śug ṛcchatu yam dviṣmas tam te śug ṛcchatu \\\\

Verse: 50

Sentence: a imam ūrṇāyum varuṇasya nābhim tvacam paśūnām dvipadāmcatuṣpadām \\

Sentence: b tvaṣṭuḥ prajānām prathamam janitram agne mā hiṁsīḥ parame vyoman \\

Sentence: c uṣṭram āraṇyam anu te diśāmi tena cinvānas tanvo ni ṣīda \\

Sentence: d uṣṭram te śug ṛcchatu yam dviṣmas tam te śug ṛcchatu \\\\

Verse: 51

Sentence: a ajo hy agner ajaniṣṭa śokāt so apaśyaj janitāram agre \\

Sentence: b tena devā devatām agram āyaṁs tena roham āyann upa medhyāsaḥ \\

Sentence: c śarabham āraṇyam anu te diśāmi tena cinvānas tanvo ni ṣīda \\

Sentence: d śarabham te śug ṛcchatu yam dviṣmas tam te śug ṛcchatu \\\\

Verse: 52

Sentence: a tvam yaviṣṭha dāśuṣo nṝṁḥ pāhi śṛṇudhī giraḥ \\

Sentence: b rakṣā tokam uta tmanā \\\\

Verse: 53

Sentence: a apām tvemant sādayāmi \\

Sentence: b apām tvodmant sādayāmi \\

Sentence: c apām tvā bhasmant sādayāmi \\

Sentence: d apām tvā jyotiṣi sādayāmi \\

Sentence: e apām tvāyane sādayāmi \\

Sentence: f arṇave tvā sadane sādayāmi \\

Sentence: g samudre tvā sadane sādayāmi \\

Sentence: h sarire tvā sadane sādayāmi \\

Sentence: i apām tvā kṣaye sādayāmi \\

Sentence: j apām tvā sadhiṣi sādayāmi \\

Sentence: k apām tvā sadane sādayāmi \\

Sentence: l apām tvā sadhasthe sādayāmi \\

Sentence: m apām tvā yonau sādayāmi \\

Sentence: n apām tvā purīṣe sādayāmi \\

Sentence: o apām tvā pāthasi sādayāmi \\

Sentence: p gāyatreṇa tvā chandasā sādayāmi \\

Sentence: q traiṣṭubhena tvā chandasā sādayāmi \\

Sentence: r jāgatena tvā chandasā sādayāmi \\

Sentence: s ānuṣṭubhena tvā chandasā sādayāmi \\

Sentence: t pāṅktena tvā chandasā sādayāmi \\\\

Verse: 54

Sentence: a ayam puro bhuvaḥ \\

Sentence: b tasya prāṇo bhauvanāyaḥ \\

Sentence: c vasantaḥ prāṇyanaḥ \\

Sentence: d gāyatrī vāsantī \\

Sentence: e gāyatryai gāyatram \\

Sentence: f gāyatrād upāṁśuḥ \\

Sentence: g upāṁśos trivṛt \\

Sentence: h trivṛto rathantaram \\

Sentence: i vasiṣṭha ṛṣiḥ \\

Sentence: j prajāpatigṛhītayā tvayā prāṇam gṛhṇāmi prajābhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 55

Sentence: a ayam dakṣiṇā viśvakarmā \\

Sentence: b tasya mano vaiśvakarmaṇam \\

Sentence: c grīṣmo mānasaḥ \\

Sentence: d triṣṭub graiṣmī \\

Sentence: e triṣṭubhaḥ svāram \\

Sentence: f svārād antaryāmaḥ \\

Sentence: g antaryāmāt pañcadaśaḥ \\

Sentence: h pañcadaśād bṛhat \\

Sentence: i bharadvāja ṛṣiḥ \\

Sentence: j prajāpatigṛhītayā tvayā mano gṛhṇāmi prajābhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 56

Sentence: a ayam paścād viśvavyacāḥ \\

Sentence: b tasya cakṣur vaiśvavyacasam \\

Sentence: c varṣāś cākṣuṣyaḥ \\

Sentence: d jagatī vārṣī \\

Sentence: e jagatyā ṛksamam \\

Sentence: f ṛksamāc chukraḥ \\

Sentence: g śukrāt saptadaśaḥ \\

Sentence: h saptadaśād vairūpam \\

Sentence: i jamadagnir ṛṣiḥ \\

Sentence: j prajāpatigṛhītayā tvayā cakṣur gṛhṇāmi prajābhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 57

Sentence: a idam uttarāt svaḥ \\

Sentence: b tasya śotraṁ sauvam \\

Sentence: c śarac chrautrī \\

Sentence: d anuṣṭup śāradī \\

Sentence: e anuṣṭubha aiḍam \\

Sentence: f aiḍān manthī \\

Sentence: g manthina ekaviṁśaḥ \\

Sentence: h ekaviṁśād vairājam \\

Sentence: i viśvāmitra ṛṣiḥ \\

Sentence: j prajāpatigṛhītayā tvayā śrotram gṛhṇāmi prajābhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 58

Sentence: a iyam upari matiḥ \\

Sentence: b tasyai vāṅ mātyā \\

Sentence: c hemanto vācyaḥ \\

Sentence: d paṅktir haimantī \\

Sentence: e paṅktyai nidhanavat \\

Sentence: f nidhanavata āgrayaṇaḥ \\

Sentence: g āgrayaṇāt triṇavatrayastriṁśau \\

Sentence: h triṇavatrayastriṁśābhyāṁ śākvararaivate \\

Sentence: i viśvakarma ṛṣiḥ \\

Sentence: j prajāpatigṛhītayā tvayā vācam gṛhṇāmi prajābhyaḥ \\

Sentence: k lokam pṛṇa chidram pṛṇātho sīda dhruvā tvam \\

Sentence: l indrāgnī tvā bṛhaspatir asmin yonāv asīṣadan \\

Sentence: m tā asya sūdadohasaḥ somaṁ śrīṇanti pṛśnayaḥ \\

Sentence: n janman devānām viśas triṣv ā rocane divaḥ \\

Sentence: o indram viśvā avīvṛdhant samudravyacasam giraḥ \\

Sentence: p rathītamaṁ rathīnām vājānāṁ satpatim patim \\\\

## Paragraph: 14

Verse: 1

Sentence: a dhruvakṣitir dhruvayonir dhruvāsi dhruvam yonim ā sīda sādhuyā \\

Sentence: b ukhyasya ketum prathamam juṣāṇā \\

Sentence: c aśvinādhvaryū sādayatām iha tvā \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a kulāyinī ghṛtavatī puramdhiḥ syone sīda sadane pṛthivyāḥ \\

Sentence: b abhi tvā rudrā vasavo gṛṇantv imā brahma pīpihi saubhagāya \\

Sentence: c aśvinādhvaryū sādayatām iha tvā \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a svair dakṣair dakṣapiteha sīda devānāṁ sumne bṛhate raṇāya \\

Sentence: b pitevaidhi sūnava ā suśevā svāveśā tanvā sam viśasva \\

Sentence: c aśvinādhvaryū sādayatām iha tvā \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a pṛthivyāḥ purīṣam asy apso nāma tām tvā viśve abhi gṛṇantu devāḥ \\

Sentence: b stomapṛṣṭhā ghṛtavatīha sīda prajāvad asme darviṇā yajasva \\

Sentence: c aśvinādhvaryū sādayatām iha tvā \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a adityās tvā pṛṣṭhe sādayāmy antarikṣasya dhartrīm viṣṭambhanīm diśāmadhipatnīm bhuvanānām ūrmir drapso apām asi viśvakarmā ta ṛṣiḥ \\

Sentence: b aśvinādhvaryū sādayatām iha tvā \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a śukraś ca śuciś ca graiṣmāv ṛtū agner antaḥśleṣo si kalpetām dyāvāpṛthivī kalpantām āpa oṣadhayaḥ kalpantām agnayaḥ pṛthaṅ nama jyaiṣṭhyāya savratāḥ \\

Sentence: b ye agnayaḥ samanaso ntarā dyāvāpṛthivī ime graiṣmāv ṛtū abhikalpamānā indram iva devā abhisam viśantu tayā devatayāṅgirasvad dhruve sīdatam \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a sajūr ṛtubhiḥ sajūr vidhābhiḥ sajūr devaiḥ sajūr devair vayonādhair agnaye tvā vaiśvānarāyāśvinādhvaryū sādayatām iha tvā \\

Sentence: b sajūr ṛtubhiḥ sajūr vidhābhiḥ sajūr vasubhiḥ sajūr devair vayonādhair agnaye tvā vaiśvānarāyāśvinādhvaryū sādayatām iha tvā \\

Sentence: c sajūr ṛtubhiḥ sajūr vidhābhiḥ sajū rudraiḥ sajūr devair vayonādhair agnaye tvā vaiśvānarāyāśvinādhvaryū sādayatām iha tvā \\

Sentence: d sajūr ṛtubhiḥ sajūr vidhābhiḥ sajūr ādityaiḥ sajūr devair vayonādhair agnaye tvā vaiśvānarāyāśvinādhvaryū sādayatām iha tvā \\

Sentence: e sajūr ṛtubhiḥ sajūr vidhābhiḥ sajūr viśvair devaiḥ sajūr devair vayonādhair agnaye tvā vaiśvānarāyāśvinādhvaryū sādayatām iha tvā \\\\

Sentence: a mūrdhā vayaḥ prajāpatiś chandaḥ \\

Sentence: b kṣatram vayo mayamdam chandaḥ \\

Sentence: c viṣṭambho vayo dhipatiś chandaḥ \\

Sentence: d viśvakarmā vayaḥ parameṣṭhī chandaḥ \\

Sentence: h vyāghro vayo nādhṛṣṭam chandaḥ \\

Sentence: i siṁho vayaś chadiś chandaḥ \\

Sentence: j paṣṭhavāḍ vayo bṛhatī chandaḥ \\

Sentence: k ukṣā vayaḥ kakup chandaḥ \\

Sentence: l ṛṣabho vayaḥ satobṛhatī chandaḥ \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a anaḍvān vayaḥ paṅktiś chandaḥ \\

Sentence: b dhenur vayo jagatī chandaḥ \\

Sentence: c tryavir vayas triṣṭup chandaḥ \\

Sentence: d dityavāḍ vayo virāṭ chandaḥ \\

Sentence: e pañcāvir vayo gāyatrī chandaḥ \\

Sentence: f trivatso vaya uṣṇik chandaḥ \\

Sentence: g turyavāḍ vayo nuṣṭup chandaḥ \\

Sentence: h lokam pṛṇa chidram pṛṇātho sīda dhruvā tvam \\

Sentence: i indrāgnī tvā bṛhaspatir asmin yonāv asīṣadan \\

Sentence: j tā asya sūdadohasaḥ somaṁ śrīṇanti pṛśnayaḥ \\

Sentence: k janman devānām viśas triṣv ā rocane divaḥ \\

Sentence: l indram viśvā avīvṛdhant samudravyacasam giraḥ \\

Sentence: m rathītamaṁ rathīnām vājānāṁ satpatim patim \\\\

Sentence: b pṛṣṭhena dyāvāpṛthivī antarikṣam ca vibādhase \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a viśvakarmā tvā sādayatv antarik=C5asya pṛṣṭhe vyacasvatīm prathasvatīm antarikṣam yacchāntarikṣam dṛṁhāntarikṣam mā hiṁsīḥ \\

Sentence: b viśvasmai prāṇāyāpānāya vyānāyodānāya pratiṣṭhāyai caritrāya \\

Sentence: c vāyuṣ ṭvābhi pātu mahyā svastyā chardiṣā śamtamena tayā devatayāṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda \\\\

Sentence: a rājñy asi prācī dik \\

Sentence: b virāḍ asi dakṣiṇā dik \\

Sentence: c samrāḍ asi praticī dik \\

Sentence: d svarāḍ asy udīcī dik \\

Sentence: e adhipatny asi bṛhatī dik \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a viśvakarmā tvā sādayatv antarik=C5asya pṛṣṭhe jyotiṣmatīm \\

Sentence: c vāyuṣ ṭe dhipatis tayā devatayāṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda \\\\

Sentence: a āśur trivṛt \\

Sentence: b bhāntaḥ pañcadaśaḥ \\

Sentence: c vyomā saptadaśaḥ \\

Sentence: j yoniś caturviṁśaḥ \\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a agner bhāgo si dīkṣāyā ādhipatyam brahma spṛtam trivṛt stomaḥ \\

Sentence: b indrasya bhāgo si viṣṇor ādhipatyam ksatraṁ spṛtam pañcadaśaḥ stomaḥ \\

Sentence: c nṛcakṣasām bhāgo si dhātur ādhipatyam janitraṁ spṛtaṁ saptadaśaḥ stomaḥ \\

Sentence: d mitrasya bhāgo si varuṇasyādhipatyam divo vṛṣṭir vāta spṛta ekaviṁśa stomaḥ \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a vasūnām bhāgo si rudrāṇām ādhipatyam catuṣpāt spṛtam caturviṁśa stomaḥ \\

Sentence: b ādityānām bhāgo si marutām ādhipatyam garbhā spṛtāḥ pañcaviṁśa stomaḥ \\

Sentence: c adityai bhāgo si pūṣṇa ādhipatyam oja spṛtam triṇava stomaḥ \\

Sentence: d devasya savitur bhāgo si bṛhaspater ādhipatyaṁ samīcīr diśa spṛtāś catuṣṭoma stomaḥ \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a yavānām bhāgo sy ayavānām ādhipatyam prajā spṛtāś catuścatvāriṁśa stomaḥ \\

Sentence: b ṛbhūṇām bhāgo si viśveṣām devānām ādhipatyam bhūtaṁ spṛtam trayastriṁśa stomaḥ \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a sahaś ca sahasyaś ca haimantikāv ṛtū agner antaḥśleṣo si kalpetām dyāvāpṛthivī kalpantām āpa oṣadhayaḥ kalpantām agnayaḥ pṛthaṅ nama jyaiṣṭhyāya savratāḥ \\

Sentence: b ye agnayaḥ samanaso ntarā dyāvāpṛthivī ime haimantikāv ṛtū abhikalpamānā indram iva devā abhisam viśantu tayā devatayāṅgirasvad dhruve sīdatam \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a ekayāstuvata prajā adhīyanta prajāpatir adhipatir āsīt \\

Sentence: b tisṛbhir astuvata brahmāsṛjyata brahmaṇaspatir adhipatir āsīt \\

Sentence: c pañcabhir astuvata bhūtāny asṛjyanta bhūtānām patir adhipatir āsīt \\

Sentence: d saptabhir astuvata sapta ṛṣayo sṛjyanta dhātādhipatir āsīt \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a navabhir astuvata pitaro sṛjyantāditir adhnipatny āsīt \\

Sentence: b ekādaśabhir astuvata ṛtavo sṛjyantārtavā adhipataya āsan \\

Sentence: c trayodaśabhir astuvata māsā asṛjyanta samvatsaro dhipatir āsīt \\

Sentence: d pañcadaśabhir astuvata kṣatram asṛjyatendro dhipatir āsīt \\

Sentence: e saptadaśabhir astuvata grāmyāḥ paśavo sṛjyanta bṛhaspatir adhipatir āsīt \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a navadaśabhir astuvata śūdrāryāv asṛjyetām ahorātre adhipatnī āstām \\

Sentence: b ekaviṁśatyāstuvataikaśaphāḥ paśavo sṛjyanta varuṇo dhipatir āsīt \\

Sentence: c trayoviṁśatyāstuvata kṣudrām paśavo sṛjyanta pūṣādhipatir āsīt \\

Sentence: d pañcaviṁśatyāstuvatāraṇyāḥ paśavo sṛjyanta vāyur adhipatir āsīt \\

Sentence: e saptaviṁśatyāstuvata dyāvāpṛthivī vyaitām vasavo rudrā ādityā anuvy āyaṁs ta evādhipataya āsan \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a navasiṁśatyāstuvata vanaspatayo sṛjyanta somo dhipatir āsīt \\

Sentence: b ekatriṁśatāstuvata prajā asṛjyanta yavāś cāyavāś cādhipataya āsan \\

Sentence: c trayastriṁśatāstuvata bhūtāny aśāmyan prajāpatiḥ parameṣṭhy adhipatir āsīt \\

Sentence: d lokam pṛṇa chidram pṛṇātho sīda dhruvā tvam \\

Sentence: e indrāgnī tvā bṛhaspatir asmin yonāv asīṣadan \\

Sentence: f tā asya sūdadohasaḥ somaṁ śrīṇanti pṛśnayaḥ \\

Sentence: g janman devānām viśas triṣv ā rocane divaḥ \\

Sentence: h indram viśvā avīvṛdhant samudravyacasam giraḥ \\

Sentence: i rathītamaṁ rathīnām vājānāṁ satpatim patim \\\\

## Paragraph: 15

Verse: 1

Sentence: a agne jātān pra ṇuda naḥ sapatnān praty ajātān nuda jātavedaḥ \\

Sentence: b adhi no brūhi sumanā aheḍaṁs tava syāma śarmaṁs trivarūtha udbhau \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a sahasā jātān pra ṇudā naḥ sapatnān praty ajātān jātavedo nudasva \\

Sentence: b adhi no brūhi sumanasyamāno vayaṁ syāma pra ṇudā naḥ sapatnān \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: c agneḥ purīṣam asya apso nāma tām tvā viśve abhi gṛṇantu devāḥ \\

Sentence: d stomapṛṣṭhā ghṛtavatīha sīda prajāvad asme draviṇā yajasva \\\\
