Verse: 10

Sentence: a rājñy asi prācī dig vasavas te devā adhipatayo gnir hetīnām pratidhartā trivṛt tvā stomaḥ pṛthivyāṁ śrayatv ājyam uktham avyathāyai stabhnātu rathantaraṁ sāma pratiṣṭhityā antarikṣa ṛṣayas tvā prathamajā deveṣu divo mātrayā varimṇā prathantu vidhartā cāyam adhipatiś ca te tvā sarve samvidātā nākasya pṛṣṭhe svarge loke yajamānam ca sādayantu \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a virāḍ asi dakṣiṇā dig rudrās te devā adhipataya indro hetīnām pratidhartā pañcadaśas tvā stomaḥ pṛthivyāṁ śrayatu praūgam uktham avyathāyai stabhnātu bṛhat sāma pratiṣṭhityā antarikṣa ṛṣayas tvā prathamajā deveṣu divo mātrayā varimṇā prathantu vidhartā cāyam adhipatiś ca te tvā sarve samvidātā nākasya pṛṣṭhe svarge loke yajamānam ca sādayantu \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a samrāḍ asi pratīcī dig ādityās te devā adhipatayo varuṇo hetīnām pratidhartā saptadaśas tvā stomaḥ pṛthivyāṁ śrayatu marutvatīyam uktham avyathāyai stabhnātu vairūpaṁ sāma pratiṣṭhityā antarikṣa ṛṣayas tvā prathamajā deveṣu divo mātrayā varimṇā prathantu vidhartā cāyam adhipatiś ca te tvā sarve samvidātā nākasya pṛṣṭhe svarge loke yajamānam ca sādayantu \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a svarāḍ asy udīcy diṅ marutas te devā adhipatayaḥ somo hetīnām pratidhartaikaviṁśas tvā stomaḥ pṛthivyāṁ śrayatu niṣkevalyam uktham avyathāyai stabhnātu vairājaṁ sāma pratiṣṭhityā antarikṣa ṛṣayas tvā prathamajā deveṣu divo mātrayā varimṇā prathantu vidhartā cāyam adhipatiś ca te tvā sarve samvidātā nākasya pṛṣṭhe svarge loke yajamānam ca sādayantu \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a adhipatny asi bṛhatī dig viśve te devā adhipatayo bṛhaspatir hetīnām pratidhartā triṇavatrayastriṁśau tvā stomau pṛthivyāṁ śrayatām vaiśvadevāgnimārute ukthe avyathāyai stabhnītāṁ śākvararaivate sāmanī pratiṣṭhityā antarikṣa ṛṣayas tvā prathamajā deveṣu divo mātrayā varimṇā prathantu vidhartā cāyam adhipatiś ca te tvā sarve samvidātā nākasya pṛṣṭhe svarge loke yajamānam ca sādayantu \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a ayam puro harikeśaḥ sūryaraśmis tasya rathagṛtsaś ca rathaujāś ca senānīgrāmaṇyā \\

Sentence: b puñjikasthalā ca kratusthalā cāpsarasau daṅkṣṇavaḥ paśavo hetiḥ pauruṣeyo vadhaḥ prahetis tebhyo namo astu te no vantu te no mṛḍayantu te yam dviṣmo yaś ca no dveṣṭi tam eṣām jambhe dadhmaḥ \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a ayam dakṣiṇā viśvakarmā tasya rathasvanaś ca rathecitraś ca senānīgrāmaṇyā \\

Sentence: b menakā ca sahajanyā cāpsarasau yātudhānā hetī rakṣāṁsi prahetis tebhyo namo astu te no vantu te no mṛḍayantu te yam dviṣmo yaś ca no dveṣṭi tam eṣām jambhe dadhmaḥ \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a ayam paścād viśvavyacās tasya rathaprotaś cāsamarathaś ca senānīgrāmaṇyā \\

Sentence: b pramlocantī cānumlocantī cāpsarasau vyāghrā hetiḥ sarpā prahetis tebhyo namo astu te no vantu te no mṛḍayantu te yam dviṣmo yaś ca no dveṣṭi tam eṣām jambhe dadhmaḥ \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a ayam uttarāt samyadvasus tasya tārkṣyaś cāriṣṭanemiś ca senānīgrāmaṇyā \\

Sentence: b viśvācī ca ghṛtācī cāpsarasāv āpo hetir vātaḥ prahetis tebhyo namo astu te no vantu te no mṛḍayantu te yam dviṣmo yaś ca no dveṣṭi tam eṣām jambhe dadhmaḥ \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a ayam upary arvāgvasus tasya senajic ca suṣeṇaś ca senānīgrāmaṇyā \\

Sentence: b urvaśī ca pūrvacittiś cāpsarasāv avasphūrjan hetir vidyut prahetis tebhyo namo astu te no vantu te no mṛḍayantu te yam dviṣmo yaś ca no dveṣṭi tam eṣām jambhe dadhmaḥ \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a agnir mūrdhā divaḥ kakut patiḥ pṛthivyā ayam \\

Sentence: b apāṁ retāṁsi jinvati \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a ayam agniḥ sahasriṇo vājasya śatinas patiḥ \\

Sentence: b mūrdhā kavī rayīṇām \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a tvām agne puṣkarād adhy atharvā nir amanthata \\

Sentence: b mūrdhno viśvasya vāghataḥ \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a bhuvo yajñasya rajasaś ca netā yatrā niyudbhiḥ sacase śivābhiḥ \\

Sentence: b divi mūrdhānam dadhiṣe svarṣām jihvām agne cakṛṣe havyavāham \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a abodhy agniḥ samidhā janānām prati dhenum ivāyatīm uṣāsam \\

Sentence: b yahvā iva pra vayām ujjihānāḥ pra bhānavaḥ sisrate nākam accha \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a avocāma kavaye medhyāya vaco vandāru vṛṣabhāya vṛṣṇe \\

Sentence: b gaviṣṭhiro namasā stomam agnau divīva rukmam uruvyañcam aśret \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a ayam iha prathamo dhāyi dhātṛbhir hotā yajiṣṭho adhvareṣv īḍyaḥ \\

Sentence: b yam apnavāno bhṛgavo virurucur vaneṣu citram vibhvam viśe-viśe \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a janasya gopā ajaniṣṭa jāgṛvir agniḥ sudakṣaḥ suvitāya navyase \\

Sentence: b ghṛtapratīko bṛhatā divispṛśā dyumad vi bhāti bharatebhyaḥ śuciḥ \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a tvām agne aṅgiraso guhā hitam anv avindañ chiśriyāṇam vane-vane \\

Sentence: b sa jāyase mathyamānaḥ saho mahat tvām āhuḥ sahasas putram aṅgiraḥ \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a sakhāyaḥ sam vaḥ samyañcam iṣaṁ stomam cāgnaye \\

Sentence: b varṣiṣṭhāya kṣitīnām ūrjo naptre sahasvate \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a saṁ-sam id yuvase vṛṣann agne viśvāny arya ā \\

Sentence: b iḍas pade sam idhyase sa no vasūny ā bhara \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a tvām citraśravastama havante vikṣu jantavaḥ \\

Sentence: b śociṣkeśam purupriyāgne havyāya voḍhave \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a enā vo agnim namasorjo napātam ā huve \\

Sentence: b priyam cetiṣṭham aratiṁ svadhvaram viśvasya dūtam amṛtam \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a viśvasya dūtam amṛtam viśvasya dūtam amṛtam \\

Sentence: b sa yojate aruṣā viśvabhojasā sa dudravat svāhutaḥ \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a sa dudravat svāhutaḥ sa dudravat svāhutaḥ \\

Sentence: b subrahmā yajñaḥ suśamī vasūnām devaṁ rādho janānām \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a agne vājasya gomata īśānaḥ sahaso yaho \\

Sentence: b asme dhehi jātavedo mahi śravaḥ \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a sa idhāno vasuṣ kavir agnir īḍenyo girā \\

Sentence: b revad asmabhyam purvaṇīka dīdihi \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a kṣapo rājann uta tmanāgne vastor utoṣasaḥ \\

Sentence: b sa tigmajambha rakṣaso daha prati \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a bhadro no agnir āhuto bhadrā rātiḥ subhaga bhadro adhvaraḥ \\

Sentence: b bhadrā uta praśastayaḥ \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a bhadrā uta praśastayo bhadram manaḥ kṛṇuṣva vṛtratūrye \\

Sentence: b yenā samatsu sāsahaḥ \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a yenā samatsu sāsaho va sthirā tanuhi bhūri śardhatām \\

Sentence: b vanemā te abhiṣṭibhiḥ \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a agnim tam manye yo vasur astam yam yanti dhenavaḥ \\

Sentence: b astam arvanta āśavo stam nityāso vājina iṣaṁ stotṛbhya ā bhara \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a so agnir yo vasur gṛṇe sam yam āyanti dhenavaḥ \\

Sentence: b sam arvanto raghudruvaḥ saṁ sujātāsaḥ sūraya iṣaṁ stotṛbhya ā bhara \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a ubhe suścandra sarpiṣo darvī śrīṇīṣa āsani \\

Sentence: b uto na ut pupūryā uktheṣu śavasas pata iṣaṁ stotṛbhya ā bhara \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a agne tam adyāśvam na stomaiḥ kratum na bhadraṁ hṛdispṛśam \\

Sentence: b ṛdhyāmā ta ohaiḥ \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a adhā hy agne krator bhadrasya dakṣasya sādhoḥ \\

Sentence: b rathīr ṛtasya bṛhato babhūtha \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a ebhir no arkair bhavā no arvāṅ svar ṇa jyotiḥ \\

Sentence: b agne viśvebhiḥ sumanā anīkaiḥ \\\\

Verse: 47

Sentence: a agniṁ hotāram manye dāsvantam vasuṁ sūnuṁ sahaso jātavedasam vipram na jātavedasam \\

Sentence: b ya ūrdhvayā svadhvaro devo devācyā kṛpā \\

Sentence: c ghṛtasya vibhrāṣṭim anu vaṣṭi śociṣājuhvānasya sarpiṣaḥ \\\\

Verse: 48

Sentence: a agne tvam no antama uta trātā śivo bhava varūthyaḥ \\

Sentence: b vasur agnir vasuśravā acchā nakṣi dyumattamaṁ rayim dāḥ \\

Sentence: c tam tvā śociṣṭha dīdivaḥ sumnāya nūnam īmahe sakhibhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 49

Sentence: a yena ṛṣayas tapasā satram āyann indhānā agniṁ svar ābharantaḥ \\

Sentence: b tasminn aham ni dadhe nāke asgnim yam āhur manava stīrṇabarhiṣam \\\\

Verse: 50

Sentence: a tam patnībhir anu gacchema devāḥ putrair bhrātṛbhir uta vā hiraṇyaiḥ \\

Sentence: b nākam gṛbhṇānāḥ sukṛtasya loke tṛtīye pṛṣṭhe adhi rocane divaḥ \\\\

Verse: 51

Sentence: a ā vāco madhyam aruhad guraṇyur ayam agniḥ satpatiś cekitānaḥ \\

Sentence: b pṛṣṭhe pṛthivyā nihito davidyutad adhas padam kṛṇutām ye pṛtanyavaḥ \\\\

Verse: 52

Sentence: a ayam agnir vīratamo vayodhāḥ sahasriyo dyotatām aprayucchan \\

Sentence: b vibhrājamānaḥ sarirasya madhya upa pra yāhi divyāni dhāma \\\\

Verse: 53

Sentence: a sampra cyavadhvam upa sampra yātāgne patho devayānān kṛṇudhvam \\

Sentence: b punaḥ kṛṇvānā pitarā yuvānānvā tāṁsīt tvayi tantum etam \\\\

Verse: 54

Sentence: a ud budhyasvāgne prati jāgṛhi tvam iṣṭāpūrte saṁ sṛjethām ayam ca \\

Sentence: b asmint sadhasthe adhy uttarasmin visve devā yajamānāś ca sīdata \\\\

Verse: 55

Sentence: a yena vahasi sahasram yenāgne sarvavedasam \\

Sentence: b tenemam yajñam no naya svar deveṣu gantave \\\\

Verse: 56

Sentence: a ayam te yonir ṛtviyo yato jāto arocathāḥ \\

Sentence: b tam jānann agna ārohāthā no vardhayā rayim \\\\

Verse: 57

Sentence: a tapaś ca tapasyaś ca śaiśirāv ṛtū agner antaḥśleṣo si kalpetām dyāvāpṛthivī kalpantām āpa oṣadhayaḥ kalpantām agnayaḥ pṛthaṅ nama jyaiṣṭhyāya savratāḥ \\

Sentence: b ye agnayaḥ samanaso ntarā dyāvāpṛthivī ime śaiśirāv ṛtū abhikalpamānā indram iva devā abhisam viśantu tayā devatayāṅgirasvad dhruve sīdatam \\\\

Verse: 58

Sentence: a parameṣṭhī tvā sādayatu divas pṛṣṭhe jyotiṣmatīm \\

Sentence: b viśvasmai prāṇāyāpānāya vyānāyodānāya pratiṣṭhāyai caritrāya \\

Sentence: c sūryas te dhipatis tayā devatayāṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda \\\\

Verse: 59

Sentence: a lokam pṛṇa chidram pṛṇātho sīda dhruvā tvam \\

Sentence: b indrāgnī tvā bṛhaspatir asmin yonāv asīṣadan \\\\

Verse: 60

Sentence: a tā asya sūdadohasaḥ somaṁ śrīṇanti pṛśnayaḥ \\

Sentence: b janman devānām viśas triṣv ā rocane divaḥ \\\\

Verse: 61

Sentence: a indram viśvā avīvṛdhant samudravyacasam giraḥ \\

Sentence: b rathītamaṁ rathīnām vājānāṁ satpatim patim \\\\

Verse: 62

Sentence: a prothad aśvo na yavase viṣyan yadā mahaḥ samvaraṇād vy asthāt \\

Sentence: b ād asya vāto anu vāti śocir adha sma te vrajanam kṛṣṇam asti \\\\

Verse: 63

Sentence: a āyoṣ ṭvā sadane sādayāmy avataś chāyāyāṁ samudrasya hṛdaye \\

Sentence: b raśmīvatīm bhāsvatīm ā yā dyām bhāsy ā pṛthivīm orv antarikṣam \\\\

Verse: 64

Sentence: a parameṣṭhī tvā sādayatu divas pṛṣṭhe vyacasvatīm prathasvatīm divam yaccha divam dṛṁha divam mā hiṁsīḥ \\

Sentence: b viśvasmai prāṇāyāpānāya vyānāyodānāya pratiṣṭhāyai caritrāya \\

Sentence: c sūryas tvābhi pātu mahyā svastyā chardiṣā śamtamena tayā devatayāṅgirasvad dhruve sīdatam \\\\

Verse: 65

Sentence: a sahasrasya pramāsi \\

Sentence: b sahasrasya pratimāsi \\

Sentence: c sahasrasyonmāsi \\

Sentence: d sāhasro si \\

Sentence: e sahasrāya tvā \\\\

## Paragraph: 16

Verse: 1

Sentence: a namas te rudra manyava uto ta iṣave namaḥ \\

Sentence: b bāhubhyām uta te namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a yā te rudra śivā tanūr aghorāpāpakāśinī \\

Sentence: b tayā nas tanvā śamtamayā giriśantābhi cākaśīhi \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a yām iṣum giriśanta haste bibharṣy astave \\

Sentence: b śivām giritra tām kuru mā hiṁsīḥ puruṣam jagat \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a śivena vacasā tvā giriśācchā vadāmasi \\

Sentence: b yathā naḥ sarvam ij jagad ayakṣmaṁ sumanā asat \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a adhy avocad adhivaktā prathamo daivyo bhiṣak \\

Sentence: b ahīṁś ca sarvān jambhayant sarvāś ca yātudhānyo dharācīḥ parā suva \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a asau yas tāmro aruṇa uta babhruḥ sumaṅgalaḥ \\

Sentence: b ye cainaṁ rudrā abhito dikṣu śritāḥ sahasraśo vaiṣāṁ heḍa īmahe \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a asau yo vasarpati nīlagrīvo vilohitaḥ \\

Sentence: b utainam gopā adṛśrann adṛśrann udahāryaḥ sa dṛṣto mṛḍayāti naḥ \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a namo stu nīlagrīvāya sahasrākṣāya mīḍhuṣe \\

Sentence: b atho ye asya satvāno ham tebhyo karam namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a pra muñca dhanvanas tvam ubhayor ārtnyor jyām \\

Sentence: b yāś ca te hasta iṣavaḥ parā tā bhagavo vapa \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a vijyam dhanuḥ kapardino viśalyo vāṇavāṁ uta \\

Sentence: b aneśann asya yā iṣava ābhur asya niṣaṅgadhiḥ \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a yā te hetir mīḍhuṣṭama haste babhūva te dhanuḥ \\

Sentence: b tayāsmān viśvatas tvam ayakṣmayā pari bhuja \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a pari te dhanvano hetir asmān vṛṇaktu viśvataḥ \\

Sentence: b atho ya iṣudhis tavāre asman ni dhehi tam \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a avatatya dhanuṣ ṭvaṁ sahasrākṣa śateṣudhe \\

Sentence: b niśīrya śalyānām mukhā śivo naḥ sumanā bhava \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a namas ta āyudhāyānātatāya dhṛṣṇave \\

Sentence: b ubhābhyām uta te namo bāhubhyām tava dhanvane \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a mā no mahāntam uta mā no arbhakam mā na ukṣantam uta mā na ukṣitam \\

Sentence: b mā no vadhīḥ pitaram mota mātaram mā naḥ priyās tanvo rudra rīriṣaḥ \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a mā nas toke tanaye mā na āyuṣi mā no goṣu mā no aśveṣu rīriṣaḥ \\

Sentence: b mā no vīrān rudra bhāmino vadhīr haviṣmantaḥ sadam it tvā havāmahe \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a namo hiraṇyabāhave senānyṝ diśām ca pataye namo namo vṛkṣebhyo harikeśebhyaḥ paśūnām pataye namo namaḥ śaṣpiñjarāya tviṣīmate pathīnām pataye namo namp harikeśāyopavītine puṣṭānām pataye namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a namo babhluśāya vyādhine nnānām pataye namo namo bhavasya hetyai jagatām pataye namo namo rudrāyātatāyine kṣetrāṇām pataye namo namaḥ sūtāyāhantyaivanānām pataye namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a namo rohitāya sthapataye vṛkṣāṇām pataye namo namo bhuvantaye vārivaskṛtāyauṣadhīnām pataye namo namo mantriṇe vāṇijāya kakṣāṇām pataye namo nama uccairghoṣāyākrandayate pattīnām pataye namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a namaḥ kṛtsnāyatayā dhāvate satvanām pataye namo namaḥ sahamānāya nivyādhina āvyādhinīnām pataye namo namo niṣaṅgiṇe kakubhāya stenānām pataye namo nam nicerave paricarāyāraṇyānām pataye namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a namo vañcate parivañcate stāyūnām pataye namo namo niṣaṅgiṇa iṣudhimate taskarāṇām pataye namo namaḥ sṛkāyibhyo jighāṁsadbhyo muṣṇatām pataye namo namo simadbhyo naktam caradbhyo vikṛntānām pataye namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a nama uṣṇīṣiṇe giricarāya kuluñcānām pataye namo nama iṣumadhbyo dhanvāyibhyaś ca vo namo nama ātanvānebhyaḥ pratidadhānebhyaś ca vo namo nama āyacchadbhyo syadbhyaś ca vo namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a namo visṛjadbhyo vidhyadbhyaś ca vo namo namaḥ svapadbhyo jāgradbhyaś ca vo namo namaḥ śayānebhya āsīnebhyaś ca vo namo namas tiṣṭhadbhyo dhāvadbhyaś ca vo namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a namaḥ sabhābhyaḥ sabhāpatibhyaś ca vo namo namo śvebhyo śvapatibhyaś ca vo namo nama āvyādhinībhyo vividhyantībhyaś ca vo namo nama ugaṇābhyas tṛṁhatībhyaś ca vo namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a namo gaṇebhyo gaṇapatibhyaś ca vo namo namo vrātebhyo vrātapatibhyaś ca vo namo namgṛtsebhyo gṛtsapatibhyaś ca vo namo namo virūpebhyo viśvarūpebhyaś ca vo namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a namaḥ senābhyaḥ senānibhyaś ca vo namo namo rathibhyo aratebhyaś ca vo namo namaḥ kṣattṛbhyaḥ samgrahītṛbhyaś ca vo namo namo mahadbhyo arbhakebhyaś ca vo namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a namas takṣabhyo rathakārebhyaś ca vo namo namaḥ kulālebhyaḥ kurmārebhyaś ca vo namo namo niṣādebhyaḥ puñjiṣṭebhyaś ca vo namo namaḥ śvanibhyo mṛgayubhyaś ca vo namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a namaḥ śvabhyaḥ śvapatibhyaś ca vo namo namo bhavāya ca rudrāya ca namaḥ śarvāya ca paśupataye ca namo nīlagrīvāya ca śitikaṇṭhāya ca \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a namaḥ kapardine ca vyḥptakeśāya ca namaḥ sahasrākṣāya ca śatadhanvane ca namo giriśayāya ca śipiviṣṭāya ca namo mīḍhuṣṭamāya ceṣumate ca \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a namo hrasvāya ca vāmanāya ca namo bṛhate ca varṣīyase ca namo vṛddhāya ca savṛdhe ca namo gryāya ca prathamāya ca \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a nama āśave cājirāya ca namaḥ śīghryāya ca śībhyāya ca nama ūrmyāya cāvasvanyāya ca namo nādeyāya ca dvīpyāya ca \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a namo jyeṣṭhāya ca kaniṣṭhāya ca namaḥ pūrvajāya cāparajāya ca namo madhyamāya cāpagalbhāya ca namo jaghanyāya ca budhnyāya ca \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a namaḥ somyāya ca pratisaryāya ca namo yāmyāya ca kṣemyāya ca namaḥ ślokyāya cāvasānyāya ca nama urvaryāya ca khalyāya ca \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a namo vanyāya ca kakṣṇyāya ca namaḥ śravāya ca pratiśravāya ca nama āśuṣeṇāya cāśurathāya ca namaḥ śūrāya cāvabhedine ca \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a namo bilmine ca kavacine ca namo varmiṇe ca varūthine ca namaḥ śrutāya ca śrutasenāya ca namo dundubhyāya cāhananyāya ca \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a namo dhṛṣṇave ca pramṛśāya ca namo niṣaṅgiṇe ceṣudhimate ca namas tīkṣṇeṣave cāyudhine ca namaḥ svāyudhāya ca sudhanvane ca \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a namaḥ srutyāya ca pathyāya ca namaḥ kāṭyāya ca nīpyāya ca namaḥ kulyāya ca sarasyāya ca namo nādeyāya ca vaiśantāya ca \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a namaḥ kūpyāya cāvaṭyāya ca namo vīdhryāya cātapyāya ca namo meghyāya ca ca vidyutyāya namo varṣyāya cāvarṣyāya ca \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a namo vātyāya ca reṣmyāya ca namo vāstavyāya ca vāstupāya ca namaḥ somāya ca rudrāya ca namas tāmrāya cāruṇāya ca \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a namaḥ śamgave ca paśupataye ca nama ugrāya ca bhīmāya ca namo agrevadhāya ca dūrevadhāya ca namo hantre ca hanīyase ca namo vṛkṣebhyo harikeśebhyo namas tārāya \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a namaḥ śambhavāya ca mayobhavāya ca namaḥ śamkarāya ca mayaskarāya ca namaḥ śivāya ca śivatarāya ca \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a namaḥ pāryāya cāvāryāya ca namaḥ prataraṇāya cottaraṇāya ca namas tīrthyāya ca kūlyāya ca namaḥ śaṣpyāya ca phenyāya ca \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a namaḥ sikatyāya ca pravāhyāya ca namaḥ kiṁśilāya ca kṣayaṇāya ca namaḥ kapardine ca pulastaye ca nama iriṇyāya ca prapathyāya ca \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a namo vrajyāya ca goṣṭhyāya ca namas talpyāya ca gehyāya ca namo hṛdayyāya ca niveṣyāya ca namaḥ kāṭyāya ca gahvareṣṭhāya ca \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a namaḥ śuṣkyāya ca harityāya ca namaḥ pāṁsavyāya ca rajasyāya ca namo lopyāya colapyāya ca nama ūrvyāya ca sūrvyāya ca \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a namaḥ parṇāya ca parṇaśadāya ca nama udguramāṇāya cābhighnate ca nama ākhidate ca prakhidate ca nama iṣukṛdbhyo dhanuṣkṛdbhyas ca vo namo namo vaḥ kirikebhyo devānāṁ hṛdayebhyo namo vicinvatkebhyo namo vikṣiṇatkebhyo nama ānirhatebhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 47

Sentence: a drāpe andhasas pate daridra nīlalohita \\

Sentence: b āsām prajānām eṣām paśūnām mā bher mā roṅ mo ca naḥ kim canāmamat \\\\

Verse: 48

Sentence: a imā rudrāya tavase kapardine kṣayadvīrāya pra bharāmahe matīḥ \\

Sentence: b yathā śam asad dvipade catuṣpade viśvam puṣṭam grāme asminn anāturam \\\\

Verse: 49

Sentence: a yā te rudra śivā tanūḥ śivā viśvāhā bheṣajī \\

Sentence: b śivā rutasya bheṣajī tayā no mṛḍa jīvase \\\\

Verse: 50

Sentence: a pari no rudrasya hetir vṛṇaktu pari tveṣasya durmatir aghāyoḥ \\

Sentence: b ava sthirā maghavadbhyas tanuṣva mīḍhvas tokāya tanayāya mṛḍa \\\\

Verse: 51

Sentence: a mīḍhuṣṭama śivatama śivo naḥ sumanā bhava \\

Sentence: b parame vṛkṣa āyudham nidhāya kṛttim vasāna ā cara pinākam bibhrad ā gahi \\\\

Verse: 52

Sentence: a vikiridra vilohita namas te astu bhagavaḥ \\

Sentence: b yās te sahasraṁ hetayo nyam asman ni vapantu tāḥ \\\\

Verse: 53

Sentence: a sahasrāṇi sahasraśo bāhvos tava hetayaḥ \\

Sentence: b tāsām īśāno bhagavaḥ parācīnā mukhā kṛdhi \\\\

Verse: 54

Sentence: a asamkhyātā sahasrāṇi ye rudrā adhi bhūmyām \\

Sentence: b teṣāṁ sahasrayojane va dhanvāni tanmasi \\\\

Verse: 55

Sentence: a asmin mahaty arṇavṝ ntarikṣe bhavā adhi \\

Sentence: b teṣāṁ sahasrayojane va dhanvāni tanmasi \\\\

Verse: 56

Sentence: a nīlagrīvāḥ śitikaṇṭhā divaṁ rudrā upāśritāḥ \\

Sentence: b teṣāṁ sahasrayojane va dhanvāni tanmasi \\\\

Verse: 57

Sentence: a nīlagrīvāḥ śitikaṇṭhāḥ śarvā adhaḥ kṣamācarāḥ \\

Sentence: b teṣāṁ sahasrayojane va dhanvāni tanmasi \\\\

Verse: 58

Sentence: a ye vṛkṣeṣu śaṣpiñjarā nīlagrīvā vilohitāḥ \\

Sentence: b teṣāṁ sahasrayojane va dhanvāni tanmasi \\\\

Verse: 59

Sentence: a ye bhūtānām adhipatayo viśikhāsaḥ kapardinaḥ \\

Sentence: b teṣāṁ sahasrayojane va dhanvāni tanmasi \\\\

Verse: 60

Sentence: a ye pathām pathirakṣasa ailabṛdā āyuryudhaḥ \\

Sentence: b teṣāṁ sahasrayojane va dhanvāni tanmasi \\\\

Verse: 61

Sentence: a ye tīrthāni pracaranti sṛkāhastā niṣaṅgiṇaḥ \\

Sentence: b teṣāṁ sahasrayojane va dhanvāni tanmasi \\\\

Verse: 62

Sentence: a ye nneṣu vividhyanti pātreṣu pibato janān \\

Sentence: b teṣāṁ sahasrayojane va dhanvāni tanmasi \\\\

Verse: 63

Sentence: a ye etāvantaś ca bhūyāṁsaś ca diśo rudrā vitasthire \\

Sentence: b teṣāṁ sahasrayojane va dhanvāni tanmasi \\\\

Verse: 64

Sentence: a namo stu rudrebhyo ye divi yeṣām varṣam iṣavaḥ \\

Sentence: b tebhyo daśa prācīr daśa dakṣiṇā daśa pratīcīr daśodīcīr daśordhvāḥ \\

Sentence: c tebhyo namo astu te no vantu te no mṛḍayantu te yam dviṣmo yaś ca no dveṣṭi tam eṣām jambhe dadhmaḥ \\\\

Verse: 65

Sentence: a namo stu rudrebhyo yṝ ntarikṣe yeṣām vāta iṣavaḥ \\

Sentence: b tebhyo daśa prācīr daśa dakṣiṇā daśa pratīcīr daśodīcīr daśordhvāḥ \\

Sentence: c tebhyo namo astu te no vantu te no mṛḍayantu te yam dviṣmo yaś ca no dveṣṭi tam eṣām jambhe dadhmaḥ \\\\

Verse: 66

Sentence: a namo stu rudrebhyo ye pṛthivyām yeṣām annam iṣavaḥ \\

Sentence: b tebhyo daśa prācīr daśa dakṣiṇā daśa pratīcīr daśodīcīr daśordhvāḥ \\

Sentence: c tebhyo namo astu te no vantu te no mṛḍayantu te yam dviṣmo yaś ca no dveṣṭi tam eṣām jambhe dadhmaḥ \\\\

## Paragraph: 17

Verse: 1

Sentence: a aśmann ūrjam parvate śiśriyāṇām adbhya oṣadhībhyo vanaspatibhyo dhi sambhṛtam payaḥ \\

Sentence: b tām na iṣam ūrjam dhatta marutaḥ saṁrarāṇāḥ \\

Sentence: c aśmaṁs te kṣut \\

Sentence: d mayi ta ūrk \\

Sentence: e yam dviṣmas tam te śug ṛcchatu \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a imā me agna iṣṭakā dhenavaḥ santv ekā ca daśa ca daśa ca śatam ca śatam ca sahasram ca sahasram cāyutam cāyutam ca niyutam ca niyutam ca prayutam cārbudam ca nyarbudaṁ samudraś ca madhyam cāntaś ca parārdhaś caitā me agna iṣṭakā dhenavaḥ santv amutrāmuṣmiml loke \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a ṛtava stha ṛtāvṛdha ṛtuṣṭhā stha ṛtāvṛdhaḥ \\

Sentence: b ghṛtaścyuto madhuścyuto virājo nāma kāmadughā akṣīyamāṇāḥ \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a samudrasya tvāvakayāgne pari vyayāmasi \\

Sentence: b pāvako asmabhyaṁ śivo bhava \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a himasya tvā jarāyuṇāgne pari vyayāmasi \\

Sentence: b pāvako asmabhyaṁ śivo bhava \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a upa jmann upa vetase va tara nadīṣv ā \\

Sentence: b agne pittam apām asi \\

Sentence: c maṇḍūki tābhir ā gahi semam no yajñam pāvakavarṇaṁ śivam kṛdhi \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a apām idam nyayanaṁ samudrasya niveśanam \\

Sentence: b anyāṁs te asmat tapantu hetayaḥ pāvako asmabhyaṁ śivo bhava \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a agne pāvaka rociṣā mandrayā deva jihvayā \\

Sentence: b ā devān vakṣi yakṣi ca \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a sa naḥ pāvaka dīdivo gne devāṁ ihā vaha \\

Sentence: b upa yajñaṁ haviś ca naḥ \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a pāvakayā yaś citayantyā kṛpā kṣāman ruruca uṣaso na bhānunā \\

Sentence: b tūrvan na yāmann etaśasya nū raṇa ā yo ghṛṇe na tatṛṣāṇo ajaraḥ \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a namas te harase śociṣe namas te astv arciṣe \\

Sentence: b anyāṁs te asmat tapantu hetayaḥ pāvako asmabhyaṁ śivo bhava \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a nṛṣade veṭ \\

Sentence: b apsuṣade veṭ \\

Sentence: c barhiṣade veṭ \\

Sentence: d vanasade veṭ \\

Sentence: e svarvide veṭ \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a ye devā devānām yajñiyā yajñiyānāṁ samvatsarīṇam upa bhāgam āsate \\

Sentence: b ahutādo haviṣo yajñe asmint svayam pibantu madhuno ghṛtasya \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a ye devā deveṣv adhi devatvam āyan ye brahmaṇaḥ puraetāro asya \\

Sentence: b yebhyo na ṛte pavate dhāma kim cana na te divo na pṛthivyā adhi snuṣu \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a prāṇadā apānadā vyānadā varcodā varivodāḥ \\

Sentence: b anyāṁs te asmat tapantu hetayaḥ pāvako asmabhyaṁ śivo bhava \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a agnis tigmena śociṣā yāsad viśvam ny atriṇam \\

Sentence: b agnir no vanate rayim \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a ya imā viśvā bhuvanāni juhvad ṛṣir hotā ny asīdat pitā naḥ \\

Sentence: b sa āśiṣā draviṇam icchamānaḥ prathamacchad avarāṁ ā viveśa \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a kiṁ svid āsīd adhiṣṭhānam ārambhaṇam katamat svit kathāsīt \\

Sentence: b yato bhūmim janayan viśvakarmā vi dyām aurṇon mahinā viśvacakṣāḥ \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a viśvataścakṣur uta viśvatomukho viśvatobāhur uta viśvataspāt \\

Sentence: b sam bāhubhyām dhamati sam patatrair dyāvābhūmī janayan deva ekaḥ \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a kiṁ svid vanam ka u sa vṛkṣa āsa yato dyāvāpṛthivī niṣṭatakṣuḥ \\

Sentence: b manīṣiṇo manasā pṛcchated u tad yad adhyatiṣṭhad bhuvanāni dhārayan \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a yā te dhāmāni paramāṇi yāvamā yā madhyamā viśvakarmann utemā \\

Sentence: b śikṣā sakhibhyo haviṣi svadhāvaḥ svayam yajasva tanvam vṛdhānaḥ \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a viśvakarman haviṣā vāvṛdhānaḥ svayam yajasva pṛthivīm uta dyām \\

Sentence: b muhyantv anye abhito sapatnā ihāsmākam maghavā sūrir astu \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a vācas patim viśvakarmāṇam ūtaye manojuvam vāje adyā huvema \\

Sentence: b sa no viśvāni havanāni joṣad viśvaśambhūr avase sādhukarmā \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a viśvakarman haviṣā vardhanena trātāram indram akṛṇor avadhyam \\

Sentence: b tasmai viśaḥ samanamanta pūrvīr ayam ugro vihavyo yathāsat \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a cakṣuṣaḥ pitā manasā hi dhīro ghṛtam ene ajanan namnamāne \\

Sentence: b yaded antā adadṛhanta pūrva ād id dyāvāpṛthivī aprathetām \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a viśvakarmā vimanā ād vihāyā dhātā vidhātā paramota samdṛk \\

Sentence: b teṣām iṣṭāni sam iṣā madanti yatrā saptaṛṣīn para ekam āhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a yo naḥ pitā janitā yo vidhātā dhāmāni veda bhuvanāni viśvā \\

Sentence: b yo devānām nāmadhā eka eva taṁ sampraśnam bhuvanā yanty anyā \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a ta āyajanta draviṇaṁ sam asmā ṛṣayaḥ pūrve jaritāro na bhūnā \\

Sentence: b asūrte sūrte rajasi niṣatte ye bhūtāni samakṛṇvann imāni \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a paro divā para enā pṛthivyā paro devebhir asurair yad asti \\

Sentence: b kaṁ svid garbham prathamam dadhra āpo yatra devāḥ samapaśyanta pūrve \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a tam id garbham prathamam dadhra āpo yatra devāḥ samagacchanta viśve \\

Sentence: b ajasya nābhāv adhy ekam arpitam yasmin viśvāni bhuvanāni tasthuḥ \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a na tam vidātha ya imā jajānānyad yuṣmākam antaram babhūva \\

Sentence: b nīhāreṇa prāvṛtā jalpyā cāsutṛpa ukthaśāsaś caranti \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a viśvakarmā hy ajaniṣṭa deva ād id gandharvo abhavad dvitīyaḥ \\

Sentence: b tṛtīyaḥ pitā janitauṣadhīnām apām garbham vy adadhāt purutrā \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a āśuḥ śiśāno vṛṣabho na bhīmo ghanāghanaḥ kṣobhaṇaś carṣaṇīnām \\

Sentence: b samkrandano nimiṣa ekavīraḥ śataṁ senā ajayat sākam indraḥ \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a samkrandanenānimiṣeṇa jiṣṇunā yutkāreṇa duścyavanena dhṛṣṇunā \\

Sentence: b tad indreṇa jayata tat sahadhvam yudho nara iṣuhastena vṛṣṇā \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a sa iṣuhastaiḥ sa niṣaṅgibhir vaśī saṁsraṣṭā sa yudha indro gaṇena \\

Sentence: b saṁsṛṣṭajit somapā bāhuśardhy ḥgradhanvā pratihitābhir astā \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a bṛhaspate pari dīyā rathena rakṣohāmitrān apabādhamānaḥ \\

Sentence: b prabhañjant senāḥ pramṛṇo yudhā jayann asmākam edhy avitā rathānām \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a balavijñāya sthaviraḥ pravīraḥ sahasvān vājī sahamāna ugraḥ \\

Sentence: b abhivīro abhisatvā sahojā jaitram indra ratham ā tiṣṭha govit \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a gotrabhidam govidam vajrabāhum jayantam ajma pramṛṇantam ojasā \\

Sentence: b imaṁ sajātā anu vīrayadhvam indraṁ sakhāyo anu saṁ rabhadhvam \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a abhi gotrāṇi sahasā gāhamāno dayo vīraḥ śatamanyur indraḥ \\

Sentence: b duścyavanaḥ pṛtanāṣāḍ ayudhyo smākaṁ senā avatu pra yutsu \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a indra āsām netā bṛhaspatir dakṣiṇā yajñaḥ pura etu somaḥ \\

Sentence: b devasenānām abhibhañjatīnām jayantīnām maruto yantv agram \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a indrasya vṛṣṇo varuṇasya rājña ādityānām marutāṁ śardha ugram \\

Sentence: b mahāmanasām bhuvanacyavānām ghoṣo devānām jayatām ud asthāt \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a ud dharṣaya maghavann āyudhāny ut satvanām māmakānām manāṁsi \\

Sentence: b ud vṛtrahan vājinām vājināny ud rathānām jayatām yantu ghoṣāḥ \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a asmākam indraḥ samṛteṣu dhvajeṣv asmākam yā iṣavas tā jayantu \\

Sentence: b asmākam vīrā uttare bhavantv asmāṁ u devā avatā haveṣu \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a amīṣām cittam pratilobhayantī gṛhāṇāṅgāny apve parehi \\

Sentence: b abhi prehi nir daha hṛtsu śokair andhenāmitrās tamasā sacantām \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a avasṛṣṭā parā pata śaravye brahmasaṁśite \\

Sentence: b gacchāmitrān pra padyasva māmīṣām kam canoc chiṣaḥ \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a pretā jayatā nara indro vaḥ śarma yacchatu \\

Sentence: b ugrā vaḥ santu bāhavo nādhṛṣyā yathāsatha \\\\

Verse: 47

Sentence: a asau yā senā marutaḥ pareṣām abhyaiti na ojasā spardhamānā \\

Sentence: b tām gūhata tamasāpavratena yathāmī anyo anyam na jānan \\\\

Verse: 48

Sentence: a yatra vāṇāḥ sampatanti kumārā viśikhā iva \\

Sentence: b tatra indro bṛhaspatir aditiḥ śarma yacchatu viśvāhā śarma yacchatu \\\\

Verse: 49

Sentence: a marmāṇi te varmaṇā chādayāmi somas tvā rājāmṛtenānu vastām \\

Sentence: b uror varīyo varuṇas te kṛṇotu jayantam tvānu devā madantu \\\\

Verse: 50

Sentence: a ud enam uttarām nayāgne ghṛtenāhuta \\

Sentence: b rāyaspoṣeṇa raṁ sṛja prajayā ca bahum kṛdhi \\\\

Verse: 51

Sentence: a indremam pratarām naya sajātānām asadvaśī \\

Sentence: b sam enam varcasā sṛja devānām bhāgadā asat \\\\

Verse: 52

Sentence: a yasaya kurmo gṛhe havis tam agne vardhayā tvam \\

Sentence: b tasmai devā adhi bravann ayam ca brahmaṇaspatiḥ \\\\

Verse: 53

Sentence: a ud u tvā viśve devā agne bharantu cittibhiḥ \\

Sentence: b sa no bahva śivas tvaṁ supratīko vibhāvasuḥ \\\\

Verse: 54

Sentence: a pañca diśo daivīr yajñam avantu devīr apāmatim durmatim bādhamānāḥ \\

Sentence: b rāyaspoṣe yajñapatim ābhajantī rāyaspoṣe adhi yajño asthāt \\\\

Verse: 55

Sentence: a samiddhe agnāv adhi māmahāna ukthapatra īḍyo gṛbhītaḥ \\

Sentence: b taptam gharmam parigṛhyāyajantorjā yad yajñam ayajanta devāḥ \\\\

Verse: 56

Sentence: a daivyāya dhartre joṣṭre devaśrīḥ śrīmanāḥ śatapayāḥ \\

Sentence: b parigṛhya devā yajñam āyan devā devebhyo adhvaryanto asthuḥ \\\\

Verse: 57

Sentence: a vītaṁ haviḥ śamitaṁ śamitā yajadhyai turīyo yajño yatra havyam eti \\

Sentence: b tato vākā āśiṣo no juṣantām \\\\

Verse: 58

Sentence: a sūryaraśmir harikeśaḥ purastāt savitā jyotir ud ayāṁ ajasram \\

Sentence: b tasya pūṣā prasave yāti vidvānt sampaśyan viśvā bhuvanāni gopāḥ \\\\

Verse: 59

Sentence: a vimāna eṣa divo madhya āsta āpaprivān rodasī antarikṣam \\

Sentence: b sa viśvācīr abhi caṣṭe ghṛtācīr antarā pūrvam aparam ca ketum \\\\

Verse: 60

Sentence: a ukṣā samudro aruṇaḥ suparṇaḥ pūrvasya yonim pitur ā viveśa \\

Sentence: b madhye divo nihitaḥ pṛśnir aśmā vi cakrame rajasas pāty antau \\\\

Verse: 61

Sentence: a indram viśvā avīvṛdhant samudravyacasam giraḥ \\

Sentence: b rathītamaṁ rathīnām vājānāṁ satpatim patim \\\\

Verse: 62

Sentence: a devahūr yajña ā ca vakṣat sumnahūr yajña ā ca vakṣat \\

Sentence: b yakṣad agnir devo devāṁ ā ca vakṣat \\\\

Verse: 63

Sentence: a vajasya mā prasave udgrābheṇod agrabhīt \\

Sentence: b adha sapatnān indro me nigrābheṇādharāṁ akaḥ \\\\

Verse: 64

Sentence: a udgrābham ca nigrābham ca brahma devā avīvṛdhan \\

Sentence: b adhā sapatnān indrāgnī me viṣūcīnān vy asyatām \\\\

Verse: 65

Sentence: a kramadhvam agninā nākam ukhyaṁ hasteṣu bibhrataḥ \\

Sentence: b divas pṛṣṭhaṁ svar gatvā miśrā devebhir ādhvam \\\\

Verse: 66

Sentence: a prācīm anu pradiśam prehi vidvān agner agne puroagnir bhaveha \\

Sentence: b viśvā āśā dīdyāno vi bhāhy ūrjam no dhehi dvipade catuṣpade \\\\

Verse: 67

Sentence: a pṛthivyā aham ud antarikṣam āruham antarikṣād divam āruham \\

Sentence: b divo nākasya pṛṣṭhāt svar jyotir agām aham \\\\

Verse: 68

Sentence: a svar yanto nāpekṣanta ā dyāṁ rohanti rodasī \\

Sentence: b yajñam ye viśvatodhāraṁ suvidvāṁso vitenire \\\\

Verse: 69

Sentence: a agne prehi prathamo devayatām cakṣur devānām uta martyānām \\

Sentence: b iyakṣamāṇā bhṛgubhiḥ sajoṣāḥ svar yantu yajamānāḥ svasti \\\\

Verse: 70

Sentence: a naktoṣāsā samanasā virūpe dhāpayete śiśum ekaṁ samīcī \\

Sentence: b dyāvākṣāmā rukmo antar vi bhāti devā agnim dhārayan draviṇodāḥ \\\\

Verse: 71

Sentence: a agne sahasrākṣa śatamūrdhañ chatam te prāṇāḥ sahasram vyānāḥ \\

Sentence: b tvaṁ sāhasrasya rāya īśiṣe tasmai te vidhema vājāya svāhā \\\\

Verse: 72

Sentence: a suparṇo si garunmān pṛṣṭhe pṛthivyāḥ sīda \\

Sentence: b bhāsāntarikṣam ā pṛṇa jyotiṣā divam ut tabhāna tejasā diśa ud dṛṁha \\\\

Verse: 73

Sentence: a ājuhvānaḥ supratīkaḥ purastād agne yonim ā sīda sādhuyā \\

Sentence: b asmint sashasthe adhy uttarasmin viśve devā yajamānaś ca sīdata \\\\

Verse: 74

Sentence: a taṁ savitur vareṇyasya citrām āham vṛṇe sumatim viśvajanyām \\

Sentence: b yām asya kaṇvo aduhat prapīnāṁ sahasradhārām payasā mahīm gām \\\\

Verse: 75

Sentence: a vidhema te parame janmann agne vidhema stomair avare sadhasthe \\

Sentence: b yasmād yoner udārithā yaje tam pra tve havīṁṣi juhure samiddhe \\\\

Verse: 76

Sentence: a preddho agne dīdihi puro no jasrayā sūrmyā yaviṣṭha \\

Sentence: b tvāṁ śaśvanta upa yanti vājāḥ \\\\

Verse: 77

Sentence: a agne tam adyāśvam na stomaiḥ kratum na bhadraṁ hṛdispṛśam \\

Verse: 78

Sentence: a śukrajyotiś ca citrajyotiś ca satyajyotiś ca jyotiṣmāṁś ca \\

Sentence: b krīḍī ca śākī cojjeṣī \\\\

Sentence: a ugraś ca bhīmaś ca dhvāntaś ca dhuniś ca \\

Verse: 87

Sentence: a imaṁ stanam ūrjasvantam dhayāpām prapīnam agne sarirasya madhye \\

Sentence: b utsam juṣasva madhumantam arvant samudriyaṁ sadanam ā viśasva \\\\

Verse: 88

Sentence: a ghṛtam mimikṣe ghṛtam asya yonir ghṛte śrito ghṛtam v asya dhāma \\

Sentence: b anuṣvadham ā vaha mādayasva svāhākṛtam vṛṣabha vakṣi havyam \\\\

Verse: 89

Sentence: a samudrād ūrmir madhumāṁ ud ārad upāṁśunā sam amṛtatvam ānaṭ \\

Sentence: b ghṛtasya nāma guhyam yad asti jihvā devānām amṛtasya nābhiḥ \\\\

Verse: 90

Sentence: a vayam nāma pra bravāmā ghṛtasyāsmin yajñe dhārayāmā namobhiḥ \\

Sentence: b upa brahmā śṛṇavac chasyamānam catuḥśṛṅgo vamīd gaura etat \\\\

Verse: 91

Sentence: a catvāri śṛṅgā trayo asya pādā dve śīrṣe sapta hastāso asya \\

Sentence: b tridhā baddho vṛṣabho roravīti maho devo martyāṁ ā viveśa \\\\

Verse: 92

Sentence: a tridhā hitam paṇibhir guhyamānam gavi devāso ghṛtam anv avindan \\

Sentence: b indra ekaṁ sūrya ekam jajāna venād ekaṁ svadhayā niṣ ṭatakṣuḥ \\\\

Verse: 93

Sentence: a etā arṣanti hṛdyāt samudrāc chatavrajā ripuṇā nāvacakṣe \\

Sentence: b ghṛtasya dhārā abhi cākaśīmi hiraṇyayo vetaso madhya āsām \\\\

Verse: 94

Sentence: a samyak sravanti sarito na dhenā antar hṛdā manasā pūyamānāḥ \\

Sentence: b ete arṣanty ūrmayo ghṛtasya mṛgā iva kṣipaṇor īṣamāṇāḥ \\\\

Verse: 95

Sentence: a sindhor iva prādhvane śūghanāso vātapramiyaḥ patayanti yahvāḥ \\

Sentence: b ghṛtasya dhārā aruṣo na vājī kāṣṭhā bhindann ūrmibhiḥ pinvamānaḥ \\\\

Verse: 96

Sentence: a abhi pravanta samaneva yoṣāḥ kalyāṇyaḥ smayamānāso agnim \\

Sentence: b ghṛtasya dhārāḥ samidho nasanta tā juṣāṇo haryati jātavedāḥ \\\\

Verse: 97

Sentence: a kanyā iva vahatum etavā u añjy añjānā abhi cākaśīmi \\

Sentence: b yatra somaḥ sūyate yatra yajño ghṛtasya dhārā abhi tat pavante \\\\

Verse: 98

Sentence: a abhy arṣata suṣṭutim gavyam ājim asmāsu bhadrā draviṇāni dhatta \\

Sentence: b imam yajñam nayata devatā no ghṛtasya dhārā madhumat pavante \\\\

Verse: 99

Sentence: a dhāman te viśvam bhuvanam adhi śritam antaḥ samudre hṛdy antar āyuṣi \\

Sentence: b apām anīke samithe ya ābhṛtas tam aśyāma madhumantam ta ūrmim \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a āyur yajñena kalpatām prāṇo yajñena kalpatām cakṣur yajñena kalpatāṁ śrotram yajñena kalpatām vāg yajñena kalpatām mano yajñena kalpatām ātmā yajñena kalpatām brahmā yajñena kalpatām jyotir yajñena kalpatāṁ svar yajñena kalpatām pṛṣṭham yajñena kalpatām yajño yajñena kalpatām \\

Sentence: c svar devā aganmāmṛtā abhūma prajāpateḥ prajā abhūma veṭ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a vājasya nu prasave mātaram mahīm aditim nāma vacasā karāmahe \\

Sentence: b yasyām idam viśvam bhuvanam āviveśa tasyām no devaḥ savitā dharma sāviṣat \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a viśve adya maruto viśva ūtī viśve bhavantv agnayaḥ samiddhāḥ \\

Sentence: b viśve no devā avasā gamantu viśvam astu draviṇam vājo asme \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a vājo naḥ sapta pradiśaś catasro vā parāvataḥ \\

Sentence: b vājo no viśvair devair dhanasātāv ihāvatu \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a vājo no adya pra suvāti dānam vājo devāṁ ṛtubhiḥ kalpayāti \\

Sentence: b vājo hi mā sarvavīram jajāna viśvā āśā vājapatir jayeyam \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a vajaḥ purastād uta madhyato no vājo devān haviṣā vardhayāti \\

Sentence: b vājo hi mā sarvavīram cakāra sarvā āśā vājapatir bhaveyam \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a sam mā sṛjāmi payasā pṛthivyāḥ sam mā sṛjāmy adbhir oṣadhībhiḥ \\

Sentence: b so ham vājaṁ saneyam agne \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a payaḥ pṛthivyām paya oṣadhīṣu payo divy antarikṣe payo dhāḥ \\

Sentence: b payasvatīḥ pradiśaḥ santu mahyam \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a devasya tvā savituḥ prasavṝ śvinor bāhubhyām pūṣṇo hastābhyām \\

Sentence: b sarasvatyai vāco yantur yantreṇāgneḥ sāmrājyenābhi ṣiñcāmi \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a ṛtāṣāḍ ṛtadhāmāgnir gandharvas tasyauṣadhayo psaraso mudo nāma \\

Sentence: b sa na idam brahma kṣatram pātu tasmai svāhā vāṭ tābhyaḥ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a saṁhito viśvasāmā sūryo gandharvas tasya marīcayo psarasa āyuvo nāma \\

Sentence: b sa na idam brahma kṣatram pātu tasmai svāhā vāṭ tābhyaḥ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a suṣumṇaḥ sūryaraśmiś candramā gandharvas tasya nakṣatrāṇy apsaraso bhekurayo nāma \\

Sentence: b sa na idam brahma kṣatram pātu tasmai svāhā vāṭ tābhyaḥ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a iṣiro viśvavyacā vāto gandharvas tasyapo apsarasa ūrjo nāma \\

Sentence: b sa na idam brahma kṣatram pātu tasmai svāhā vāṭ tābhyaḥ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a bhujyuḥ suparṇo yajño gandharvas tasya dakṣiṇā apsarasa stāvā nāma \\

Sentence: b sa na idam brahma kṣatram pātu tasmai svāhā vāṭ tābhyaḥ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a prajāpatir viśvakarmā mano gandharvas tasya ṛksāmāny apsarasa eṣṭayo nāma \\

Sentence: b sa na idam brahma kṣatram pātu tasmai svāhā vāṭ tābhyaḥ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a sa no bhuvanasya pate prajāpate yasya ta upari gṛhā yasya veha \\

Sentence: b asmai brahmaṇe smai kṣatrāya mahi śarma yaccha svāhā \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a samudro si nabhasvān ārdradānuḥ śambhūr mayobhūr abhi mā vāhi svāhā \\

Sentence: b māruto si marutām gaṇaḥ śambhūr mayobhūr abhi mā vāhi svāhā \\

Sentence: c avasyūr asi duvasvāñ chambhūr mayobhūr abhi mā vāhi svāhā \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a yās te agne sūrye ruco divam ātanvanti raśmibhiḥ \\

Sentence: b tābhir no adya sarvābhī ruce janāya nas kṛdhi \\\\

Verse: 47

Sentence: a yā vo devāḥ sūrye ruco goṣv aśveṣu yā rucaḥ \\

Sentence: b indrāgnī tābhiḥ sarvābhī rucam no dhatta bṛhaspate \\\\

Verse: 48

Sentence: a rucam no dhehi brāhmaṇeṣu rucaṁ rājasu nas kṛdhi \\

Sentence: b rucam viśyeṣu śūdreṣu mayi dhehi rucā rucam \\\\

Verse: 49

Sentence: a tat tvā yāmi brahmaṇā vandamānas tad ā śāste yajamāno havirbhiḥ \\

Sentence: b aheḍamāno varuṇeha bodhy uruśaṁsa mā na āyuḥ pra moṣīḥ \\\\

Verse: 50

Sentence: a svar ṇa gharmaḥ svāhā \\

Sentence: b svar ṇārkaḥ svāhā \\

Sentence: c svar ṇa śukraḥ svāhā \\

Sentence: d svar ṇa jyotiḥ svāhā \\

Sentence: e svar ṇa sūryaḥ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 51

Sentence: a agnim yunajmi śavasā ghṛtena divyaṁ suparṇam vayasā bṛhantam \\

Sentence: b tena vayam gamema bradhnasya viṣṭapaṁ svo ruhāṇā adhi nākam uttamam \\\\

Verse: 52

Sentence: a imau te pakṣāv ajarau patatriṇau yābhyāṁ rakṣāṁsy apahaṁsy agne \\

Sentence: b tābhyām patema sukṛtām u lokam yatra ṛṣayo jagmuḥ prathamajāḥ purāṇāḥ \\\\

Verse: 53

Sentence: a indur dakṣaḥ śyena ṛtāvā hiraṇyapakṣaḥ śakuno bhuraṇyuḥ \\

Sentence: b mahānt sadhasthe dhruva ā niṣatto namas te astu mā mā hiṁsīḥ \\\\

Verse: 54

Sentence: a divo mūrdhāsi pṛthivyā nābhir ūrg apām oṣadhīnām \\

Sentence: b viśvāyuḥ śarma saprathā namas pathe \\\\

Verse: 55

Sentence: a viśvasya mūrdhann adhi tiṣṭhasi śritaḥ samudre te hṛdayam apsv āyur apo dattodadhim bhintta \\

Sentence: b divas parjanyād antarikṣāt pṛthivyās tato no vṛṣṭyāva \\\\

Verse: 56

Sentence: a iṣṭo yajño bhṛgubhir āśīrdā vasubhiḥ \\

Sentence: b tasya na iṣṭasya prītasya draviṇehā gameḥ \\\\

Verse: 57

Sentence: a iṣṭo agnir āhutaḥ pipartu na iṣṭaṁ haviḥ \\

Sentence: b svagedam devebhyo namaḥ \\\\

Verse: 58

Sentence: a yad ākūtāt samasusrod dhṛdo vā manaso vā sambhṛtam cakṣuṣo vā \\

Sentence: b tad anu preta sukṛtām u olkam yatra ṛṣayo jagmuḥ prathamajāḥ purāṇāḥ \\\\

Verse: 59

Sentence: a etaṁ sadhastha pari te dadāmi yam āvahāc chevadhim jātavedāḥ \\

Sentence: b anvāgantā yajñapatir vo atra taṁ sma jānīta parame vyoman \\\\

Verse: 60

Sentence: a etam jānātha parame vyoman devāḥ sadhasthā vida rūpam asya \\

Sentence: b yad āgacchāt pathibhir devayānair iṣṭāpūrte kṛṇavāthāvir asmai \\\\

Verse: 61

Sentence: a ud budhyasvāgne prati jāgṛhi tvam iṣṭāpūrte saṁ sṛjethām ayam ca \\

Sentence: b asmint sadhasthe adhy uttarasmin visve devā yajamānāś ca sīdata \\\\

Verse: 62

Sentence: a yena vahasi sahasram yenāgne sarvavedasam \\

Sentence: b tenemam yajñam no naya svar deveṣu gantave \\\\

Verse: 63

Sentence: a prastareṇa paridhinā srucā vedyā ca barhiṣā \\

Sentence: b ṛcemam yajñam no naya svar deveṣu gantave \\\\

Verse: 64

Sentence: a yad dattam yat parādānam yat pūrtam yāś ca dakṣiṇāḥ \\

Sentence: b tad agnir vaiśvakarmaṇaḥ svar deveṣu no dadhat \\\\

Verse: 65

Sentence: a yatra dhārā anapetā madhor ghṛtasya ca yāḥ \\

Sentence: b tad agnir vaiśvakarmaṇaḥ svar deveṣu no dadhat \\\\

Verse: 66

Sentence: a agnir asmi janmanā jātavedā ghṛtam me cakṣur amṛtam ma āsan \\

Sentence: b arkas tridhātū rajaso vimāno jasro gharmo havir asmi nāma \\\\

Verse: 67

Sentence: a ṛco nāmāsmi yajūṁṣi nāmāsmi sāmāni nāmāsmi \\

Sentence: b ye agnayaḥ prāñcajanyā asyām pṛthivyām adhi \\

Sentence: c teṣām asi tvam uttamaḥ pra no jīvatave suva \\\\

Verse: 68

Sentence: a vārtrahatyāya śavase pṛtanāṣāhyāya ca \\

Sentence: b indra tvā vartayāmasi \\\\

Verse: 69

Sentence: a sahadānum puruhūta kṣiyantam ahastam indra sam piṇak kuṇārum \\

Sentence: b abhi vṛtram vardhamānam piyārum apādam indra tavasā jaghantha \\\\

Verse: 70

Sentence: a vi na indra mṛdho jahi nīcā yaccha pṛtanyataḥ \\

Sentence: b yo asmāṁ abhidāsaty adharam gamayā tamaḥ \\\\

Verse: 71

Sentence: a mṛgo na bhīmaḥ kucaro giriṣṭhāḥ parāvata ā jaganthā parasyāḥ \\

Sentence: b sṛkaṁ saṁśāya pavim indra tigmam vi śatrūn tāḍhi vi mṛdho nudasva \\\\

Verse: 72

Sentence: a vaiśvānaro na ūtaya ā pra yātu parāvataḥ \\

Sentence: b agnir naḥ suṣṭutīr upa \\\\

Verse: 73

Sentence: a pṛṣṭo divi pṛṣṭo agniḥ pṛthivyām pṛṣṭo viśvā oṣadhīr ā viveśa \\

Sentence: b vaiśvānaraḥ sahasā pṛṣṭo agniḥ sa no divā sa riṣas pātu naktam \\\\

Verse: 74

Sentence: a aśyāma te kāmam agne tavotī aśyāma rayiṁ rayivaḥ suvīram \\

Sentence: b aśyāma vājam abhi vājayanto śyāma dyumnam ajarājaram te \\\\

Verse: 75

Sentence: a vayam te adya rarimā hi kāmam uttānahastā namasopasadya \\

Sentence: b yajiṣṭhena manasā yakṣi devān asredhatā manmanā vipro agne \\\\

Verse: 76

Sentence: a dhāmacchad agnir indro brahmā devo bṛhaspatiḥ \\

Sentence: b sacetaso viśve devāyajñam prāvantu naḥ śubhe \\\\

Verse: 77

Sentence: a tvam yaviṣṭha dāśuṣo nṝṁḥ pāhi śṛṇudhī giraḥ \\

Sentence: b rakṣā tokam uta tmanā \\\\

## Paragraph: 19

Verse: 1

Sentence: a svādvīm tvā svādunā tīvrām tīvreṇāmṛtām amṛtena \\

Sentence: b madhumatīm madhumatā sṛjāmi saṁ somena \\

Sentence: c somo si \\

Sentence: d aśvibhyām pacyasva \\

Sentence: e sarasvatyai pacyasva \\

Sentence: f indrāya sutrāmṇe pacyasva \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a parīto ṣiñcatā sutaṁ somo ya uttamaṁ haviḥ \\

Sentence: b dadhanvā yo naryo apsv antar ā suṣāva somam adribhiḥ \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a vāyoḥ pūtaḥ pavitreṇa pratyaṅk somo atidrutaḥ \\

Sentence: b indrasya yujyaḥ sakhā \\

Sentence: c vāyoḥ pūtaḥ pavitreṇa pratyaṅk somo atidrutaḥ \\

Sentence: d indrasya yujyaḥ sakhā \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a punāti te parisrutaṁ somaṁ sūryasya duhitā \\

Sentence: b vāreṇa śaśvatā tanā \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a brahma kṣatram pavate teja indriyaṁ surayā somaḥ suta āsuto madāya \\

Sentence: b śukreṇa deva devatāḥ pipṛgdhi rasenānnam yajamānāya dhehi \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a kuvid aṅga yavamanto vayam cid yathā dānty anupūrvam viyūya \\

Sentence: b ihehaiṣām kṛṇuhi bhojanāni ye barhiṣo namaūktim yajanti \\

Sentence: c upayāmagṛhīto sy aśvibhyām tvā sarasvatyai tvendrāya tvā sutrāmṇe \\

Sentence: d eṣa te yonis tejase tvā vīryāya tvā balāya tvā \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a nānā hi vām devahitaṁ sadas kṛtam mā saṁ sṛkṣāthām parame vyoman \\

Sentence: b surā tvam asi śuṣmiṇī soma eṣa mā mā hiṁsīḥ svām yonim āviśantī \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a upayāmagṛhīto sy āśvinam tejaḥ sārasvatam vīryam aindram balam \\

Sentence: b eṣa te yonir modāya tvānandāya tvā mahase tvā \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a tejo si tejo mayi dhehi \\

Sentence: b vīryam asi vīryam mayi dhehi \\

Sentence: c balam asi balam mayi dhehi \\

Sentence: d ojo sy ojo mayi dhehi \\

Sentence: e manyur asi manyum mayi dhehi \\

Sentence: f saho si saho mayi dhehi \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a yā vyāghram viṣūcikobhau vṛkam ca rakṣati \\

Sentence: b śyenam patatriṇaṁ siṁhaṁ semam pātv aṁhasaḥ \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a yad āpipeṣa mātaram putraḥ pramudito dhayan \\

Sentence: b etat tad agne anṛṇo bhavāmy ahatau pitarau mayā \\

Sentence: c sampṛca stha sam mā bhadreṇa pṛṅkta \\

Sentence: d vipṛca stha vi mā pāpmanā pṛṅkta \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a devā yajñam atanvata bheṣajam bhiṣajāśvinā \\

Sentence: b vācā sarasvatī bhiṣag indrāyendriyāṇi dadhataḥ \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a dīkṣāyai rūpaṁ śaṣpāṇi prāyaṇīyasya tokmāni \\

Sentence: b krayasya rūpaṁ somasya lājāḥ somāṁśavo madhu \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a ātithyarūpam māsaram mahāvīrasya nagnahuḥ \\

Sentence: b rūpam upasadām etat tisro rātrīḥ surāsutā \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a somasya rūpa krītasya parisrut pari ṣicyate \\

Sentence: b aśvibhyām dudgdham bheṣajam indrāyaindraṁ sarasvatyā \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a āsandī rūpaṁ rājāsandyai vedyai kumbhī surādhānī \\

Sentence: b antara uttaravedyā rūpam kārotaro bhiṣak \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a vedyā vediḥ sam āpyate barhiṣā barhir indriyam \\

Sentence: b yūpena yūpa āpyate praṇīto agnir agninā \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a havirdhānam yad aśvināgnīdhram yat sarasvatī \\

Sentence: b indrāyaindraṁ sadas kṛtam patnīśālam gārhapatyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a praiṣebhiḥ praiṣān āpnoty āprībhir āprīr yajñasya \\

Sentence: b prayājebhir anuyājān vaṣaṭkārebhir āhutīḥ \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a paśubhiḥ paśūn āpnoti puroḍāśair havīṁṣy ā \\

Sentence: b chandobhiḥ sāmidhenīr yājyābhir vaṣaṭkārān \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a dhānāḥ karambhaḥ saktavaḥ parīvāpaḥ payo dadhi \\

Sentence: b somasya rūpaṁ haviṣa āmikṣā vājinam madhu \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a dhānānāṁ rūpam kuvalam parīvāpasya godhūmāḥ \\

Sentence: b saktūnāṁ rūpam badaram upavākāḥ karambhasya \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a payaso rūpam yad yavā dadhno rūpam karkandhūni \\

Sentence: b saomasya rūpam vājinaṁ saumyasya rūpam āmikṣā \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a ā śrāvayeti stotriyāḥ pratyāśrāvo anurūpaḥ \\

Sentence: b yajeti dhayyārūpam pragāthā yeyajāmahāḥ \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a ardhaṛcair ukthānāṁ rūpam padair āpnoti nividaḥ \\

Sentence: b praṇavaiḥ śastrāṇāṁ rūpam payasā soma āpyate \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a aśvibhyām prātaḥsavanam indreṇaindram mādhyandinam \\

Sentence: b vaiśvadevaṁ sarasvatyā tṛtīyam āptaṁ savanam \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a vāyavyair vāyavyān āpnoti satena droṇakalaśam \\

Sentence: b kumbhībhyām ambhṛṇau sute sthālībhi sthālīr āpnoti \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a yajurvhir āpyante grahā grahai stomāś ca viṣṭutīḥ \\

Sentence: b chandobhir ukthāśastrāṇi sāmnāvabhṛtha āpyate \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a iḍābhir bhakṣān āpnoti sūktavākenāśiṣaḥ \\

Sentence: b śamyunā patnīsamyājānt samiṣṭayajuṣā saṁsthām \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a vratena dīkṣām āpnoti dīkṣayāpnoti dakṣiṇām \\

Sentence: b dakṣiṇā śraddhām āpnoti śraddhayā satyam āpyate \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a etāvad rūpam yajñasya yad devair brahmaṇā kṛtam \\

Sentence: b tad etat sarvam āpnoti yajñe sautrāmaṇī sute \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a surāvantam barhiṣadaṁ suvīram yajñaṁ hinvanti mahiṣā namobhiḥ \\

Sentence: b dadhānāḥ somam divi devatāsu mademendram yajamānāḥ svarkāḥ \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a yas te rasaḥ sambhṛta oṣadhīṣu somasya śuṣmaḥ surayā sutasya \\

Sentence: b tena jinva yajamānam madena sarasvatīm aśvināv indram agnim \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a yam aśvinā namucer āsurād adhi sarasvaty asunod indriyāya \\

Sentence: b imam taṁ śukram madhumantam induṁ somaṁ rājānam iha bhakṣayāmi \\\\

{K21,1,34}

Verse: 35

Sentence: a yad atra riptaṁ rasinaḥ sutasya yad indro apibac chacībhiḥ \\

Sentence: b aham tad asya manasā śivena somaṁ rājānam iha bhakṣayāmi \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a pitṛbhyaḥ svadhāyibhyaḥ svadhā namaḥ \\

Sentence: b pitāmahebhyaḥ svadhāyibhyaḥ svadhā namaḥ \\

Sentence: c prapitāmahebhyaḥ svadhāyibhyaḥ svadhā namaḥ \\

Sentence: d akṣan pitaraḥ \\

Sentence: e amīmadanta pitaraḥ \\

Sentence: f atītṛpanta pitaraḥ \\

Sentence: g pitaraḥ śundhadhvam \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a punantu mā pitaraḥ somyāsaḥ punantu mā pitāmahāḥ \\

Sentence: b punantu prapitāmahāḥ pavitreṇa śatāyuṣā \\

Sentence: c punantu mā pitāmahāḥ somyāsaḥ punantu prapitāmahāḥ \\

Sentence: d pavitreṇa śatāyuṣā viśvam āyur vy aśnavai \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a agna āyūṁṣi pavasva ā suvorjam iṣam ca naḥ \\

Sentence: b āre bādhasva ducchunām \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a punantu mā devajanāḥ punantu manasā dhiyaḥ \\

Sentence: b punantu viśvā bhūtāni jātavedaḥ punīhi mā \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a pavitreṇa punīhi mā śukreṇa deva dīdyat \\

Sentence: b agne kratvā kratūṁr anu \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a yat te pavitram arciṣy agne vitatam antarā \\

Sentence: b brahma tena punātu mā \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a pavamānaḥ so adya naḥ pavitreṇa vicarṣaṇiḥ \\

Sentence: b yaḥ potā sa punātu mā \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a ubhābhyām deva savitaḥ pavitreṇa savena ca \\

Sentence: b mām punīhi viśvataḥ \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a vaiśvadevī punatī devy āgād yasyām imā bahvyas tanvo vītapṛṣṭhāḥ \\

Sentence: b tayā madantaḥ sadhamādeṣu vayaṁ syāma patayo rayīṇām \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a ye samānāḥ samanasaḥ pitaro yamarājye \\

Sentence: b teṣām lokaḥ svadhā namo yajño deveṣu kalpatām \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a ye samānāḥ samanaso jīvā jīveṣu māmakāḥ \\

Sentence: b teṣāṁ śrīr mayi kalpatām asmim loke sataṁ samāḥ \\\\

Verse: 47

Sentence: a dve sṛtī aśṛnavam pitṝṇām aham devānām uta martyānām \\

Sentence: b tābhyām idam viśvam ejat sam eti yad antarā pitaram mātaram ca \\\\

Verse: 48

Sentence: a idaṁ haviḥ prajananam me astu daśavīraṁ sarvagaṇaṁ svastaye \\

Sentence: b ātmasani prajāsani paśusani lokasany abhayasani \\

Sentence: c agniḥ prajām bahulām me karotv annam payo reto asmāsu dhatta \\\\

Verse: 49

Sentence: a ud īratām avara ut parāsa un madhyamāḥ pitaraḥ somyāsaḥ \\

Sentence: b asum ya īyur avṛkā ṛtajñās te no vantu pitaro haveṣu \\\\

Verse: 50

Sentence: a aṅgiraso naḥ pitaro navagvā atharvāṇo bhṛgavaḥ somyāsaḥ \\

Sentence: b teṣām vayaṁ sumatau yajñiyānām api bhadre saumanase syāma \\\\

Verse: 51

Sentence: a ye naḥ pūrve pitaraḥ somyāso nūhire somapītham vasiṣṭhāḥ \\

Sentence: b tebhir yamaḥ saṁrarāṇo havīṁṣy uśann uśadbhiḥ pratikāmam attu \\\\

Verse: 52

Sentence: a tvaṁ soma pra cikito manīṣā tvaṁ rajiṣṭham anu neṣi panthām \\

Sentence: b tava praṇītī pitaro na indo deveṣu ratnam abhajanta dhīrāḥ \\\\

Verse: 53

Sentence: a tvayā hi naḥ pitaraḥ soma pūrve karmāṇi cakruḥ pavamāna dhīrāḥ \\

Sentence: b vanvann avātaḥ paridhīṁr aporṇu vīrebhir aśvair maghavā bhavā naḥ \\\\

Verse: 54

Sentence: a tvaṁ soma pitṛbhiḥ samvidāno nu dyāvāpṛthivī ā tatantha \\

Sentence: b tasmai ta indo haviṣā vidhema vayaṁ syāma patayo rayīṇām \\\\

Verse: 55

Sentence: a barhiṣadaḥ pitara ūty arvāg imā vo havyā cakṛmā juṣadhvam \\

Sentence: b ta ā gatāvasā śamtamenāthā naḥ śam yor arapo dadhāta \\\\

Verse: 56

Sentence: a āham pitṝn suvidatrāṁ avitsi napātam ca vikramaṇam ca viṣṇoḥ \\

Sentence: b barhiṣado ye svadhayā sutasya bhajanta pitvas ta ihāgamiṣṭhāḥ \\\\

Verse: 57

Sentence: a upahūtāḥ pitaraḥ somyāso barhiṣyṝṣu nidhiṣu priyeṣu \\

Sentence: b ta ā gamantu ta iha śruvantv adhi bruvantu tṝ vantv asmān \\\\

Verse: 58

Sentence: a ā yantu naḥ pitaraḥ somyāso gniṣvāttāḥ pathibhir devayānaiḥ \\

Sentence: b asmin yajñe svadhayā madanto dhi bruvantu tṝ vantv asmān \\\\

Verse: 59

Sentence: a agniṣvāttāḥ pitara eha gacchata sadaḥ-sadaḥ sadata supraṇītayaḥ \\

Sentence: b attā havīṁṣi prayatāni barhiṣy athā rayiṁ sarvavīram dadhātana \\\\

Verse: 60

Sentence: a ye agniṣvāttā ye anagniṣvāttā madhye divaḥ svadhayā mādayante \\

Sentence: b tebhyaḥ svarāḍ asunītim etām yathāvaśam tanvam kalpayāti \\\\

Verse: 61

Sentence: a agniṣvāttāṁ ṛtumato havāmahe nārāśaṁse somapītham ya āśuḥ \\

Sentence: b te no viprāsaḥ suhavā bhavantu vayaṁ syāma patayo rayīṇām \\\\

Verse: 62

Sentence: a ācyā jānu dakṣiṇato niṣadyemam yajñam abhi gṛṇīta viśve \\

Sentence: b mā hiṁsiṣṭa pitaraḥ kena cin no yad va āgaḥ puruṣatā karāma \\\\

Verse: 63

Sentence: a āsīnāso aruṇīnām upasthe rayim dhatta dāśuṣe martyāya \\

Sentence: b putrebhyaḥ pitaras tasya vasvaḥ pra yacchata ta ihorjam dadhāta \\\\

Verse: 64

Sentence: a yam agne kavyavāhana tvam cin manyase rayim \\

Sentence: b tam no gīrbhiḥ śravāyyam devatrā panayā yujam \\\\

{K21,1,64}

Verse: 65

Sentence: a yo agniḥ kavyavāhanaḥ pitṝn yakṣad ṛtāvṛdhaḥ \\

Sentence: b pred u havyāni vocati devebhyaś ca pitṛbhya ā \\\\

Verse: 66

Sentence: a tvam agna īḍitaḥ kavyavāhanāvāḍ ḍhavyāni surabhīṇi kṛtvī \\

Sentence: b prādāḥ pitṛbhyaḥ svadhayā te akṣann addhi tvam deva prayatā havīṁṣi \\\\

Verse: 67

Sentence: a ye ceha pitaro ye ca neha yāṁś ca vidma yāṁ u ca na pravidma \\

Sentence: b tvam vettha yati te jātavedaḥ svadhābhir yajñaṁ sukṛtam juṣasva \\\\

Verse: 68

Sentence: a idam pitṛbhyo namo astv adya ye pūrvāso ya uparāsa īyuḥ \\

Sentence: b ye pārthive rajasy ā niṣattā ye vā nūnaṁ suvṛjanāsu vikṣu \\\\

Verse: 69

Sentence: a adhā yathā naḥ pitaraḥ parāsaḥ pratnāso agna ṛtam āśuṣāṇāḥ \\

Sentence: b śucīd ayan dīdhitim ukthaśāsaḥ kṣāmā bhindanto aruṇīr apa vran \\\\

Verse: 70

Sentence: a uśantas tvā ni dhīmahy uśantaḥ sam idhīmahi \\

Sentence: b uśann uśata ā vaha pitṝn haviṣe attave \\\\

Verse: 71

Sentence: a apām phenena namuceḥ śira indrod avartayaḥ \\

Sentence: b viśvā yad ajaya spṛdhaḥ \\\\

Verse: 72

Sentence: a somo rājāmṛtaṁ suta ṛjīṣeṇājahān mṛtyum \\

Sentence: b ṛtena satyam indriyam vipānaṁ śukram andhasa indrasyendriyam idam payo mṛtam madhu \\\\

Verse: 73

Sentence: a adbhyaḥ kṣīram vy apibat kruṅṅ āṅgiraso dhiyā \\

Sentence: b ṛtena satyam indriyam vipānaṁ śukram andhasa indrasyendriyam idam payo mṛtam madhu \\\\

Verse: 74

Sentence: a somam adbhyo vy apibac chandasā haṁsaḥ śuciṣat \\

Sentence: b ṛtena satyam indriyam vipānaṁ śukram andhasa indrasyendriyam idam payo mṛtam madhu \\\\

Verse: 75

Sentence: a annāt parisruto rasam brahmaṇā vy apibat kṣatram payaḥ somam prajāpatiḥ \\

Sentence: b ṛtena satyam indriyam vipānaṁ śukram andhasa indrasyendriyam idam payo mṛtam madhu \\\\

Verse: 76

Sentence: a reto mūtram vi jahāti yonim praviśad rayim \\

Sentence: b garbho jarāyuṇāvṛta ulvam jahāti janmanā \\

Sentence: c ṛtena satyam indriyam vipānaṁ śukram andhasa indrasyendriyam idam payo mṛtam madhu \\\\

Verse: 77

Sentence: a dṛṣṭvā rūpe vyākarot satyānṛte prajāpatiḥ \\

Sentence: b aśraddhām anṛte dadhāc chraddhāṁ satye prajāpatiḥ \\

Sentence: c ṛtena satyam indriyam vipānaṁ śukram andhasa indrasyendriyam idam payo mṛtam madhu \\\\

Verse: 78

Sentence: a vedena rūpe vy apibat sutāsutau prajāpatiḥ \\

Sentence: b ṛtena satyam indriyam vipānaṁ śukram andhasa indrasyendriyam idam payo mṛtam madhu \\\\

Verse: 79

Sentence: a dṛṣṭvā parisruto rasaṁ śukreṇa śukram vy apibat \\

Sentence: b payaḥ somam prajāpatiḥ \\

Sentence: c ṛtena satyam indriyam vipānaṁ śukram andhasa indrasyendriyam idam payo mṛtam madhu \\\\

Verse: 80

Sentence: a sīsena tantram manasā manīṣiṇā ūrṇāsūtreṇa kavayo vayanti \\

Sentence: b aśvinā yajñaṁ savitā sarasvatīndrasya rūpam varuṇo bhiṣajyan \\\\

Verse: 81

Sentence: a tad asya rūpam amṛtaṁ śacībhis tasro dadhur devatāḥ saṁrarāṇāḥ \\

Sentence: b lomāni śaṣpair bahudhā na tokmabhis tvag asya māṁsam abhavan na lājāḥ \\\\

Verse: 82

Sentence: a tad aśvinā bhiṣajā rudravartanī sarasvatī vayati peśo antaram \\

Sentence: b asthi majjānam māsaraiḥ kārotareṇa dadhato gavām tvaci \\\\

Verse: 83

Sentence: a sarasvatī manasā peśalam vasu nāsatyābhyām vayati darśatam vapuḥ \\

Sentence: b rasam parisrutā na rohitam nagnahur dhīras tasaram na vema \\\\

Verse: 84

Sentence: a payasā śukram amṛtam janitraṁ surayā mūtrāj janayanta retaḥ \\

Sentence: b apāmatim durmatim bādhamānā ūvadhyam vātaṁ sabvam tad ārāt \\\\

Verse: 85

Sentence: a indraḥ sutrāmā hṛdayeṇa satyam puroḍāśena savitā jajāna \\

Sentence: b yakṛt klomānam varuṇo bhiṣajyan matasne vāyavyair na mināti pittam \\\\

Verse: 86

Sentence: a āntrāṇi sthālīr madhu pinvamānā gudāḥ pātrāṇi sudughā na dhenuḥ \\

Sentence: b śyenasya patram na plīhā śacībhir āsandī nābhir udaram na mātā \\\\

Verse: 87

Sentence: a kumbho vaniṣṭhur janitā śacībhie yasminn agre yonyām garbho antaḥ \\

Sentence: b plāśir vyaktaḥ śatadhāra utso duhe na kumbhī svadhām pitṛbhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 88

Sentence: a mukhaṁ sad asya śira it satena jihvā pavitram aśvināsant sarasvatī \\

Sentence: b capyam na pāyur bhiṣag asya vālo vastir na śepo harasā tarasvī \\\\

Verse: 89

Sentence: a aśvibhyām cakṣur amṛtam grahābhyām chāgena tejo haviṣā śṛtena \\

Sentence: b pakṣmāṇi godhūmaiḥ kuvalair utāni peśo na śukram asitam vasāte \\\\

Verse: 90

Sentence: a avir na meṣo nasi vīryāya prāṇasya panth amṛto grahābhyām \\

Sentence: b sarasvaty upavākair vyānam nasyāni barhir badarair jajāna \\\\

Verse: 91

Sentence: a indrasya rūpam vṛṣabho balāya karṇābhyāṁ śrotram amṛtam grahābhyām \\

Sentence: b yavā na barir bhruvi kesarāṇi karkandhu jajñe madhu sāragham mukhāt \\\\

Verse: 92

Sentence: a ātmann upasthe na vṛkasya loma mukhe śmaśrūṇi na vyāghraloma \\

Sentence: b keśā na śīrṣan yaśase śriyai śikhā siṁhasya loma tviṣir indriyāṇi \\\\

Verse: 93

Sentence: a aṅgāny ātman bhiṣajā tad aśvinātmānam aṅgaiḥ sam adhāt sarasvatī \\

Sentence: b indrasya rūpaṁ śatamānam āyuś candreṇa jyotir amṛtam dadhānāḥ \\\\

Verse: 94

Sentence: a sarasvatī yonyām garbham antar asar asbhyām patnī sukṛtam bibharti \\

Sentence: b apāṁ rasena varuṇo na sāmnendraṁ śriyai janayann apsu rājā \\\\

Verse: 95

Sentence: a tejaḥ paśūnāṁ havir indriyāvat parisrutā payasā sāragham madhu \\

Sentence: b aśvibhyām dugdham bhiṣajā sarasvatyā sutāsutābhyām amṛtaḥ soma induḥ \\\\

## Paragraph: 20

Verse: 1

Sentence: a kṣatrasya yonir asi kṣatrasya nābhir asi \\

Sentence: b mā tvā hiṁsīn mā mā hiṁsīḥ \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a ni ṣasāda ghṛtavrato varuṇaḥ pastyāsv ā \\

Sentence: b sāmrājyāya sukratuḥ \\

Sentence: c mṛtyoḥ pāhi \\

Sentence: d vidyot pāhi \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a devasya tvā savituḥ prasavṝ śvinor bāhubhyām pūṣṇo hastābhyām \\

Sentence: b aśvinor bhaiṣajyena tejase brahmavarcasāyābhi ṣiñcāmi \\

Sentence: c sarasvatyai bhaiṣajyena vīryāyānnādyāyābhi ṣiñcāmi \\

Sentence: d indrasyendriyeṇa balāya śriyai yaśase bhi ṣiñcāmi \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a ko si katamo si kasmai tvā kāya tvā \\

Sentence: b suśloka sumaṅgala satyarājan \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a śiro me śrīr yaśo mukham tviṣiḥ keśāś ca śmaśrūṇi \\

Sentence: b rājā me prāṇo amṛtaṁ samrāṭ cakṣur virāṭ śrotram \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a jihvā me bhadram vāṅ maho mano manyuḥ svarāḍ bhāmaḥ \\

Sentence: b modāḥ pramodā aṅgulīr aṅgāni mitram me sahaḥ \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a bāhū me balam indriyaṁ hastau me karma vīryam \\

Sentence: b ātmā kṣatram uro mama \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a pṛṣṭīr me rāṣṭram udaram aṁsau grīvāś ca śroṇī \\

Sentence: b ūrū aratnī jānunī viśo me ṅgāni sarvataḥ \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a nābhir me cittam vijñānam pāyur me pacitir bhasat \\

Sentence: b ānandanandāv āṇḍau me bhagaḥ saubhāgyam pasaḥ \\

Sentence: c jaṅghābhyām pādbhyām dharmo smi viśi rājā pratiṣṭhitaḥ \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a prati kṣatre prati tiṣṭhāmi rāṣṭre praty aśveṣu prati tiṣṭhāmi goṣu \\

Sentence: b praty aṅgeṣu prati tiṣṭhāmy ātman prati prāṇeṣu kṣatre prati tiṣṭhāmi puṣṭe prati dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ prati tiṣṭhāmi \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a trayā devā ekādaśa trayastriṁśāḥ surādhasaḥ \\

Sentence: b bṛhaspatipurohitā devasya savituḥ save \\

Sentence: c devā devair avantu mā \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a prathamā dvitīyair dvitīyās tṛtīyais tṛtīyāḥ satyena satyam yajñena yajño yajurbhir yajūṁṣi sāmabhiḥ sāmāny ṛgbhir ṛcaḥ puronuvākyābhiḥ puronuvākyā yājyābhir yājyā vaṣaṭkārair vaṣaṭkārā āhutibhir ahutayo me kāmānt sam ardhayantu bhūḥ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a lomāni prayatir mama tvaṅ ma ānatir āgatiḥ \\

Sentence: b māṁsam ma upanatir vasv asthi majjā ma ānatiḥ \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a yad devā devaheḍanam devāsaś cakṛmā vayam \\

Sentence: b agnir mā tasmād enaso viśvān muñcatv aṁhasaḥ \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a yadi divā yadi naktam enāṁsi cakṛmā vayam \\

Sentence: b vāyur mā tasmād enaso viśvān muñcatv aṁhasaḥ \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a yadi jāgrad yadi svapna enāṁsi cakṛmā vayam \\

Sentence: b sūryo mā tasmād enaso viśvān muñcatv aṁhasaḥ \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a yad grāme yad araṇye yat sabhāyām yad indriye \\

Sentence: b yac chūdre yad arye yad enaś cakṛmā vayam yad ekasyādhi dharmaṇi tasyāvayajanam asi \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a yad āpo aghnyā iti varuṇeti śapāmahe tato varuṇa no muñca \\

Sentence: b avabhṛtha nicumpuṇa nicerur asi nicumpuṇaḥ \\

Sentence: c ava devair devakṛtam eno yakṣy ava martyair martyakṛtam \\

Sentence: d pururāvṇo deva riṣas pāhi \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a samudre te hṛdayam apsv antaḥ sam tvā viśantv oṣadhīr utāpaḥ \\

Sentence: b sumitriyā na āpa oṣadhayaḥ santu durmitriyās tasmai santu yo smān dveṣṭi yam ca vayam dviṣmaḥ \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a drupadād iva mumucānaḥ svinnaḥ snāto malād iva \\

Sentence: b pūtam pavitreṇevājyam āpaḥ śundhantu mainasaḥ \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a ud vayam tamasas pari svaḥ paśyanta uttaram \\

Sentence: b devam devatrā sūryam aganma jyotir uttamam \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a apo adyānv acāriṣaṁ rasena sam asṛkṣmahi \\

Sentence: b payasvān agna āgamam tam mā saṁ sṛja varcasā prajayā ca dhanena ca \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a edho sy edhiṣīmahi \\

Sentence: b samid asi tejo si tejo mayi dhehi \\

Sentence: c samā vavarti pṛthivī sam uṣāḥ sam u sūryaḥ \\

Sentence: d sam u viśvam idam jagat \\

Sentence: e vaiśvānarajyotir bhūyāsam vibhūn kāmān vy aśnavai bhūḥ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a abhyā dadhāmi samidham agne vratapate tvayi \\

Sentence: b vratam ca śraddhām copaimīndhe tvā dīkṣito aham \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a yatra brahma ca kṣatram ca samyañco carataḥ saha \\

Sentence: b tam lokam puṇyam pra jñeṣam yatra devāḥ sahāgninā \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a yatrendraś ca vāyuś ca samyañco carataḥ saha \\

Sentence: b tam lokam puṇyam pra jñeṣam yatra sedir na vidyate \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a aṁśunā te aṁśuḥ pṛcyatām paruṣā paruḥ \\

Sentence: b gandhas te somam avatu madāya raso acyutaḥ \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a siñcanti pari ṣiñcanty ut siñcanti punanti ca \\

Sentence: b surāyai babhrvai made kimtvo vadati kimtvaḥ \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a dhānāvantam karambhiṇam apūpavantam ukthinam \\

Sentence: b indra prātar juṣasva naḥ \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a bṛhad indrāya gāyata maruto vṛtrahantamam \\

Sentence: b yena jyotir ajanayann ṛtāvṛdho devam devāya jāgṛvi \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a adhvaryo adribhiḥ sutaṁ somam pavitra ā naya \\

Sentence: b punīhīndrāya pātave \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a yo bhūtānām adhipatir yasmim lokā adhi śritāḥ \\

Sentence: b ya īśe mahato mahāṁs tena gṛhṇāmi tvām aham mayi gṛhṇāmi tvām aham \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a upayāmagṛhīto sy aśvibhyām tvā sarasvatyai tvendrāya tvā sutrāmṇe \\

Sentence: b eṣa te yonir aśvibhyām tvā sarasvatyai tvendrāya tvā sutrāmṇe \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a prāṇapā me apānapāś cakṣuṣpāḥ śrotrapāś ca me \\

Sentence: b vāco me viśvabheṣajo manaso si vilāyakaḥ \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a aśvinakṛtasya te sarasvatikṛtasyendreṇa sumtrāmṇā kṛtasya \\

Sentence: b upahūta upahūtasya bhakṣayāmi \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a samiddha indra uṣasām anīke purorucā pūrvakṛd vāvṛdhānaḥ \\

Sentence: b tribhir devais triṁśatā vajrabāhur jaghāna vṛtram vi duro vavāra \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a narāśaṁsaḥ prati śūro mimānas tanūnapāt prati yajñasya dhāma \\

Sentence: b gobhir vapāvān madhunā samañjan hiraṇyaiś candrī yajati pracetāḥ \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a īḍito devair harivāṁ abhiṣṭir ājuhvāno haviṣā śardhamānaḥ \\

Sentence: b puramdaro gotabhid vajrabāhur ā yātu yajñam upa no juṣāṇaḥ \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a juṣāṇo barhir harivān na indraḥ prācīnaṁ sīdat pradiśā pṛthivyāḥ \\

Sentence: b uruprathāḥ prathamānaṁ syonam ādityair aktam vasubhiḥ sajoṣāḥ \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a indram duraḥ kavaṣyo dhāvamānā vṛṣāṇam yantu janayaḥ supatnīḥ \\

Sentence: b dvāro devīr abhito vi śrayantāṁ suvīrā vīram prathamānā mahobhiḥ \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a uṣāsānaktā bṛhatī bṛhantam payasvatī sudughe śūram indram \\

Sentence: b tantum tatam peśasā samvayantī devānām devam yajataḥ surukme \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a daivyā mimānā manuṣaḥ purutrā hotārāv indram prathamā suvācā \\

Sentence: b mūrhdan yajñasya madhunā dadhānā prācīnam jyotir haviṣā vṛdhātaḥ \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a tisro devīr haviṣā vardhamānā indram juṣāṇā janayo na patnīḥ \\

Sentence: b acchinnam tantum payasā sarasvatīḍā devī bhāratī viśvatūrtiḥ \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a tvaṣṭā dadhac chuṣmam indrāya vṛṣṇe pāko ciṣṭur yaśase purūṇi \\

Sentence: b vṛṣā yajan vṛṣaṇam bhūriretā mūrdhan yajñasya sam anaktu devān \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a vanaspatir avasṛṣṭo na pāśais tmanyā samañjañ chamitā na devaḥ \\

Sentence: b indrasya havyair jaṭharam pṛṇānaḥ svadāti yajñam madhunā ghṛtena \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a stokānā, indum prati śūra indro vṛṣāyamāṇo vṛṣabhas turāṣāṭ \\

Sentence: b ghṛtapruṣā manasā modamānāḥ svāhā devā amṛtā mādayantām \\\\

Verse: 47

Sentence: a ā yātv indro vasa upa na iha stutaḥ sadhamād astu śūraḥ \\

Sentence: b vāvṛdhānas taviṣīr yasya pūrvīr dyaur na kṣatram abhibhūti puṣyāt \\\\

Verse: 48

Sentence: a ā na indro dūrād ā na āsād abhiṣṭikṛd avase yāsad ugraḥ \\

Sentence: b ojiṣṭhebhir nṛpatir vajrabāhuḥ samge samatsu turvaṇiḥ pṛtanyūn \\\\

Verse: 49

Sentence: a ā na indro haribhir yātv acchārvācīno vase rādhase ca \\

Sentence: b tiṣṭhāti vajrī maghavā virapśīmam yajñam anu no vājasātau \\\\

Verse: 50

Sentence: a trātāram indram avitāram indraṁ have-have suhavaṁ śūram indram \\

Sentence: b hvayāmi śakram puruhūtam indraṁ svasti no maghavā dhātv indraḥ \\\\

Verse: 51

Sentence: a indraḥ sutrāmā svavāṁ avobhiḥ sumṛḍīko bhavatu viśvavedāḥ \\

Sentence: b bādhatām dveṣo abhayam kṛṇotu suvīryasya patayaḥ syāma \\\\

Verse: 52

Sentence: a tasya vayaṁ sumatau yajñiyasyāpi bhadre saumanase syāma \\

Sentence: b sa sutrāmā svavāṁ indro asme ārāc cid dveṣaḥ sanutar yuyotu \\\\

Verse: 53

Sentence: a ā mandrair indra haribhir yāhi mayūraromabhiḥ \\

Sentence: b mā tvā ke cin ni yaman vim nā pāśino ti dhanveva tāṁ ihi \\\\

Verse: 54

Sentence: a eved indram vṛṣaṇam vajrabāhum vasiṣṭhāso abhy arcanty arkaiḥ \\

Sentence: b sa na stuto vīravad dhātu gomad yūyam pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ \\\\

Verse: 55

Sentence: a samiddho agnir aśvinā tapto gharmo virāṭ sutaḥ \\

Sentence: b duhe dhenuḥ sarasvatī somaṁ śukram ihendriyam \\\\

Verse: 56

Sentence: a tanūpā bhiṣajā sutṝ svinobhā sarasvatī \\

Sentence: b adhvā rajāṁsīndriyam indrāya pathibhir vahān \\\\

Verse: 57

Sentence: a indrāyenduṁ sarasvatī narāśaṁsena nagnahum \\

Sentence: b adhātām aśvinā madhu bheṣajam bhiṣajā sute \\\\

Verse: 58

Sentence: a ājuhvānā sarasvatīndrāyendriyāṇi vīryam \\

Sentence: b iḍābhir aśvināv iṣaṁ sam ūrjaṁ saṁ rayim dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 59

Sentence: a aśvinā namuceḥ sutaṁ somaṁ śukram parisrutā \\

Sentence: b sarasvatī tām ābharad barhiṣendrāya pātave \\\\

Verse: 60

Sentence: a kavaṣyo na vyacasvatīr aśvibhyām na duro diśaḥ \\

Sentence: b indro na rodasī ubhe duhe kāmānt sarasvatī \\\\

Verse: 61

Sentence: a uṣāsānaktāśvinā divendraṁ sāyam indriyaiḥ \\

Sentence: b samjānāne supeśasā sam añjāte sarasvatyā \\\\

Verse: 62

Sentence: a pātam no aśvinā divā pāhi naktaṁ sarasvati \\

Sentence: b daivyā hotārā bhiṣajā pātām indraṁ sacā sute \\\\

Verse: 63

Sentence: a tisras tredhā sarasvaty aśvinā bhāratīḍā \\

Sentence: b tīvram parisrutā somam indrāya suṣuvur madam \\\\

Verse: 64

Sentence: a aśvinā bheṣajam madhu bheṣajam naḥ sarasvatī \\

Sentence: b indre tvaṣṭā yaśaḥ śriyaṁ rūpaṁ-rūpam adhuḥ sute \\\\

Verse: 65

Sentence: a ṛtuthendro vanaspatiḥ śaśamānaḥ parisrutā \\

Sentence: b kīlālam aśvibhyām madhu duhe dhenuḥ sarasvatī \\\\

Verse: 66

Sentence: a gobhir na somam aśvinā māsareṇa parisrutā \\

Sentence: b sam adhātaṁ sarasvatyā svāhendre sutam madhu \\\\

Verse: 67

Sentence: a aśvinā havir indriyam namucer dhiyā sarasvatī \\

Sentence: b ā śukram āsurād vasu magham indrāya jabhrire \\\\

Verse: 68

Sentence: a yam aśvinā sarasvatī haviṣendram avardhayan \\

Sentence: b sa bidheda valam magham namucāv āsure sacā \\\\

Verse: 69

Sentence: a tam indraṁ sacāśvinobhā sarasvatī \\

Sentence: b dadhānā abhy anūṣata haviṣā yajña indriyaiḥ \\\\

Verse: 70

Sentence: a ya indra indriyam dadhuḥ savitā varuṇo bhagaḥ \\

Sentence: b sa sutrāmā haviṣpatir yajamānāya saścata \\\\

Verse: 71

Sentence: a savitā varuṇo dadhad yajamānāya dāśuṣe \\

Sentence: b ādatta namucer vasu sutrāmā balam indriyam \\\\

Verse: 72

Sentence: a varuṇaḥ kṣatram indriyam bhagena savitā śriyam \\

Sentence: b sutrāmā yaśasā balam dadhānā yajñam āśata \\\\

Verse: 73

Sentence: a aśvinā gobhir indriyam aśvebhir vīryam balam \\

Sentence: b haviṣendraṁ sarasvatī yajamānam avardayan \\\\

Verse: 74

Sentence: a tā nāsatyā supeśasā hiraṇyavartanī narā \\

Sentence: b sarasvatī haviṣmatīndra karmasu no vata \\\\

Sentence: a tā bhiṣajā sukarmaṇā sā sudughā sarasvatī \\

Verse: 76

Sentence: a yuvaṁ surāmam aśvinā namucāv āsure sacā \\

Sentence: b vipipānāḥ sarasvatīndram karmasv āvata \\\\

Verse: 77

Sentence: b yat surāmam vyapibaḥ śacībhiḥ sarasvatī tvā maghavann abhiṣṇak \\\\

Verse: 78

Verse: 79

Verse: 80

Sentence: a gomad ū ṣu ṇāsatyā aśvāvad yātam aśvinā \\

Sentence: b vartī rudrā nṛpāyyam \\\\

## Paragraph: 21

Verse: 1

Sentence: a imam me varuṇa śrudhī havam adyā ca mṛḍaya \\

Sentence: b tvām asvasyur ā cake \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a tat tvā yāmi brahmaṇā vandamānas tad ā śāste yajamāno havirbhiḥ \\

Sentence: b aheḍamāno varuṇeha bodhy uruśaṁsa mā na āyuḥ pra moṣīḥ \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a tvam no agne varuṇasya vidvān devasya heḍo va yāsisīṣṭhāḥ \\

Sentence: b yajiṣṭho vahnitamaḥ śośucāno viśvā dveṣāṁsi pra mumugdhy asmat \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a sa tvam no agne vamo bhavotī nediṣṭho asyā uṣaso vyḥṣṭau \\

Verse: 5

Verse: 6

Verse: 7

Sentence: a sunāvam ā ruheyam asravantīm anāgasam \\

Sentence: b śatāritrāṁ svastaye \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a ā no mitrāvaruṇā ghṛtair gavyūtim ukṣatam \\

Sentence: b madhvā rajāṁsi sukratū \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a pra bāhavā sisṛtam jīvase na ā no gavyūtim ukṣatam ghṛtena \\

Sentence: b ā no jane śravayatam yuvānā śrutam me mitrāvaruṇā havemā \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a śam no bhavantu vājino haveṣu devatātā mitadravaḥ svarkāḥ \\
