Verse: 11

Sentence: a vāje-vāje vata vājino no dhaneṣu viprā amṛtā ṛtajñāḥ \\

Sentence: b asya madhvaḥ pibata mādayadhvam tṛptā yāta pathibhir devayānaiḥ \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a samiddho agniḥ samidhā susamiddho vareṇyaḥ \\

Sentence: b gāyatrī chanda indriyam tryavir gaur vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a tanūnapāc chucivratas tanūpāś ca sarasvatī \\

Sentence: b uṣṇihā chanda indriyam dityavāḍ gaur vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a iḍābhir agnir īḍyaḥ somo devo amartyaḥ \\

Sentence: b anuṣṭup chanda indriyam pañcāvir gaur vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a subarhir agniḥ pūṣaṇvānt stīrṇabarhir amartyaḥ \\

Sentence: b bṛhatī chanda indriyam trivatso gaur vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a duro devīr diśo mahīr brahmā devo bṛhaspatiḥ \\

Sentence: b paṅktiś chanda ihendriyam turyavāḍ gaur vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a uṣe yahvī supeśasā viśve devā amartyāḥ \\

Sentence: b triṣṭup chanda ihendriyam paṣṭhavāḍ gaur vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a daivyā hotārā bhiṣajendreṇa sayujā yujā \\

Sentence: b jagatī chanda indriyam anaḍvān gaur vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a tisra iḍā sarasvatī bhāratī maruto viśaḥ \\

Sentence: b virāṭ chanda ihendriyam dhenur gaur na vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a tvaṣṭā turīpo adbhuta indrāgnī puṣṭivardhanā \\

Sentence: b dvipadā chanda indriyam ukṣā gaur na vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a śamitā no vanaspatiḥ savitā prasuvan bhagam \\

Sentence: b kakup chanda indriyam vaśā vehad vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a svāhā yajñam varuṇaḥ sukṣatro bheṣajam karat \\

Sentence: b aticchandā indriyam bṛhad ṛṣabho gaur vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a vasantena ṛtunā devā vasvavas trivṛtā stutāḥ \\

Sentence: b rathantareṇa tejasā havir indre vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a grīṣmeṇa ṛtunā devā rudrāḥ pañcadaśe stutāḥ \\

Sentence: b bṛhatā yaśasā balaṁ havir indre vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a varṣābhir ṛtunādityā stome saptadaśe stutāḥ \\

Sentence: b vairūpeṇa viśaujasā havir indre vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a śāradena ṛtunā devā ekaviṁśa ṛbhava stutāḥ \\

Sentence: b vairājena śriyā śriyaṁ havir indre vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a hemantena ṛtunā devās triṇave maruta stutāḥ \\

Sentence: b balena śakvarīḥ saho havir indre vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a śaiśireṇa ṛtunā devās trayastriṁśṝ mṛtā stutāḥ \\

Sentence: b satyena revatīḥ kṣatraṁ havir indre vayo dadhuḥ \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat samidhāgnim iḍas pade śvinendraṁ sarasvatīm ajo dhūmro na godhūmaiḥ kuvalair bheṣajam madhu śaṣpair na teja indriyam payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtam madhu vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat tanūnapāt sarasvatī avir meṣo na bheṣajam pathā madhumatā bharann aśvinendrāya vīryam badarair upavākābhir bheṣajam tokmabhiḥ payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtam madhu vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a hotā yakṣan narāśaṁsam na nagnahum patiṁ surayā bheṣajam meṣaḥ sarasvatī bhiṣag ratho na candry aśvinor vapā indrasya vīryam badarair upavākābhir bheṣajam tokmabhiḥ payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtam madhu vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad iḍeḍita ājuhvānaḥ sarasvatīm indram balena vardhayann ṛṣabheṇa gavendriyam aśvinendrāya bheṣajam yavair karkandhubhir madhu lājair na māsaram payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtam madhu vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad barhir ūrṇamradā bhiṣaṅ nāsatyā bhiṣajāśvināśvā śiśumatī bhiṣag dhenuḥ sarasvatī bhiṣag duha indrāya bheṣajam payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtam madhu vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad duro diśaḥ kavaṣyo na vyacasvatīr aśvibhyām na duro diśa indro na rodasī dughe duhe dhenuḥ sarasvaty aśvinendrāya bheṣajaṁ śukram na jyotir indriyam payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtam madhu vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat supeśasoṣe naktam divāśvinā samañjati sarasvatyā tviṣim indre na bheṣajaṁ śyeno na rajasā hṛdā śriyā na māsaram payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtam madhu vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad daivyā hotārā bhiṣajāśvinendram na jāgṛvi divā naktam na bheṣajaiḥ śūṣaṁ sarasvatī bhiṣak sīsena duha indriyam payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtam madhu vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat tisro devīr na bheṣajam trayastridhātavo paso rūpam indre hiraṇyayam aśvineḍā na bhāratī vācā sarasvatī maha indrāya duha indriyam payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtam madhu vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat surerasam ṛṣabham naryāpasam tvaṣṭāram indram aśvinā bheṣajam na sarasvatīm ojo na hūtir indriyam vṛko na rabhaso bhiṣag yaśaḥ surayā bheṣajaṁ śriyā na māsaram payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtam madhu vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad vanaspatiṁ śamitāraṁ śatakratum bhīmam na manyuṁ rājānam vyāghram namasāśvinā bhāmaṁ sarasvatī bhiṣag indrāya duha indriyam payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtam madhu vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad agniṁ svāhājyasya stokānāṁ svāhā medasām pṛthak svāhā chāgam aśvibhyāṁ svāhā meṣaṁ sarasvatyai svāha ṛṣabham indrāya siṁhāya sahasa indriyaṁ svāhāgnim na bheṣajaṁ svāhā somam indriyaṁ svāhendraṁ sutrāmāṇaṁ savitāram varuṇam bhiṣajām patiṁ svāhā vanaspatim priyam pātho na bheṣajaṁ svāhā devā ājyapā juṣāṇo agnir bheṣajam payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtam madhu vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad aśvinau chāgasya vapāyā medaso juṣetāṁ havir hotar yaja \\

Sentence: b hotā yakṣat sarasvatīm meṣasya vapāyā medaso juṣatāṁ havir hotar yaja \\

Sentence: c hotā yakṣad indram ṛṣabhasya vapāyā medaso juṣatāṁ havir hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad aśvinau sarasvatīm indraṁ sutrāmāṇam ime somāḥ surāmāṇaś chāgair na meṣair ṛṣabhaiḥ sutāḥ śaṣpair ba tokmabhir lājair mahasvanto madā māsareṇa pariṣkṛtāḥ śukrāḥ payasvanto mṛtāḥ prasthitā vo madhuścutas tān aśvinā sarasvatīndraḥ sutrāmā vṛtrahā juṣantāṁ somyam madhu pibantu vyantu hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad aśvinau chāgasya haviṣa āttām adya madhyato meda udbhṛtam purā dveṣobhyaḥ purā pauruṣeyyā gṛbho ghastām nūnam ghāseajrāṇām yavasaprathamānāṁ sumatkṣarāṇāṁ śatarudriyāṇām agniṣvāttānām pīvopavasānām pārśvataḥ śroṇitaḥ śitāmata utsādato ṅgād-aṅgād avattānām karata evāśvinā juṣetāṁ havir hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat sarasvatīm meṣasya haviṣa āvayad adya madhyato meda udbhṛtam purā dveṣobhyaḥ purā pauruṣeyyā gṛbho ghasan nūnam ghāseajrāṇām yavasaprathamānāṁ sumatkṣarāṇāṁ śatarudriyāṇām agniṣvāttānām pīvopavasānām pārśvataḥ śroṇitaḥ śitāmata utsādato ṅgād-aṅgād avattānām karad evaṁ sarasvatī juṣatāṁ havir hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad indram ṛṣabhasya haviṣa āvayad adya madhyato meda udbhṛtam purā dveṣobhyaḥ purā pauruṣeyyā gṛbho ghasan nūnam ghāseajrāṇām yavasaprathamānāṁ sumatkṣarāṇāṁ śatarudriyāṇām agniṣvāttānām pīvopavasānām pārśvataḥ śroṇitaḥ śitāmata utsādato ṅgād-aṅgād avattānām karad evam indro juṣatāṁ havir hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad vanaspatim abhi hi piṣṭatamayā rabhiṣṭayā raśanayādhita \\

Sentence: b yatrāśvinoś chāgasya haviṣaḥ priyā dhāmāni yatra sarasvatyā meṣasya haviṣaḥ priyā dhāmāni yatrendrasya ṛṣabhasya haviṣaḥ priyā dhāmāni yatrāgneḥ priyā dhāmāni yatra somasya priyā dhāmāni yatrendrasya sutrāmṇaḥ priyā dhāmāni yatra savituḥ priyā dhāmāni yatra varuṇasya priyā dhāmāni yatra vanaspateḥ priyā pāthāṁsi yatra devānām ājyapānām priyā dhāmāni yatrāgner hotuḥ priyā dhāmāni tatraitān prastutyevopastutyevopāva srakṣad rabhīyasa iva kṛtvī karad evam devo vanaspatir juṣatāṁ havir hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 47

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad agniṁ sviṣṭakṛtam ayāḍ agnir aśvinoś chāgasya haviṣaḥ priyā dhāmāny ayāṭ sarasvatyā meṣasya haviṣaḥ priyā dhāmāny ayāḍ indrasya ṛṣabhasya haviṣaḥ priyā dhāmāny ayāḍ agneḥ priyā dhāmāny ayāṭ somasya priyā dhāmāny ayāḍ indrasya sutrāmṇaḥ priyā dhāmāny ayāṭ savituḥ priyā dhāmāny ayāḍ varuṇasya priyā dhāmāny ayāḍ vanaspateḥ priyā pāthāṁsy ayāḍ devānām ājyapānām priyā dhāmāni yakṣad agner hotuḥ priyā dhāmāni yakṣat svam mahimānam ā yajatām ejyā iṣaḥ kṛṇotu so adhvarā jātavedā juṣatāṁ havir hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 48

Sentence: a devam barhiḥ sarasvatī sudevam indre aśvinā \\

Sentence: b tejo na cakṣur akṣyor barhiṣā dadhur indriyam vasuvane vasudheyasya vyantu yaja \\\\

Verse: 49

Sentence: a devīr dvāro aśvinā bhiṣajendre sarasvatī \\

Sentence: b prāṇam na vīryam nasi dvāro dadhur indriyam vasuvane vasudheyasya vyantu yaja \\\\

Verse: 50

Sentence: a devī uṣāsāv aśvinā sutrāmendre sarasvatī \\

Sentence: b balam na vācam āsya uṣābhyām dadhur indriyam vasuvane vasudheyasya vyantu yaja \\\\

Verse: 51

Sentence: a devī joṣṭrī sarasvaty aśvinendram avardhayan \\

Sentence: b śrotram na karṇayor yaśo joṣṭrībhām dadhur indriyam vasuvane vasudheyasya vyantu yaja \\\\

Verse: 52

Sentence: a devī ūrjāhutī dughe sudughendre sarasvaty aśvinā bhiṣajāvataḥ \\

Sentence: b śukram na jyoti stanayor āhutī dhatta indriyam vasuvane vasudheyasya vyantu yaja \\\\

Verse: 53

Sentence: a devā devānām bhiṣajā hotārāv indram aśvinā \\

Sentence: b vaṣaṭkāraiḥ sarasvatī tviṣim na hṛdaye matiṁ hotṛbhyām dadhur indriyam vasuvane vasudheyasya vyantu yaja \\\\

Verse: 54

Sentence: a devīs tisras tisro devīr aśvineḍā sarasvatī \\

Sentence: b śūṣam na madhye nābhyām indrāya dadhur indriyam vasuvane vasudheyasya vyantu yaja \\\\

Verse: 55

Sentence: a deva indro narāśaṁsas trivarūthaḥ sarasvatyāśvibhyām īyate rathaḥ \\

Sentence: b reto na rūpam amṛtam janitram indrāya tvaṣṭā dadhad indriyāṇi vasuvane vasudheyasya vyantu yaja \\\\

Verse: 56

Sentence: a devo devair vanaspatir hiraṇyaparṇo aśvibhyāṁ sarasvatyā supippala indrāya pacyate madhu \\

Sentence: b ojo na jūtir ṛṣabho na bhāmam vanaspatir no dadhad indriyāṇi vasuvane vasudheyasya vyantu yaja \\\\

Verse: 57

Sentence: a devam barhir vāritīnām adhvare stīrṇam aśvibhyām ūrṇamradāḥ sarasvatyā syonam indra te sadaḥ \\

Sentence: b īśāyai manyuṁ rājānam barhiṣā dadhur indriyam vasuvane vasudheyasya vyantu yaja \\\\

Verse: 58

Sentence: a devo agniḥ sviṣṭakṛd devān yakṣad yathāyathaṁ hotārāv indram aśvinā vācā vācaṁ sarasvatīm agniṁ somaṁ sviṣṭakṛt svṅṣṭa indraḥ sutrāmā savitā varuṇo bhiṣag iṣṭo devovanaspatiḥ svṅṣṭā devā ājyapāḥ svṅṣṭo agnir agninā hotā hotre sviṣṭakṛd yaśo na dadhad indriyam ūrjam apacitiṁ svadhām vasuvane vasudheyasya vyantu yaja \\\\

Verse: 59

Sentence: a agnim adya hotāram avṛṇītāyam yajamānaḥ pacan paktīḥ pacan puroḍāśān badhnann aśvibhyām chāgaṁ sarasvatyai meṣam indrāya ṛṣabhaṁ sunvann aśvibhyāṁ sarasvatyā indrāya sutrāmṇe surāsomān \\\\

Verse: 60

Sentence: a sūpasthā adya devo vanaspatir abhavad aśvibhyām chāgena sarasvatyai meṣeṇendrāya ṛṣabheṇākṣaṁs tān medastaḥ prati pacatāgṛbhīṣatāvīvṛdhanta puroḍāśair apur aśvinā sarasvatīndraḥ sutrāmā surāsomān \\\\

Verse: 61

Sentence: a tvām adya ṛṣa ārṣeya ṛṣīṇām napād avṛṇītāym yajamāno bahubhya ā samgatebhya eṣa me deveṣu vasu vāryāyakṣyata iti tā yā devā deva dānāny adus tāny asmā ā ca śāssvā ca gurasveṣitaś ca hotar asi bhadravācyāya preṣito mānuṣaḥ sūktavākāya sūktā brūhi \\\\

## Paragraph: 22

Verse: 1

Sentence: a tejo si śukram amṛtam āyuṣpā āyur me pāhi \\

Sentence: b devasya tvā savituḥ prasavṝ śvinor bāhubhyām pūṣṇo hastābhyām \\

Sentence: c ā dade \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a imām agṛbhṇan raśanām ṛtasya pūrva āyuṣi vidatheṣu kavyā \\

Sentence: b sā no asmint suta ā babhūva ṛtasya sāmant saramārapantī \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a abhidhā asi bhuvanam asi yantāsi dhartā \\

Sentence: b sa tvam agnim vaiśvānaraṁ saprathasam gaccha svāhākṛtaḥ \\\\

## Paragraph: 23

Verse: 1

Sentence: a hiraṇyagarbhaḥ sam avartatāgre bhūtasya jātaḥ patir eka āsīt \\

Sentence: b sa dādhāra pṛthivīm dyām utemām kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a upayāmagṛhīto si prajāpataye tvā juṣṭam gṛhṇāmi \\

Sentence: b eṣa te yoniḥ sūryas te mahimā \\

Sentence: c yas te hant samvatsare mahimā sambabhūva yas te vāyāv antarikṣe mahimā sambabhūva yas te divi sūrye mahimā sambabhūva tasmai te mahimne prajāpataye svāhā devebhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a yaḥ prāṇato nimiṣato mahitvaika id rājā jagato babhūva \\

Sentence: b ya īśe asya dvipadaś catuṣpadaḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a upayāmagṛhīto si prajāpataye tvā juṣṭam gṛhṇāmi \\

Sentence: b eṣa te yoniś candras te mahimā \\

Sentence: c yas te rātrau samvatsare mahimā sambabhūva yas te pṛthivyām agnau mahimā sambabhūva yas te nakṣatreṣu candramasi mahimā sambabhūva tasmai te mahimne prajāpataye devebhyaḥ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a yuñjanti bradhnam aruṣam carantam pari tasthuṣaḥ \\

Sentence: b rocante rocanā divi \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a yuñjanty asya kāmyā harī vipakṣasā rathe \\

Sentence: b śoṇā dhṛṣṇū nṛsāhasā \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a yad vāto apo aganīgan priyām indrasya tanvam \\

Sentence: b etaṁ stotar anena pathā punar aśvam ā vartayāsi naḥ \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a vasavas tvāñjantu gāyatreṇa chandasā \\

Sentence: b rudrās tvāñjantu traiṣṭubhena chandasā \\

Sentence: c ādityās tvāñjantu jāgatena chandasā \\

Sentence: d bhūr bhuvaḥ svar lājī3ñ chācī3n yavye gavya etad annam atta devā etad annam addhi prajāpate \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a kaḥ svid ekākī carati ka u svij jāyate punaḥ \\

Sentence: b kiṁ svid dhimasya bheṣajam kim v āvapanam mahat \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: b agnir dhimasya bheṣajam bhūmir āvapanam mahat \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a kā svid āsīt pūrvacittiḥ kiṁ svid āsīd bṛhad vayaḥ \\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a dyaur āsīt pūrvacittiḥ aśva āsīd bṛhad vayaḥ \\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a vāyuṣ ṭvā pacatair avatu \\

Sentence: c nyagrodhaś camasaiḥ \\

Sentence: d śalmalir vṛddhyā \\

Sentence: e eṣa sya rāthyo vṛṣā \\

Sentence: f paḍbhiś caturbhir ed agan \\

Sentence: g brahmākṛṣṇaś ca no vatu \\

Sentence: h namo gnaye \\\\

Sentence: b saṁśito apsv apsujā brahmā somapurogavaḥ \\\\

Sentence: a svayam vājiṁs tanvam kalpayasva svayam yajasva svayam juṣasva \\

Verse: 17

Sentence: b vāyuḥ paśur āsīt tenāyajanta sa etam lokam ajayad yasmin vāyuḥ sa te loko bhaviṣyati tam jeṣyasi pibaitā apaḥ \\

Sentence: a utsakthyā ava gudam dhehi sam añjim cārayā vṛṣan \\

Sentence: a yakāsakau śakuntikāhalag iti vañcati \\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a yad devāso lalāmagum pra viṣṭīminam āviṣuḥ \\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a yad dhariṇo yavam atti na puṣṭam paśu manyate \\

Sentence: a dadhikrāvṇo akāriṣam jiṣṇor aśvasya vājinaḥ \\

Verse: 33

Verse: 34

Sentence: b maighīr vidyuto vācaḥ sūcībhiḥ śamyantu tvā \\\\

Verse: 36

Verse: 38

Verse: 42

Verse: 43

Verse: 44

Sentence: a śam te parebhyo gātrebhyaḥ śam astv avarebhyaḥ \\

Sentence: b śam asthabhyo majjabhyaḥ śam v astu tanvai tava \\\\

Sentence: a kaḥ svid ekākī carati ka u svij jāyate punaḥ \\

Sentence: b kiṁ svid dhimasya bheṣajam kim v āvapanam mahat \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a sūrya ekākī carati candramā jāyate punaḥ \\

Sentence: b agnir dhimasya bheṣajam bhūmir āvapanam mahat \\\\

Sentence: a kiṁ svit sūryasamam jyotiḥ kiṁ samudrasamaṁ saraḥ \\

Sentence: b kiṁ svit pṛthivyai varṣīyaḥ kasya mātrā na vidyate \\\\

Verse: 48

Sentence: a brahma sūryasamam jyotir dyauḥ samudrasamaṁ saraḥ \\

Sentence: b indraḥ pṛthivyai varṣīyān gos tu mātrā na vidyate \\\\

Verse: 49

Sentence: a pṛcchāmi tvā citaye devasakha yadi tvam atra manasā jagantha \\

Sentence: b yeṣu viṣṇus triṣu padeṣv eṣṭas teṣu viśvam bhuvanam ā viveśā3ṁ \\\\

Verse: 50

Sentence: a api teṣu triṣu padeṣv asmi yeṣu viśvam bhuvanam āviveśa \\

Sentence: b sadyaḥ pary emi pṛthivīm uta dyām ekenāṅgena divo asya pṛṣṭham \\\\

Verse: 51

Sentence: a keṣv antaḥ puruṣa ā viveśa kāny antaḥ puruṣe arpitāni \\

Sentence: b etad brahmann upavalhāmasi tvā kiṁ svin naḥ prati vocāsy atra \\\\

Verse: 52

Sentence: a pañcasv antaḥ puruṣa ā viveśa tāny antaḥ puruṣe arpitāni \\

Sentence: b etat tvātra pratimanvāno asmi na māyayā bhavasy uttaro mat \\\\

Verse: 53

Sentence: a kā svid āsīt pūrvacittiḥ kiṁ svid āsīd bṛhad vayaḥ \\

Sentence: b kā svid āsīt pilippilā kā svid āsīt piśaṅgilā \\\\

Verse: 54

Sentence: a dyaur āsīt pūrvacittiḥ aśva āsīd bṛhad vayaḥ \\

Sentence: b avir āsīt pilippilā rātrir āsīt piśaṅgilā \\\\

Verse: 55

Sentence: a ka īm are piśamgilā ka īm kurupiśamgilā \\

Sentence: b ka īm āskandam arṣati ka īm panthām vi sarpati \\\\

Verse: 56

Sentence: a ajāre piśamgilā śvāvit kurupiśamgilā \\

Sentence: b śaśa āskandam arṣaty ahiḥ panthām vi sarpati \\\\

Verse: 57

Sentence: a katy asya viṣṭhāḥ katy akṣarāṇi kati homāsaḥ katidhā samiddhaḥ \\

Sentence: b yajñasya tvā vidathā pṛccham atra kati hotāra ṛtuśo yajanti \\\\

Verse: 58

Sentence: a ṣaḍ asya viṣṭhāḥ śatam akṣarāṇy aśītir homāḥ samidho ha tisraḥ \\

Sentence: b yajñasya te vidathā pra bravīmi sapta hotāra ṛtuśo yajanti \\\\

Verse: 59

Sentence: a ko asya veda bhuvanasya nābhim ko dyāvāpṛthivī antarikṣam \\

Sentence: b kaḥ sūryasya veda bṛhato janitram ko veda candramasam yatojāḥ \\\\

Verse: 60

Sentence: a vedāham asya bhuvanasya nābhim veda dyāvāpṛthivī antarikṣam \\

Sentence: b veda sūryasya bṛhato janitram atho veda candramasam yatojāḥ \\\\

Verse: 61

Sentence: a pṛcchāmi tvā param antam pṛthivyāḥ pṛcchāmi yatra bhuvanasya nābhiḥ \\

Sentence: b pṛcchāmi tvā vṛṣṇo aśvasya retaḥ pṛcchāmi vācaḥ paramam vyoma \\\\

Verse: 62

Sentence: a iyam vediḥ paro antaḥ pṛthivyā ayam yajño yatra bhuvanasya nābhiḥ \\

Sentence: b ayaṁ somo vṛṣṇo aśvasya retaḥ brahmāyam vācaḥ paramam vyoma \\\\

Verse: 63

Sentence: a subhūḥ svayambhūḥ prathamo ntar mahaty arṇave \\

Sentence: b dadhe ha garbham ṛtviyam yato jātaḥ prajāpatiḥ \\\\

Verse: 64

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat prajāpatiṁ somasya mahimnaḥ \\

Sentence: b juṣatām pibatu somaṁ hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 65

Sentence: a prajāpate na tvad etāny anyo viśvā rūpāṇi pari tā babhūva \\

Sentence: b yatkāmās te juhumas tan no astu \\\\

## Paragraph: 24

Verse: 1

Sentence: a aśvas tūparo gomṛgas te prājāpatyāḥ kṛṣṇagrīva āgneyo rarāṭe purastāt sārasvatī meṣy adhastād dhanvor āśvināv adhorāmau bāhvoḥ saumapauṣṇaḥ śyāmo nābhyāṁ sauryayāmau śvetaś ca kṛṣṇaś ca pārśvayos tvāṣṭrau lomaśasakthau sakthyor vāyavyaḥ śvetaḥ puccha indrāya svapasyāya vehad vaiṣṇavo vāmanaḥ \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a rohito dhūmrarohitaḥ karkandhurohitas te saumyā babhrur aruṇababhruḥ śukababhrus te vāruṇāḥ śitirandhro nyataḥśitirandhraḥ samantaśitirandhras te sāvitrāḥ śitibāhur anyataḥśitibāhuḥ samantaśitibāhus te bārhaspatyāḥ pṛṣatī kṣudrapṛṣatī sthūlapṛṣatī tā maitrāvaruṇyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a śuddhavālaḥ sarvaśuddhavālo maṇivālas ta āśvināḥ śyetaḥ śyetākṣo ruṇas te rudrāya paśupataye karṇā yāmā avaliptā raudrā nabhorūpāḥ pārjanyāḥ \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a pṛśnis tiraścīnapṛśnir ūrdhvapṛśnis te mārutāḥ phalgūr lohitorṇī palakṣī tāḥ sārasvatyaḥ plīhākarṇaḥ śuṇṭhākarṇo ddhyālohakarṇas te tvāṣṭrāḥ kṛṣṇagrīvaḥ śitikakṣo ñjisakthas ta aindrāgnāḥ kṛṣṇāñjir alpāñjir mahāñjis ta uṣasyāḥ \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a śilpā vaiśvadevyo rohiṇyas tryavayo vāce vijñātā adityai sarūpā dhātre vatsataryo devānām patnībhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a kṛṣṇagrīvā āgneyāḥ śitibhravo vasūnāṁ rohitā rudrāṇāṁ śvetā avarokiṇa ādityānām nabhorūpāḥ pārjanyāḥ \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a unnata ṛṣabho vāmanas ta aindravaiṣṇavā unnataḥ śitibāhuḥ śitipṛṣṭhas ta aindrābārhaspatyāḥ śukarūpā vājināḥ kalmāṣā āgnimārutāḥ śyāmāḥ pauṣnāḥ \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a etā aindrāgnā dvirūpā agnīṣomīyā vāmanā anaḍvāha āgnāvaiṣṇavā vaśā maitrāvaruṇyo nyataenyo maitryaḥ \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a kṛṣṇagrīvā āgneyā babhravaḥ saumyāḥ śvetā vāyavyā avijñātā adityai sarūpā dhātre vatsataryo devānām patnībhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a kṛṣṇā bhaumā dhūmrā āntarikṣā bṛhanto divyāḥ śabalā vaidyutāḥ sidhmās tārakāḥ \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a dhūmrān vasantāyā labhate śvetān grīṣmāya kṛṣṇān varṣābhyo ruṇāñ charade pṛṣato hemantāya piśaṅgāñ chiśirāya \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a tryavayo gāyatryai pañcāvayas triṣṭubhe dityavāho jagatyai trivatsā anuṣṭubhe turyavāha uṣṇihe \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a paṣthavāho virāja ukṣaṇo bṛhatyā ṛṣabhāḥ kakubhe naḍvāhaḥ paṅktyai dhenavo ticchandase \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a kṛṣṇagrīvā āgneyā babhravaḥ saumyā upadhvastāḥ sāvitrā vatsataryaḥ sārasvatyaḥ śyāmāḥ pauṣṇāḥ pṛśnayo mārutā bahurūpā vaiśvadevā vaśā dyāvāpṛthivīyāḥ \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a uktāḥ samcarā etā aindrāgnāḥ kṛṣṇāḥ vāruṇāḥ pṛśnayo mārutāḥ kāyās tūparāḥ \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a agnaye nīkavate prathamajān ā labhate marudbhyaḥ sāmtapanebhyaḥ savātyān marudbhyo gṛhamedhibhyo baṣkihān marudbhyaḥ krīḍibhyaḥ saṁsṛṣṭān marudbhyaḥ svatavadbhyo nusṛṣṭān \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a uktāḥ samcarā etā aindrāgnāḥ prāśṛṅgā māhendrā bahurūpā vaiśvakarmaṇāḥ \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a dhūmrā babhrunīkāśāḥ pitṝṇāṁ somavatām babhravo babhrunīkāśāḥ pitṝṇām barhiṣadām kṛṣṇā babhrunīkāśāḥ pitṝṇām agniṣvāttānām kṛṣṇāḥ pṛṣantas traiyambakāḥ \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a uktāḥ samcarā etā śunāsīrīyāḥ śvetā vāyavyāḥ śvetāḥ sauryāḥ \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a vasantāya kapiñjalān ā labhate grīṣmāya kalaviṅgān varṣābhyas tittirīñ charade vartikā hemantāya kakarāñ chiśirāya vikakarān \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a samudrāya śiśumārān a labhate parjanyāya maṇḍūkān adbhyo matsyān mitrāya kulīpayān varuṇāya nākrān \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a somāya haṁsān ā labhate vāyave balākā indrāgnibhyām kruñcān mitrāya madgūn varuṇāya cakravākān \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a agnaye kuṭarūn ā labhate vanaspatibhya ulūkān agnīṣomābhyām cāṣān aśvibhyām mayūrān mitrāvaruṇābhyām kapotān \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a somāya labān ā labhate tvaṣṭre kaulīkān goṣādīr devānām patnībhyaḥ kulīkā devajāmibhyo gnaye gṛhapataye pāruṣṇān \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a ahne pārāvatān ā labhate rātryai sīcāpūr ahorātrayoḥ samdhibhyo jatūr māsebhyo dātyauhānt samvatsarāya mahataḥ suparṇān \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a bhūmyā ākhūn ā labhate ntarikṣāya pāṅktrān dive kaśān digbhyo nakulān babhrukān avāntaradiśābhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a vasubhya ṛśyān ā labhate rudrebhyaḥ rurūn ādityebhyo nyaṅkūn viśvebhyo devebhyaḥ pṛṣatānt sādhyebhyaḥ kuluṅgān \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a īśānāya parasvata ā labhate mitrāya gaurān varuṇāya mahiṣān bṛhaspataye gavayāṁs tvaṣṭra uṣṭrān \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a prajāpataye puruṣān hastina ā labhate vāce pluṣīṁś cakṣuṣe maśakāñ chrotrāya bhṛṅgāḥ \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a prajāpataye ca vāyave ca gomṛgo varuṇāyāraṇyo meṣo yamāya kṛṣṇo manuṣyarājāya markaṭaḥ śārdūlāya rohid ṛṣabhāya gavayī kṣipraśyenāya vartikā nīlamgoḥ kṛmiḥ samudrāya śiśumāro himavate hastī \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a mayuḥ prājāpatya ulo halikṣṇo vṛṣadaṁśas te dhātre diśām kaṅko dhuṅkṣāgneyī kalaviṅko lohitāhiḥ puṣkarasādas te tvāṣṭrā vāce kruñcaḥ \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a somāya kuluṅga āraṇyo jo nakulaḥ śakā te pauṣṇāḥ kroṣṭā māyor indrasya gauramṛgaḥ pidvo nyaṅkuḥ kakkaṭas te numatyai pratiśrutkāyai cakravākaḥ \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a saurī balākā śārgaḥ sṛjayaḥ śayāṇḍakas te maitrāḥ sarasvatyai śāriḥ puruṣavāk śvāvid bhaumī śārdūlo vṛkaḥ pṛdākus te manyave sarasvate śukaḥ puruṣavāk \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a suparṇaḥ pārjanya ātir vāhaso darvidā te vāyave bṛhaspataye vācas pataye paiṅgarājo laja āntarikṣaḥ plavo madgur matsyas te nadīpataye dyāvāpṛthivīyaḥ kūrmaḥ \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a puruṣamṛgaś candramaso godhā kālakā dārvāghāṭas te vanaspatīnām kṛkavākuḥ sāvitro haṁso vātasya nākro makaraḥ kulīpayas te kūpārasya hriyai śalpakaḥ \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a eṇy ahno maṇḍūko mūṣikā tittiris te sarpāṇām lopāśa āśvinaḥ kṛṣṇo rātryā ṛkṣo jatūḥ suṣilīkā ta itarajanānām jahakā vaiṣṇavī \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a anyavāpo rdhamāsānām ṛśyo mayūraḥ suparṇas te gandharvāṇām apām udro māsām kaśyapo rohit kuṇḍṛṇācī golattikā tṝ psarasām mṛtyave sitaḥ \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a varṣāhūr ṛtūnām ākhuḥ kaśo mānthālas te pitṝṇām balāyājagaro vasūnām kapiñjalaḥ kapota ulūkaḥ śaśas te nirṛtyai varuṇāyāraṇyo meṣaḥ \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a śvitra ādityānām uṣṭro ghṛṇīvān vārdhrīṇasas te matyā araṇyāya sṛmaro rurū raudraḥ kvayiḥ kuṭarur dātyauhas te vājinām kāmāya pikaḥ \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a khaṅgo vaiśvadevaḥ śvā kṛṣṇaḥ karṇo gardabhas tarakṣus te rakṣasām indrāya sūkaraḥ siṁho mārutāḥ kṛkalāsaḥ pippakā śakunis te śaravyāyai viśveṣām devānām pṛṣataḥ \\\\

## Paragraph: 25

Verse: 1

Sentence: a śādam dadbhir avakām dantamūlair mṛdam barsvais tegān daṁṣṭrābhyāṁ sarasvatyā agrajihvam jihvāyā utsādam avakrandena tālu vājaṁ hanubhyām apa āsyṝna vṛṣaṇam āṇḍābhyām ādityāṁ śmaśrubhiḥ panthānam bhrūbhyām dyāvāpṛthivī vartobhyām vidyutam kanīnakābhyāṁ śuklāya svāhā kṛṣṇāya svāhā pāryāṇi pakṣmāṇy avāryā ikṣavo vāryāṇi pakṣmāṇi pāryā ikṣavaḥ \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a vātam prāṇenāpānena nasike upayāmam adhareṇauṣṭhena sad uttareṇa prakāśenāntaram anūkāśena bāhvyam niveṣyam mūrdhnā stanayitnum nirbādhenāśanim mastiṣkeṇa vidyutam kanīnakābhyām karṇābhyāṁ śrotraṁ śrotrābhyām karṇau tedanīm adharakaṇṭhenāpaḥ śuṣkakaṇṭhena cittam manyābhir aditiṁ śīrṣṇā nirṛtim nirjarjalpena śīrṣṇā samkrośaiḥ prāṇān reṣmāṇaṁ stupena \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a maśakān keśair indraṁ svapasā vahena bṛhaspatiṁ śakunisādena kūrmāñ chapair ākramanaṁ sthūrābhyām ṛkṣalābhiḥ kapiñjalān javam jaṅghābhyām adhvānam bāhubhyām jāmbīlenāraṇyam agnim atirugbhyām pūṣaṇam dorbhyām aśvināv aṁsābhyāṁ rudraṁ rorābhyām \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a agneḥ pakṣatir vāyor nipakṣatir indrasya tṛtīyā somasya caturthy adityai pañcamīndrāṇyai ṣaṣṭhī marutāṁ saptamī bṛhaspater aṣṭamy aryamṇo navamī dhātur daśamīndrasyaikādaśī varuṇasya dvādaśī yamasya trayodaśī \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a indrāgnyoḥ pakṣatir sarasvatyai nipakṣatir mitrasya tṛtīyāpām caturthī nirṛtyai pañcamy agnīṣomayoḥ ṣaṣṭhī sarpāṇāṁ saptamī viṣṇor aṣṭamī pūṣṇo navamī tvaṣṭur daśamīndrasyaikādaśī varuṇasya dvādaśī yamyai trayodaśī dyāvāpṛthivyor dakṣiṇam pārśvam viśveṣām devānām uttaram \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a marutāṁ skandhā viśveṣām devānām prathamā kīkasā rudrāṇām dvitīyādityānām tṛtīyā vāyoḥ puccham agnīṣomayor bhāsadau kruñcau śroṇibhyām indrābṛhaspatī ūrubhyām mitrāvaruṇāv algābhyām ākramaṇaṁ sthūrābhyām balam kuṣṭhābhyām \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a pūṣaṇam vaniṣṭhunāndhāhīnt sthūlagudayā sarpān gudābhir vihruta āntrair apo vastinā vṛṣaṇam āṇḍābhyām vājinaṁ śepena prajāṁ retasā cāṣān pittena pradarān pāyunā kūśmāñ chakapiṇḍaiḥ \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a indrasya krīḍo dityai pājasyam diśām jatravo dityai bhasaj jīmūtān hṛdayaupaśenāntarikṣam purītatā nabha udaryṝṇa cakravākau matasnābhyām divam vṛkkābhyām girīn plāśibhir upalān plīhnā valmīkān klomabhir glaubhir gulmān hirābhiḥ sravantīr hradān kukṣibhyāṁ samudram udareṇa vaiśvānaram bhasmanā \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a vidhṛtim nābhyā dhṛtaṁ rasenāpo yūṣṇā marīcīr vipruḍbhir nīhāram ūṣmaṇā śīnam vasayā pruṣvā aśrubhir hrādunīr dūṣīkābhir asnā rakṣāṁsi citrāṇy aṅgair nakṣatrāṇi rūpeṇa pṛthivīm tvacā jumbakāya svāhā \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a hiraṇyagarbhaḥ sam avartatāgre bhūtasya jātaḥ patir eka āsīt \\

Sentence: b sa dādhāra pṛthivīm dyām utemām kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a yaḥ prāṇato nimiṣato mahitvaika id rājā jagato babhūva \\

Sentence: b ya īśe asya dvipadaś catuṣpadaḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a yasyeme himavanto mahitvā yasya samudraṁ rasayā sahāhuḥ \\

Sentence: b yasyemāḥ pradiśo yasya bāhū kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a ya ātmadā baladā yasya viśva upāsate praśiṣam yasya devāḥ \\

Sentence: b yasya chāyāmṛtam yasya mṛtyuḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a ā no bhadrāḥ kratavo yantu viśvato dabdhāso aparītāsa udbhidaḥ \\

Sentence: b devā no yathā sadam id vṛdhe asann aprāyuvo rakṣitāro dive-dive \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a devānām bhadrā sumatir ṛjūyatām devānāṁ rātir abhi no ni vartatām \\

Sentence: b devānāṁ sakhyam upa sedimā vayam devā na āyuḥ pra tirantu jīvase \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a tān pūrvayā nividā hūmahe vayam bhagam mitram aditim dakṣam asridham \\

Sentence: b aryamaṇam varuṇaṁ somam aśvinā sarasvatī naḥ subhagā mayas karat \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a tan no vāto mayobhu vātu bheṣajam tan mātā pṛthivī tat pitā dyauḥ \\

Sentence: b tad grāvāṇaḥ somasuto mayobhuvas tad aśvinā śṛṇutam dhiṣṇyā yuvam \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a tam īśānam jagatas tasthuṣas patim dhiyamjinvam avase hūmahe vayam \\

Sentence: b pūṣā no yathā vedasām asad vṛdhe rakṣitā pāyur adabdhaḥ svastaye \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a svasti na indro vṛddhaśravāḥ svasti naḥ pūṣā viśvavedāḥ \\

Sentence: b svasti nas tārkṣyo ariṣṭanemiḥ svasti no bṛhaspatir dadhātu \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a pṛṣadaśvā marutaḥ pṛśnimātaraḥ śubhamyāvāno vidatheṣu jagmayaḥ \\

Sentence: b agnijihvā manavaḥ sūracakṣaso viśve no devā avasā gamann iha \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a bhadram karṇebhiḥ śṛṇuyāma devā bhadram paśyemākṣabhir yajatrāḥ \\

Sentence: b sthirair aṅgais tuṣṭuvāṁsas tanūbhir vy aśemahi devahitam yad āyuḥ \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a śatam in nu śarado anti devā yatrā naś cakrā jarasam tanūnām \\

Sentence: b putrāso yatra pitaro bhavanti mā no madhyā rīriṣatāyur gantoḥ \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a aditir dyaur aditir antarikṣam aditir mātā sa pitā sa putraḥ \\

Sentence: b viśve devā aditiḥ pañca janā aditir jātam aditir janitvam \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a mā no mitro varuṇo aryamāyur indra ṛbhukṣā marutaḥ pari khyan \\

Sentence: b yad vājino devajātasya sapteḥ pravakṣyāmo vidathe vīryāṇi \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a yan nirṇijā rekṇasā prāvṛtasya rātim gṛbhītām mukhato nayanti \\

Sentence: b suprāṅ ajo memyad viśvarūpa indrāpūṣṇoḥ priyam apy eti pāthaḥ \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a eṣa chāgaḥ puro aśvena vājinā pūṣṇo bhāgo nīyate viśvadevyaḥ \\

Sentence: b abhipriyam yat puroḍāśam arvatā tvaṣṭed enaṁ sauśravasāya jinvati \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a yad dhaviṣyam ṛtuśo devayānam trir mānuṣāḥ pary aśvam nayanti \\

Sentence: b atrā pūṣṇaḥ prathamo bhāga eti yajñam devebhyaḥ prativedayann ajaḥ \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a hotādhvaryur āvayā agnimindho grāvagrābha uta śaṁstā suvipraḥ \\

Sentence: b tena yajñena svaramkṛtena svṅṣṭena vakṣaṇā ā pṛṇadhvam \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a yūpavraskā uta ye yūpavāhāś caṣālam ye aśvayūpāya takṣati \\

Sentence: b ye cārvate pacanaṁ sambharanty uto teṣām abhigūrtir na invatu \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a upa prāgāt suman me dhāyi manma devānām āśā upa vītapṛṣṭhaḥ \\

Sentence: b anv enam viprā ṛṣayo madanti devānām puṣṭe cakṛmā subandhum \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a yad vājino dāma samdānam arvato yā śīrṣaṇyā raśanā rajjur asya \\

Sentence: b yad vā ghāsya prabhṛtam āsyṝ tṛṇaṁ sarvā tā te api deveṣv astu \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a yad aśvasya kraviṣo makṣikāśa yad vā svarau svadhitau riptam asti \\

Sentence: b yad dhastayoḥ śamitur yan nakheṣu sarvā tā te api deveṣv astu \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a yad ūvadhyam udarasyāpavāti ya āmasya kraviṣo gandho asti \\

Sentence: b sukṛtā tac chamitāraḥ kṛṇvantūta medhaṁ śṛtapākam pacantu \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a yat te gātrād agninā pacyamānād abhi śūlam nihatasyāvadhāvati \\

Sentence: b mā tad bhūmyām ā śriṣan mā tṛṇeṣu devebhyas tad uśadbhyo rātam astu \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a ye vājinam paripaśyanti pakvam ya īm āhuḥ surabhir nir hareti \\

Sentence: b ye cārvato māṁsabhikṣām upāsata uto teṣām abhigūrtir na invatu \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a yan nīkṣaṇam māṁspacanyā ukhāyā yā pātrāṇi yūṣṇa āsecanāni \\

Sentence: b ūṣmaṇyāpidhānā carūṇām aṅkāḥ sūnāḥ pari bhūṣanty aśvam \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a mā tvāgnir dhvanayīd dhūmagandhir mokhā bhrājanty abhi vikta jaghriḥ \\

Sentence: b iṣṭam vītam abhigūrtam vaṣaṭkṛtam tam devāsaḥ prati gṛbhṇanty aśvam \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a nikramaṇam niṣadanam vivartanam yac ca paḍvīśam arvataḥ \\

Sentence: b yac ca papau yac ca ghāsim jaghāsa sarvā tā te api deveṣv astu \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a yad aśvāya vāsa upastṛṇanty adhīvāsam yā hiraṇyāny asmai \\

Sentence: b samdānam arvantam paḍvīśam priyā deveṣv ā yāmayanti \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a yat te sāde mahasā śūkṛtasya pārṣṇyā vā kaśayā vā tutoda \\

Sentence: b sruceva tā haviṣo adhvareṣu sarvā tā te brahmaṇā sūdayāmi \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a catustriṁśad vājino devabandhor vaṅkrīr aśvasya svadhitiḥ sam eti \\

Sentence: b acchidrā gātrā vayunā kṛṇota paruṣ-parur anughuṣyā vi śasta \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a ekas tvaṣṭur aśvasyā viśastā dvā yantārā bhavatas tatha ṛtuḥ \\

Sentence: b yā te gātrāṇām ṛtuthā kṛṇomi tā-tā piṇḍānām pra juhomy agnau \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a mā tvā tapat priya ātmāpiyantam mā svadhitis tanva ā tiṣṭhipat te \\

Sentence: b mā te gṛdhnur aviśastātihāya chidrā gātrāṇy asinā mithū kaḥ \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a na vā u etan mriyase na riṣyasi devāṁ id eṣi pathibhiḥ sugebhiḥ \\

Sentence: b harī te yuñjā pṛṣatī abhūtām upāsthād vājī dhuri rāsabhasya \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a sugavyam no vājī svaśvyam puṁsaḥ putrāṁ uta viśvāpuṣaṁ rayim \\

Sentence: b anāgāstvam no aditiḥ kṛṇotu kṣatram no aśvo vanatāṁ haviṣmān \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a imā nu kam bhuvanā sīṣadhāmendraś ca viśve ca devāḥ \\

Sentence: b ādityair idnraḥ sagaṇo marudbhir asmabhyam bheṣajā karat \\

Sentence: c yajñam ca nas tanvam ca prajām cādityair indraḥ saha sīṣadhāti \\\\

Verse: 47

Sentence: a agne tvam no antama uta trātā śivo bhava varūthyaḥ \\

Sentence: b vasur agnir vasuśravā acchā nakṣi dyumattamaṁ rayim dāḥ \\

Sentence: c tam tvā śociṣṭha dīdivaḥ sumnāya nūnam īmahe sakhibhyaḥ \\\\

## Paragraph: 26

Verse: 1

Sentence: a agniś ca pṛthivī ca samnate te me sam namatām adaḥ \\

Sentence: b vāyuś cāntarikṣam ca samnate te me sam namatām adaḥ \\

Sentence: c ādityaś ca dyauś ca samnate te me sam namatām adaḥ \\

Sentence: d āpaś ca varuṇaś ca samnate te me sam namatām adaḥ \\

Sentence: e sapta saṁsado aṣṭamī bhūtasādhanī \\

Sentence: f sakāmāṁ adhvanas kuru samjñānam astu me munā \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a yathemām vācam kalyāṇīm āvadāni janebhyaḥ \\

Sentence: b brahmarājanyābhyāṁ śūdrāya cāryāya ca svāya cāraṇāya \\

Sentence: c priyo devānām dakṣiṇāyai dātur iha bhūyāsam ayam me kāmaḥ sam ṛdhyatām upa mādo namatu \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a bṛhaspate ati yad aryo arhād dyumad vibhāti kratumaj janeṣu \\

Sentence: b yad dīdayac chavasa ṛtaprajāta tad asmāsu draviṇam dhehi citram \\

Sentence: c upayāmagṛhīto si bṛhaspataye tvā \\

Sentence: d eṣa te yonir bṛhaspataye tvā \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a indra gomann ihā yāhi pibā somaṁ śatakrato \\

Sentence: b vidyadbhir grāvabhiḥ sutam \\

Sentence: c upayāmagṛhīto sīndrāya tvā gomate \\

Sentence: d eṣa te yonir indrāya tvā gomate \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a indrā yāhi vṛtrahan pibā somaṁ śatakrato \\

Sentence: b gomadbhir grāvabhiḥ sutam \\

Sentence: c upayāmagṛhīto sīndrāya tvā gomate \\

Sentence: d eṣa te yonir indrāya tvā gomate \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a ṛtāvānam vaiśvānaram ṛtasya jyotiṣas patim \\

Sentence: b ajasram gharmam īmahe \\

Sentence: c upayāmagṛhīto si vaiśvānarāya tvā \\

Sentence: d eṣa te yonir vaiśvānarāya tvā \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a vaiśvānarasya sumatau syāma rājā hi kam bhuvanānām abhiśrīḥ \\

Sentence: b ito jāto viśvam idam vi caṣṭe vaiśvānaro yatate sūryeṇa \\

Sentence: c upayāmagṛhīto si vaiśvānarāya tvā \\

Sentence: d eṣa te yonir vaiśvānarāya tvā \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a vaiśvānaro na ūtaya ā pra yātu parāvataḥ \\

Sentence: b agnir ukthena vāhasā \\

Sentence: c upayāmagṛhīto si vaiśvānarāya tvā \\

Sentence: d eṣa te yonir vaiśvānarāya tvā \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a agnir ṛṣiḥ pavamānaḥ pāñcajanyaḥ purohitaḥ \\

Sentence: b tam īmahe mahāgayam \\

Sentence: c upayāmagṛhīto sy agnaye tvā varcase \\

Sentence: d eṣa te yonir agnaye tvā varcase \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a mahāṁ indro vajrahastaḥ ṣoḍaśī śarma yacchatu \\

Sentence: b hantu pāpmānam yo smān dveṣṭi \\

Sentence: c upayāmagṛhīto si mahendrāya tvā \\

Sentence: d eṣa te yonir mahendrāya tvā \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a tam vo dasmam ṛtīṣaham vasor mandānam andhasaḥ \\

Sentence: b abhi vatsam na svasareṣu dhenava indram gīrbhir navāmahe \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a yad vāhiṣṭham tad agnaye bṛhad arca vibhāvaso \\

Sentence: b mahiṣīva tvad rayis tvad vājā ud īrate \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a ehy ū ṣu bravāṇi te gna itthetarā giraḥ \\

Sentence: b ebhir vardhāsa indubhiḥ \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a ṛtavas te yajñam vi tanvantu māsā rakṣantu te haviḥ \\

Sentence: b samvatsaras te yajñam dadhātu naḥ prajām ca pari pātu naḥ \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a upahvare girīṇāṁ samgame ca nadīnām \\

Sentence: b dhiyā vipro ajāyata \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a uccā te jātam andhaso divi sad bhūmy ā dade \\

Sentence: b ugraṁ śarma mahi śravaḥ \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a sa na indrāya yajyave varuṇāya marudbhyaḥ \\

Sentence: b varivovit pari srava \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a enā viśvāny arya ā dyumnāni mānuṣāṇām \\

Sentence: b siṣāsanto vanāmahe \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a anu vīrair anu puṣyāsma gobhir anv aśvair anu sarveṇa puṣṭaiḥ \\

Sentence: b anu dvipadānu catuṣpadā vayam devā no yajñam ṛtuthā nayantu \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a agne patnīr ihā vaha devānām uśatīr upa \\

Sentence: b tvaṣṭāraṁ somapītaye \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a abhi yajñam gṛṇīhi no gnāvo neṣṭaḥ piba ṛtunā \\

Sentence: b tvaṁ hi ratnadhā asi \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a draviṇodāḥ pipīṣati juhota pra ca tiṣṭhata \\

Sentence: b neṣṭrād ṛtubhir iṣyata \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a tavāyaṁ somas tvam ehy arvāṅ chaśvattamaṁ sumanā asya pāhi \\

Sentence: b asmin yajñe barhiṣy ā niṣadyā dadhiṣvemam jaṭhara indum indra \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a ameva naḥ suhavā ā hi gantana ni barhiṣi sadatanā raṇiṣṭana \\

Sentence: b athā mandasva jujuṣāṇo andhasas tvaṣṭar devebhir janibhiḥ sumadgaṇaḥ \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayā pavasva soma dhārayā \\

Sentence: b indrāya pātave sutaḥ \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a rakṣohā viśvacarṣaṇir abhi yonim ayohatam \\

Sentence: b druṇā sadhastham āsadat \\\\

## Paragraph: 27

Verse: 1

Sentence: a sās tvāgna ṛtavo vardhayantu samvatsarā ṛṣayo yāni satyā \\

Sentence: b sam divyena dīdihi rocanena viśvā ā bhāhi pradiśaś catasraḥ \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a sam cedhyasvāgne pra ca bodhayainam uc ca tiṣṭha mahate saubhagāya \\

Sentence: b mā ca riṣad upasattā te agne brahmāṇas te yaśasaḥ santu mānye \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a tvām agne vṛṇate brāhmaṇā ime śivo agne samvaraṇe bhavā naḥ \\

Sentence: b sapatnahā no abhimātijic ca sve gaye gāgṛhy aprayucchan \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a ihaivāgne adhi dhārayā rayim mā tvā ni kran pūrvacito nikāriṇaḥ \\

Sentence: b kṣatram agne suyamam astu tubhyam upasattā vardhatām te aniṣṭṛtaḥ \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a kṣatreṇāgne svāyuḥ saṁ rabhasva mitreṇāgne mitradheye yatasva \\

Sentence: b sajātānām madhyamasthā edhi rājñām agne vihavyo dīdihīha \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a ati niho ati sridho ty acittim aty arātim agne \\

Sentence: b viśvā hy agne duritā sahasvāthāsmabhyaṁ sahavīrāṁ rayim dāḥ \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a anādhṛṣyo jātavedā anādhṛṣṭo virāḍ agne kṣatrabhṛd dīdihīha \\

Sentence: b viśvā āśāḥ pramuñcan mānuṣīr bhayaḥ śivebhir adya pari pāhi no vṛdhe \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a bṛhaspate savitar bodhayainaṁ saṁśitam cit samtarāṁ saṁ śiśādhi \\

Sentence: b vardhayainam mahate saubhagāya viśva enam anu madantu devāḥ \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a amutrabhūyādadha yad yamasya bṛhaspate abhisaster amuñcaḥ \\

Sentence: b praty auhatām aśvinā mṛtyum asmād devānām agne bhiṣajā śacībhiḥ \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a ud vayam tamasas pari svaḥ paśyanta uttaram \\

Sentence: b devam devatrā sūryam aganma jyotir uttamam \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a ūrdhvā asya samidho bhavanty ūrdhvā śukrā śocīṁṣy agneḥ \\

Sentence: b dyumattamā supratīkasya sūnoḥ \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a tanūnapād asuro viśvavedā devo deveṣu devaḥ \\

Sentence: b patho anaktu madhvā ghṛtena \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a madhvā yajñam nakṣase prīṇāno narāśaṁso agne \\

Sentence: b sukṛd devaḥ savitā viśvavāraḥ \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a acchāyam eti śavasā ghṛteneḍāno vahnir namasā \\

Sentence: b agniṁ sruco adhvareṣu prayatsu \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a sa yakṣad asya mahimānam agneḥ sa īm mandrā suprayasaḥ \\

Sentence: b vasuś vetiṣṭho vasudhātamaś ca \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a dvāro devīr anv asya viśve vratā dadante agneḥ \\

Sentence: b uruvyacaso dhāmnā patyamānāḥ \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a te asya yoṣaṇe divye na yonā uṣāsānaktā \\

Sentence: b imam yajñam avatām adhvaram naḥ \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a daivyā hotārā ūrdhvam adhvaram no gner juhvām abhi gṛṇītam \\

Sentence: b kṛṇutam naḥ svṅṣṭam \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a tisro devīr barhir idaṁ sadantv iḍā sarasvatī bhāratī \\

Sentence: b mahī gṛṇānā \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a tan nas turīpam adbhutam purukṣu tvaṣṭā suvīryam \\

Sentence: b rāyaspoṣam vi ṣyatu nābhim asme \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a vanaspate va sṛjā rarāṇas tmanā deveṣu \\

Sentence: b agnir havyaṁ śamitā sūdayāti \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a agne svāhā kṛṇuhi jātavedo indrāya havyam \\

Sentence: b viśve devā havir idam juṣantām \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a pīvoannā rayimvṛdhaḥ sumedhāḥ śvetaḥ siṣakti niyutām abhiśrīḥ \\

Sentence: b te vāyave samanaso vi tasthur viśven naraḥ svapatyāni cakruḥ \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a rāye nu yam jajñatū rodasīme rāye devī dhiṣaṇā dhāti devam \\

Sentence: b adha vāyum niyutaḥ saścata svā uta śvetam vasudhitim nireke \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a āpo ha yad bṛhatīr viśvam āyan garbham dadhānā janayantīr agnim \\

Sentence: b tato devānāṁ sam avartatāsur ekaḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a yaś cid āpo mahinā paryapaśyad dakṣam dadhānā janayantīr yajñam \\

Sentence: b yo deveṣv adhi deva eka āsīt kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a pra yābhir yāsi dāśvāṁsam acchā niyudbhir vāyav iṣṭaye duroṇe \\

Sentence: b ni no rayiṁ subhojasam yuvasva ni vīram gavyam aśvyam ca rādhaḥ \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a ā no niyudbhiḥ śatinībhir adhvaraṁ sahasriṇībhir upa yāhi yajñam \\

Sentence: b vāyo asmin savane mādayasva yūyam pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a niyutvān vāyav ā gahy ayaṁ śukro ayāmi te \\

Sentence: b gantāsi sunvato gṛham \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a vāyo śukro ayāmi te madhvo agram diviṣṭiṣu \\

Sentence: b ā yāhi somapītaye spārho deva niyutvatā \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a vāyur agregā yajñaprīḥ sākam gan manasā yajñam \\

Sentence: b śivo niyudbhiḥ śivābhiḥ \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a vāyo ye te sahasriṇo rathāsas tebhir ā gahi \\

Sentence: b niyutvānt somapītaye \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a ekayā ca daśabhiś ca svabhūte dvābhyām iṣṭaye viṁśatī ca \\

Sentence: b tisṛbhiś ca vahase triṁśatā ca niyudbhir vāyav iha tā vi muñca \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a tava vāyav ṛtaspate tvaṣṭur jāmātar adbhuta \\

Sentence: b avāṁsy ā vṛṇīmahe \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a abhi tvā śūra nonumo dugdhā iva dhenavaḥ \\

Sentence: b īśānam asya jagataḥ svardṛśam īśānam indra tasthuṣaḥ \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a na tvāvāṁ anyo divyo na pārthivo na jāto na janiṣyate \\

Sentence: b aśvāyanto maghavann indra vājino gavyantas tvā havāmahe \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a tvām id dhi havāmahe sātā vājasya kāravaḥ \\

Sentence: b tvām vṛtreṣv indra satpatim naras tvām kāṣṭhāsv arvataḥ \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a sa tvam naś citra vajrahasta dhṛṣṇuyā maha stavāno adrivaḥ \\

Sentence: b gām aśvaṁ rathyam indra sam kira satrā vājam na jigyuṣe \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a kayā naś citra ā bhuvad ūtī sadāvṛdhaḥ sakhā \\

Sentence: b kayā śaciṣṭhayā vṛtā \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a kas tvā satyo madānām maṁhiṣṭho matsad andhasaḥ \\

Sentence: b dṛḍhā cid āruje vasu \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a abhī ṣu ṇaḥ sakhīnām avitā jaritṝṇām \\

Sentence: b śatam bhavāsy ūtaye \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a yajñā-yajñā vo agnaye girā-girā ca dakṣase \\

Sentence: b pra-pra vayam amṛtam jātavedasam priyam mitram na śaṁsiṣam \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a pāhi no agna ekayā pāhy ḥta dvitīyayā \\

Sentence: b pāhi gīrbhis tisṛbhir ūrjām pate pāhi catasṛbhir vaso \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a ūrjo napātaṁ sa hināyam asmayur dāśema havyadātaye \\

Sentence: b bhuvad vājeṣv avitā bhuvad vṛdha uta trātā tanūnām \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a samvatsaro si parivatsaro sīdāvatsaro sīdvatsaro si vatsaro si \\

Sentence: b uṣasas te kalpantām ahorātrās te kalpantām ardhamāsās te kalpantām māsās te kalpantām ṛtavas te kalpantāṁ samvatsaras te kalpatām \\

Sentence: c pretyā etyai sam cāñca pra ca sāraya \\

Sentence: d suparṇacid asi tayā devatayāṅgirasvad dhruvaḥ sīda \\\\

## Paragraph: 28

Verse: 1

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad samidhendram iḍas pade nābhā pṛthivyā adhi \\

Sentence: b divo varṣmant sam idhyata ojiṣṭhaś carṣaṇīsahām vetv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat tanūnapātam ūtibhir jetāram aparājitam \\

Sentence: b indram devaṁ svarvidam pathibhir madhumattamair narāśaṁsena tejasā vetv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad iḍābhir indram īḍitam ājuhvānam amartyam \\

Sentence: b devo devaiḥ savīryo vajrahastaḥ puramdaro vetv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad bariṣīndram niṣadvaram vṛṣabham naryāpasam \\

Sentence: b vasubhī rudrair ādityaiḥ suyugbhir barhir āsadad vetv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad ojo na vīryaṁ saho dvāra indram avardhayan \\

Sentence: b suprāyaṇā asmin yajñe vi śrayantām ṛtāvṛdho svāra indrāya mīḍhuṣe vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad uṣe indrasya dhenū sudughe mātarā mahī \\

Sentence: b savātarau na tejasā vatsam indram avardhatām vītām ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad daivyā hotārā bhiṣajā sakhāyā haviṣendram bhiṣajyataḥ \\

Sentence: b kavī devau pracetasāv indrāya dhatta indriyam vītām ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat tisro devīr na bheṣajam trayas tridhātavo pasa iḍā sarasvat bhāratī mahīḥ \\

Sentence: b indrapatnīr haviṣmatīr vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat tvaṣṭāram indram devam bhiṣajaṁ suyajam ghṛtaśriyam \\

Sentence: b pururūpaṁ suretasam maghonam indrāya tvaṣṭā dadhad indriyāṇi vetv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad vanaspatiṁ śamitāraṁ śatakratum dhiyo joṣṭāram indriyam \\

Sentence: b madhvā samañjan pathibhiḥ sugebhiḥ svadāti yajñam madhunā ghṛtena vetv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad indraṁ svāhājyasya svāhā medasaḥ svāhā stokānāṁ svāhā svāhākṛtīnāṁ svāhā havyasūktīnām \\

Sentence: b svāhā devā ājyapā juṣāṇā indra ājyasya vyantu hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a devam barhir indraṁ sudevam devair vīravat stīrṇam vedyām avardhayat \\

Sentence: b vastor vṛtam prāktor bhṛtaṁ rāyā barhiṣmato ty agād vasuvane vasudheyasya vetu yaja \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a devīr dvāra indraṁ samghāte vīḍvīr yāmann avardhayan \\

Sentence: b ā vatsena taruṇena kumāreṇa ca mīvatāpārvāṇaṁ reṇukakāṭam nudantām vasuvane vasudheyasya vyantu yaja \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a devī uṣāsānaktendram yajñe prayaty ahvetām \\

Sentence: b daivīr viśaḥ prāyāsiṣṭāṁ suprīte sudhite vasuvane vasudheyasya vītām yaja \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a devī joṣṭrī vasudhitī devam indram avardhatām \\

Sentence: b ayāvy anyāghā dveṣāṁsy ānyā vakṣad vasu vāryāṇi yajamānāya śikṣite vasuvane vasudheyasya vītām yaja \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a devī ūrjāhutī dughe payasendram avardhatām \\

Sentence: b iṣam ūrjam anyā vakṣat sagdhiṁ sapītim anyā navena pūrvam dayamāne purāṇena navam adhātām ūrjam ūrjāhutī ūrjayamāne vasu vṛyāṇi yajamānāya śikṣite vasuvane vasudheyasya vītām yaja \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a devā devyā hotārā devam indram avardhatām \\

Sentence: b hatāghaśaṁsāv ābhārṣṭām vasu vāryāṇi yajamānāya śikṣitau vasuvane vasudheyasya vītām yaja \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a devīs tisras tisro devīḥ patim indram avardhayan \\

Sentence: b aspṛkṣad bhāratī divaṁ rudrair yajñaṁ sarasvatīḍā vasumatī gṛhān vasuvane vasudheyasya vyantu yaja \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a deva indro narāśaṁsas trivarūthas tribandhuro devam indram avardhayat \\

Sentence: b śatena śitipṛṣṭhānām āhitaḥ sahasreṇa pra vartate mitrāvaruṇed asya hotram arhato bṛhaspati stotram aśvinādhvaryavam vasuvane vasudheyasya vetu yaja \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a devo devair vanaspatir hiraṇyaparṇo madhuśākhaḥ supippalo devam indram avardhayat \\

Sentence: b divam agreṇāspṛkṣad āntariksam pṛthivīm adṛmhīd vasuvane vasudheyasya vetu yaja \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a devam barhir vāritīnām devam indram avardhayat \\

Sentence: b svāsastham indreṇāsannam anyā barhīṁṣy abhy abhūd vasuvane vasudheyasya vetu yaja \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a devo agniḥ sviṣṭakṛd devam indram avardhayat \\

Sentence: b svṅṣṭam kurvant sviṣṭakṛt svṅṣṭam adya karotu no vasuvane vasudheyasya vetu yaja \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a agnim adya hotāram avṛṇītāyam yajamānaḥ pacan paktīḥ pacan puroḍāśam badhnann indrāya chāgam \\

Sentence: b sūpasthā adya devo vanaspatir abhavad indrāya chāgena \\

Sentence: c aghat tam medastaḥ prati pacatāgrabhīd avīvṛdhat puroḍāśena \\

Sentence: d tvām adya ṛṣa ārṣeya ṛṣīṇām napād avṛṇītāym yajamāno bahubhya ā samgatebhya eṣa me deveṣu vasu vāryāyakṣyata iti tā yā devā deva dānāny adus tāny asmā ā ca śāssvā ca gurasveṣitaś ca hotar asi bhadravācyāya preṣito mānuṣaḥ sūktavākāya sūktā brūhi \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat samidhānam mahad yaśaḥ susamiddham vareṇyam agnim indram vayodhasam \\

Sentence: b gāyatrīm chanda indriyam tryavim gām vayo dadhad vetv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat tanūnapātam udbhidam yam garbham aditir dadhe śucim indram vayodhasam \\

Sentence: b uṣṇiham chanda indriyam dityavāham gām vayo dadhad vetv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad īḍenyam īḍitam vṛtrahantamam iḍābhir īḍyaṁ sahaḥ somam indraṁ vayodhasam \\

Sentence: b anuṣṭubham chanda indriyam pañcāvim gām vayo dadhad vetv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat subarhiṣam pūṣaṇvantam amartyaṁ sīdantam barhiṣi priyṝ mṛtendram vayodhasam \\

Sentence: b bṛhatīm chanda indriyam trivatsam gām vayo dadhad vetv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad vyacasvatīḥ suprāyaṇā ṛtāvṛdho dvāro devīr hiraṇyayīr brahmāṇam indram vayodhasam \\

Sentence: b paṅktim chanda ihendriyam turyavāham gām vayo dadhad vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat supeśasā suśilpe bṛhatī ubhe naktoṣāsā na darśate viśvam indram vayodhasam \\

Sentence: b triṣṭubham chanda ihendriyam paṣṭhavaham gām vayo dadhad vītām ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat pracetasā devānām uttamam yaśo hotārā daivyā kavī sayujendram vayodhasam \\

Sentence: b jagatīm chanda indriyam anaḍvāham gām vayo dadhad vītām ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat peśasvatīs tisro devīr hiraṇyayīr bhāratīr bṛhatīr mahīḥ patim indram vayodhasam \\

Sentence: b virājam chanda ihendriyam dhenum gām na vayo dadhad vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat suretasam tvaṣṭāram puṣṭivardhanaṁ rūpāṇi bibhratam pṛthak puṣṭim indram vayodhasam \\

Sentence: b dvipadam chanda indriyam ukṣāṇam gām na vayo dadhad vetv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a hotā yakṣad vanaspatiṁ śamitāraṁ śatakratuṁ hiraṇyaparṇam ukthinaṁ raśanām bibhratam vaśim bhagam indram vayodhasam \\

Sentence: b kakubham chanda ihendriyam vaśām vehatam gām vayo dadhad vetv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a hotā yakṣat svāhākṛtīr agnim gṛhapatim pṛthag varuṇam bheṣajam kavim kṣatram indram vayodhasam \\

Sentence: b aticchandasam chanda indriyam bṛhad ṛṣabham gām vayo dadhad vyantv ājyasya hotar yaja \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a devam barhir vayodhasam devam indram avardhayat \\

Sentence: b gāyatryā chandasendriyam cakṣur indre vayo dadhad vasuvane vasudheyasya vetu yaja \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a devīr dvāro vayodhasaṁ śucim indram avardhayan \\

Sentence: b uṣṇihā chandasendriyam prāṇam indre vayo dadhad vasuvane vasudheyasya vyantu yaja \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a devī uṣāsānaktā devam indram vayodhasam devī devam avardhatām \\

Sentence: b anuṣṭubhā chandasendriyam balam indre vayo dadhad vasuvane vasudheyasya vītām yaja \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a devī joṣṭrī vasudhitī devam indram vayodhasam devī devam avardhatām \\

Sentence: b bṛhatyā chandasendriyaṁ śrotram indre vayo dadhad vasuvane vasudheyasya vītām yaja \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a devī ūrjāhutī dughe sudughe payasendram vayodhasam devī devam avardhatām \\

Sentence: b paṅktyā chandasendriyaṁ śukram indre vayo dadhad vasuvane vasudheyasya vītām yaja \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a devā daivyā hotārā devam indram vayodhasam devau devam avardhatām \\

Sentence: b triṣṭubhā chandasendriyam tviṣim indre vayo dadhad vasuvane vasudheyasya vītām yaja \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a devīs tisras tisro devīr vayodhasam patim indram avardhayan \\

Sentence: b jagatyā chandasendriyaṁ śūṣam indre vayo dadhad vasuvane vasudheyasya vyantu yaja \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a devo narāśaṁso devam indram vayodhasam devo devam avardhayat \\

Sentence: b virājā chandasendriyaṁ rupam indre vayo dadhad vasuvane vasudheyasya vetu yaja \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a devo vanaspatir devam indram vayodhasam devo devam avardhayat \\

Sentence: b dvipadā chandasendriyam bhagam indre vayo dadhad vasuvane vasudheyasya vetu yaja \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a devam barhir vāritīnām devam indram vayodhasam devam devam avardhayat \\

Sentence: b kakubhā chandasendriyam yaśa indre vayo dadhad vasuvane vasudheyasya vetu yaja \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a devo agniḥ sviṣṭakṛd devam indram vayodhasam devo devam avardhayat \\

Sentence: b aticchandasā chandasendriyam kṣatram indre vayo dadhad vasuvane vasudheyasya vetu yaja \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a agnim adya hotāram avṛṇītāyam yajamānaḥ pacan paktīḥ pacan puroḍāśam badhnann indrāya vayodhase chāgam \\

Sentence: b sūpasthā adya devo vanaspatir abhavad indrāya vayodhase chāgena \\

Sentence: c aghat tam medastaḥ prati pacatāgrabhīd avīvṛdhat puroḍāśena \\

Sentence: d tvām adya ṛṣa ārṣeya ṛṣīṇām napād avṛṇītāym yajamāno bahubhya ā samgatebhya eṣa me deveṣu vasu vāryāyakṣyata iti tā yā devā deva dānāny adus tāny asmā ā ca śāssvā ca gurasveṣitaś ca hotar asi bhadravācyāya preṣito mānuṣaḥ sūktavākāya sūktā brūhi \\\\

## Paragraph: 29

Verse: 1

Sentence: a samiddho añjan kṛdarammatīnām ghṛtam agne madhumat pinvamānaḥ \\

Sentence: b vājī vahan vājinam jātavedo devānām vakṣi priyam ā sadhastham \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a ghṛtenāñjant sam patho devayānān prajānan vājy apy etu devān \\

Sentence: b anu tvā sapte pradiśaḥ sacantāṁ svadhām asmai yajamānāya dhehi \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a īḍyaś cāsi vandyaś ca vājinn āśuś cāsi medhyaś ca sapte \\

Sentence: b agniṣ ṭvā devair vasubhiḥ sajoṣāḥ prītam vahnim vahatu jātavedāḥ \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a stīrṇam barhiḥ suṣṭarīmā juṣāmoru pṛthu prathamānam pṛthivyām \\

Sentence: b devebhir yuktam aditiḥ sajoṣāḥ syonam kṛṇvāna suvite dadhātu \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a etā u vaḥ subhagā viśvarūpā vi pakṣobhiḥ śrayamāṇā ud ātaiḥ \\

Sentence: b ṛṣvāḥ satīḥ kavaṣaḥ śumbhamānā dvāro devīḥ suprāyaṇā bhavantu \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a antarā mitrāvaruṇā carantī mukham yajñānām abhi samvidāne \\

Sentence: b uṣāsā vāṁ suhiraṇye suśilpe ṛtasya yonāv iha sādayāmi \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a prathamā vāṁ sarathinā suvarṇā devau paśyantau bhuvanāni viśvā \\

Sentence: b apiprayam codanā vām mimānā hotārā jyotiḥ pradiśā diśantā \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a ādityair no bhāratī vaṣṭu yajñaṁ sarasvatī saha rudrair na āvīt \\

Sentence: b iḍopahūtā vasubhiḥ sajoṣā yajñam no devīr amṛteṣu dhatta \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a tvaṣṭā vīram devakāmam jajāna tvaṣṭur arvā jāyata āśur aśvaḥ \\

Sentence: b tvaṣṭedam viśvam bhuvanam jajāna bahoḥ kartāram iha yakṣi hotaḥ \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a aśvo ghṛtena tmanyā samakta upa devāṁ ṛtuśaḥ pātha etu \\

Sentence: b vanaspatir devalokam prajānann agninā havyā svaditāni vakṣat \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a prajāpates tapasā vāvṛdhānaḥ sadyo jāto dadhiṣe yajñam agne \\

Sentence: b svāhākṛtena haviṣā purogā yāhi sādhyā havir adantu devāḥ \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a yad akrandaḥ prathamam jāyamāna udyant samudrād uta vā purīṣāt \\

Sentence: b śyenasya pakṣā hariṇasya bāhū upastutyam mahi jātam te arvan \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a yamena dattam trita enam āyunag indra eṇam prathamo adhy atiṣṭhat \\

Sentence: b gandharvo asya raśanām agṛbhṇāt sūrād aśvam vasavo nir ataṣṭa \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a asi yamo asy ādityo arvann asi trito guhyena vratena \\

Sentence: b asi somena samayā vipṛkta āhus te trīṇi divi bandhanāni \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a trīṇi ta āhur divi bandhanāni trīṇy apsu trīṇy antaḥ samudre \\

Sentence: b uteva me varuṇaś chantsy arvan yatrā ta āhuḥ paramam janitram \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a imā te vājinn avamārjanānīmā śaphānāṁ sanitur nidhānā \\

Sentence: b atrā te bhadrā raśanā apaśyam ṛtasya yā abhirakṣanti gopāḥ \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a ātmānam te manasārād ajānām avo divā patayantam patamgam \\

Sentence: b śiro apaśyam pathibhiḥ sugebhir areṇubhir jehamānam patatri \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a atrā te rūpam uttamam apaśyam jigīṣamāṇam iṣa ā pade goḥ \\

Sentence: b yadā te marto anu bhogam ānaḍ ād id grasiṣṭha oṣadhīr ajīgaḥ \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a anu tvā ratho anu maryo arvann anu gāvo nu bhagaḥ kanīnām \\

Sentence: b anu vrātāsas tava sakhyam īyur anu devā mamire vīryam te \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a hiraṇyaśṛṅgo yo asya pādā manojavā avara indra āsīt \\

Sentence: b devā id asya haviradyam āyan yo arvantam prathamo adhyatiṣṭhat \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a īrmāntāsaḥ silikamadhyamāsaḥ saṁ śūraṇāso divyāso atyāḥ \\

Sentence: b haṁsā iva śreṇiśo yatante yad ākṣiṣur divyam ajmam aśvāḥ \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a tava śarīram patayiṣṇv arvan tava cittam vāta iva dhrajīmān \\

Sentence: b tava śṛṅgāṇi viṣṭhitā purutrāraṇyeṣu jarbhurāṇā caranti \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a upa prāgāc chasanam vājy arvā devadrīcā manasā dīdhyānaḥ \\

Sentence: b ajaḥ puro nīyate nābhir asyānu paścāt kavayo yanti rebhāḥ \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a upa prāgāt paramam yat sadhastham arvāṁ acchā pitaram mātaram ca \\

Sentence: b adyā devān juṣṭatamo hi gamyā athā śāste dāśuṣe vāryāṇi \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a samiddho adya manuṣo duroṇe devo devān yajasi jātavedaḥ \\

Sentence: b ā ca vaha mitramahaś cikitvān tvam dūtaḥ kavir asi pracetāḥ \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a tanūnapāt patha ṛtasya yānān madhvā samañjant svadayā sujihva \\

Sentence: b manmāni dhībhir uta yajñam ṛndhan devatrā ca kṛṇuhy adhvaram naḥ \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a narāśaṁsasya mahimānam eṣām upa stoṣāma yajatasya yajñaiḥ \\

Sentence: b ye sukratavaḥ śucayo dhiyamdhāḥ svadanti devā ubhayāni havyā \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a ājuhvāna īḍyo vandyaś cā yāhy agne vasubhiḥ sajoṣāḥ \\

Sentence: b tvam devānām asi yahva hotā sa enān yakṣīṣito yajīyān \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a prācīnam barhiḥ pradiśā pṛthivyā vastor asyā vṛjyate agre ahnām \\

Sentence: b vy ḥ prathate vitaram varīyo devebhyo aditaye syonam \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a vyacasvatīr urviyā vi śrayantām patibhyo na janayaḥ śumbhamānāḥ \\

Sentence: b devīr dvāro bṛhatīr viśvaminvā devebhyo bhavata suprāyaṇāḥ \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a ā suṣvayantī yajate upāke uṣāsānaktā sadatām ni yonau \\

Sentence: b divye yoṣaṇe bṛhatī surukme adhi śriyaṁ śukrapiśam dadhāne \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a daivyā hotārā prathamā suvācā mimānā yajñam manuṣo yajadhyai \\

Sentence: b pracodayantā vidatheṣu kārū prācīnam jyotiḥ pradiśā diśantā \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a ā no yajñam bhāratī tūyam etv iḍā manuṣvad iha cetayantī \\

Sentence: b tisro devīr barhir edaṁ syonaṁ sarasvatī svapasaḥ sadantu \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a ya ime dyāvāpṛthivī janitrī rūpair apiṁśad bhuvanāni viśvā \\

Sentence: b tam adya hotar iṣito yajīyān devam tvaṣṭāram iha yakṣi vidvān \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a upāva sṛja tmanyā samañjan devānām pātha ṛtuthā havīṁṣi \\

Sentence: b vanaspatiḥ śamitā devo agniḥ svadantu havyam madhunā ghṛtena \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a sadyo jāto vy amimīta yajñam agnir devānām abhavat purogāḥ \\

Sentence: b asya hotuḥ pradiśy ṛtasya vāci svāhākṛtaṁ havir adantu devāḥ \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a ketum kṛṇvann aketave peśo maryā apeśase \\

Sentence: b sam uṣadbhir ajāyathāḥ \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a jīmūtasyeva bhavati pratīkam yad varmī yāti samadām upasthe \\

Sentence: b anāviddhayā tanvā jaya tvaṁ sa tvā varmaṇo mahimā pipartu \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a dhanvanā gā dhanvanājim jayema dhanvanā tīvrāḥ samado jayema \\

Sentence: b dhanuḥ śatror apakāmam kṛṇoti dhanvanā sarvāḥ pradiśo jayema \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a vakṣyantīved ā ganīganti karṇam priyaṁ sakhāyam pariṣasvajānā \\

Sentence: b yoṣeva śiṅkte vitatādhi dhanvan jyā iyaṁ samane pārayantī \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a te ācarantī samaneva yoṣā māteva putram bibhṛtām upasthe \\

Sentence: b apa śatrūn vidhyatāṁ samvidāne ārtnī ime viṣphurantī amitrān \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a bahvīnām pitā bahur asya putraś ciścā kṛṇoti samanāvagatya \\

Sentence: b iṣudhiḥ saṅkāḥ pṛtanāś ca sarvāḥ pṛṣṭhe ninaddho jayati prasūtaḥ \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a rathe tiṣṭhan nayati vājinaḥ puro yatra-yatra kāmayate suṣārathiḥ \\

Sentence: b abhīśūnām mahimānam panāyata manaḥ paścād anu yacchanti raśmayaḥ \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a tīvrān ghoṣān kṛṇvate vṛṣapāṇayo śvā rathebhiḥ saha vājayantaḥ \\

Sentence: b avakrāmantaḥ prapadair amitrān kṣiṇanti śatrūṁr anapavyayantaḥ \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a rathavāhanaṁ havir asya nāma yatrāyudham nihitam asya varma \\

Sentence: b tatrā ratham upa śagmaṁ sadema viśvāhā vayaṁ sumanasyamānāḥ \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a svāduṣaṁsadaḥ pitaro vayodhāḥ kṛcchreśritaḥ śaktīvanto gabhīrāḥ \\

Sentence: b citrasenā iṣubalā amṛdhrāḥ satovīrā uravo vrātasāhāḥ \\\\

Verse: 47

Sentence: a brāhmaṇāsaḥ pitaraḥ somyāsaḥ śive no dyāvāpṛthivī anehasā \\

Sentence: b pūṣā naḥ pātu duritād ṛtāvṛdho rakṣā mākir no aghaśaṁsa īśata \\\\

Verse: 48

Sentence: a suparṇam vaste mṛgo asyā danto gobhiḥ samnaddhā patati prasūtā \\

Sentence: b yatrā naraḥ sam ca vi ca dravanti tatrāsmabhyam iṣavaḥ śarma yaṁsan \\\\

Verse: 49

Sentence: a ṛjīte pari vṛṅdhi no śmā bhavatu nas tanūḥ \\

Sentence: b somo adhi bravītu no ditiḥ śarma yacchatu \\\\

Verse: 50

Sentence: a ā jaṅghanti sānv eṣām jaghanāṁ upa jighnate \\

Sentence: b aśvājani pracetaso śvānt samatsu codaya \\\\

Verse: 51

Sentence: a ahir iva bhogaiḥ pary eti bāhum jyāyā hetim paribādhamānaḥ \\

Sentence: b hastaghno viśvā vayunāni vidvān pumān pumāṁsam pari pātu viśvataḥ \\\\

Verse: 52

Sentence: a vanaspate vīḍvaṅgo hi bhūyā asmatsakhā prataraṇaḥ suvīraḥ \\

Sentence: b gobhiḥ samnaddho asi vīḍayasvāsthātā te jayatu jetvāni \\\\

Verse: 53

Sentence: a divaḥ pṛthivyāḥ pary oja udbhṛtam vanaspatibhyaḥ pary ābhṛtaṁ sahaḥ \\

Sentence: b apām ojmānam pari gobhir āvṛtam indrasya vajraṁ haviṣā ratham yaja \\\\

Verse: 54

Sentence: a indrasya vajro marutām anīkam mitrasya garbho varuṇasya nābhiḥ \\

Sentence: b semām no havyadātim juṣāṇo deva ratha prati havyā gṛbhāya \\\\

Verse: 55

Sentence: a upa śvāsaya pṛthivīm uta dyām purutrā te manutām viṣṭhitam jagat \\

Sentence: b sa dundubhe sajūr indreṇa devair dūrād davīyo apa sedha śatrūn \\\\

Verse: 56

Sentence: a ā krandaya balam ojo na ā dhā ni ṣṭanihi duritā bādhamānaḥ \\

Sentence: b apa protha dundubhe ducchunā ita indrasya muṣṭir asi vīḍayasva \\\\

Verse: 57

Sentence: a āmūr aja pratyā vartayemāḥ ketumad dundubhir vāvadīti \\

Sentence: b sam aśvaparṇāś caranti no naro smākam indra rathino jayantu \\\\

Verse: 58

Sentence: a āgneyaḥ kṛṣṇagrīvaḥ sārasvatī meṣī babhruḥ saumyaḥ pauṣṇaḥ śyāmaḥ śitipṛṣṭho bārhaspatyaḥ śilpo vaiśvadeva aindro ruṇo mārutaḥ kalmāṣa aindrāgnaḥ saṁhito dhorāmaḥ sāvitro vāruṇaḥ kṛṣṇa ekaśitipāt petvaḥ \\\\

Verse: 59

Sentence: a agnaye nīkavate rohitāñjir anaḍvān adhorāmau sāvitrau pauṣṇau rajatanābhī vaiśvadevau piśamgau tūparau mārutaḥ kalmāṣa āgneyaḥ kṛṣṇo jaḥ sārasvatī meṣī vāruṇaḥ petvaḥ \\\\

Verse: 60

Sentence: a agnaye gāyatrāya trivṛte rāthantarāyāṣṭākapāla indrāya traiṣṭubhāya pañcadaśāya barhatāyaikādaśakapālo viśvebhyo devebhyo jāgatebhyaḥ saptadaśebhyo vairūpebhyo dvādaśakapālo mitrāvaruṇābhyām ānuṣṭubhābhyām ekaviṁśābhyām vairājābhyām payasyā bṛhaspataye pāṅktāya triṇavāya śākvarāya caruḥ savitra auṣṇihāya trayastriṁśāya raivatāya dvādaśakapālaḥ prājāpatyaś carur adityai viṣṇupatnyai carur agnaye vaiśvānarāya dvādaśakapālo numatyā aṣṭākapālaḥ \\\\

## Paragraph: 30

Verse: 1

Sentence: a deva savitaḥ pra suva yajñam pra suva yajñapatim bhagāya \\

Sentence: b divyo gandharvaḥ ketupūḥ ketam naḥ punātu vācaspatir vājam naḥ svadatu \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a tat savitur vareṇyam bhargo devasya dhīmahi \\

Sentence: b dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayat \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a viśvāni deva savitar duritāni parā suva \\

Sentence: b yad bhadram tan na ā suva \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a vibhaktāraṁ havāmahe vasoś citrasya rādhasaḥ \\

Sentence: b savitāram nṛcakṣasam \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a brahmaṇe brāhmaṇam kṣatrāya rājanyam marudbhyo vaiśyam tapase śūdram tamase taskaram nārakāya vīrahaṇam pāpmane klībam ākrayāyā ayogūm kāmāya puṁścalūm atikruṣṭāya māgadham \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a nṛttāya sūtam gītāya śailūṣam dharmāya sabhācaram nariṣṭhāyai bhīmalam narmāya rebhaṁ hasāya kārim ānandāya strīṣukham pramade kumārīputram medhāyai rathakāram dhairyāya takṣāṇam \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a tapase kaulālam māyāyai karmāraṁ rūpāya maṇikāraṁ śubhe vapaṁ śaravyāyā iṣukāraṁ hetyai dhanuṣkāram karmaṇe jyākāram diṣṭāya rajjusarjam mṛtyave mṛgayum antakāya śvaninam \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a nadībhyaḥ pauñjiṣṭham ṛkṣīkābhyo naiṣādam puruṣavyāghrāya durmadam gandharvāpsarobhyo vrātyam prayugbhya unmattaṁ sarpadevajanebhyo pratipadamayebhyaḥ kitavam īryatāyā akitavam piśācebhyo bidalakārīm yātudhānebhyaḥ kaṇṭakīkārīm \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a samdhaye jāram gehāyopapatim ārtyai parivittam nirṛtyai parivividānam arāddhyā edidhiṣuḥpatim niṣkṛtyai peśaskārīṁ samjñānāya smarakārīm prakāmodyāyopasadam varṇāyānurudham balāyopadām \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a utsādebhyaḥ kubjam pramude vāmanam dvārbhyaḥ srāmaṁ svapnāyāndham ardhamāya badhiram pavitrāya bhiṣajam prajñānāya nakṣatradarśam āśikṣāyai praśninam upaśikṣāyā abhipraśninam maryādāyai praśnavivākam \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a amrbhyo hastipam javāyāśvapam puṣṭyai gopālam vīryāyāvipālam tejase japālam irāyai kīnāśam kīlālāya surākāram bhadrāya gṛhapaṁ śreyase vittadham ādhyakṣyāyānukṣattāram \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a bhāyai dārvāhāram prabhāyā agnyedham bradhnasya viṣṭapāyābhiṣektāram varṣiṣṭhāya nākāya pariveṣṭāram devalokāya peśitāram manuṣyalokāya prakaritāraṁ sarvebhyo lokebhyo upasektāram avaṛtyai badhāyopamanthitāram medhāya vāsaḥpalpūlīm prakāmāya rajayitrīm \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a ṛtaye stenahṛdayam vairahatyāya piśunam viviktyai kṣattāram aupadraṣṭryāyānukṣattāram bālāyānucaram bhūmne pariṣkandam priyāya priyavādinam ariṣṭyā aśvasādaṁ svargāya lokāya bhāgadugham varṣiṣṭhāya nākāya pariveṣṭāram \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a manyave yastāpam krodhāya nisaram yogāya yoktāraṁ śokāyābhisartāram kṣemāya vimoktāram utkūlebhyas triṣṭhinam vapuṣe mānaskṛtaṁ śīlāyāñjanīkārīm nirṛtyai kośakārīm yamāyāsūm \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a yamāya yamasūm atharvabhyo vatokāṁ samvatsarāya paryāyiṇīm parivatsarāyāvijātām idāvatsarāyātītvarīm idvatsarāyātiṣkadvarīm vatsarāya vijarjarāṁ samvatsarāya paliknīm ṛbhubhyo jinasamdhaṁ sādhyebhyaś carmamnam \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a sarebhyo dhaivaram upasthāvarābhyo dāśam vaiśantābhyo baindam naḍvalābhyaḥ śauṣkalam pārāya mārgāram avarāya kevartam tīrthebhya āndam viṣamebhyo mainālaṁ svanebhyaḥ parṇakam guhābhyaḥ kirātaṁ sānubhyo jambhakam parvatebhyaḥ kimpūruṣam \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a bībhatsāyai paulkasam varṇāya hiraṇyakāram tulāyai vāṇijam paścādoṣāya glāvinam viśvebhyo bhūtebhyaḥ sidhmalam bhūtyai jāgaraṇam abhūtyai svapanam ārtyai janavādinam vyṛrddhyā apagalbhaṁ saṁśarāya pracchidam \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a akṣarājāya kitavam kṛtāyādinavadarśam tretāyai kalpinam dvāparāyādhikalpinam āskandāya sabhāsthāṇum mṛtyave govyaccham antakāya goghātam kṣudhe yo gām vikṛntantam bhikṣamāṇa upatiṣṭhati duṣkṛtāya carakācāryam pāpmane sailagam \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a pratiśrutkāyā artanam ghoṣāya bhaṣam antāya bahuvādinam anantāya mūkaṁ śabdāyāḍambarāghātam mahase vīṇāvādam krośāya tūṇavadhmam avarasparāya śaṅkhadhmam vanāya vatapam anyatoraṇyāya dāvapam \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a narmāya puṁścalūṁ hasāya kārim yādase śābalyām grāmaṇyam gaṇakam abhikrośakam tān mahase vīṇāvādam pāṇighnam tūṇavadhmam tān nṛtāyānandāya talavam \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a agnaye pīvānam pṛthivyai pīṭhasarpiṇam vāyave cāṇḍālam antarikṣāya vaṁśanartinam dive khalatiṁ sūryāya haryakṣam nakṣatrebhyaḥ kirmiram candramase kilāsam ahne śuklam piṅgākṣaṁ rātryai kṛṣṇam piṅgākṣam \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a ataitān aṣṭau virūpān ā labhate tidīrgham cātihrasvam cātisthūlam cātikṛśam cātiśuklam cātikṛṣṇam cātikulvam cātilomaśam ca \\

Sentence: b aśūdrā abrāhmaṇās te prājāpatyāḥ \\

Sentence: c māgadhaḥ puṁścalī kitavaḥ klībo śūdrā abrāhmaṇās te prājāpatyāḥ \\\\

## Paragraph: 31

Verse: 1

Sentence: a sahasraśīrṣā puruṣaḥ sahasrākṣaḥ sahasrapāt \\

Sentence: b sa bhūmiṁ sarvata spṛtvāty atiṣṭhad daśāṅgulam \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a puruṣa evedaṁ sarvam yad bhūtam yac ca bhāvyam \\

Sentence: b utāmṛtatvasyeśāno yad annenātirohati \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a etāvān asya mahimāto jyāyāṁś ca pūruṣaḥ \\

Sentence: b pādo sya viśvā bhūtāni tripād asyāmṛtam divi \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a tripād ūrdhva ud ait puruṣaḥ pādo syehābhavat punaḥ \\

Sentence: b tato viṣvaṅ vy akrāmat sāśanānaśane abhi \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a tasmād virāḍ ajāyata virājo adhi pūruṣaḥ \\

Sentence: b sa jāto aty aricyata paścād bhūmim atho puraḥ \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a tasmād yajñāt sarvahutaḥ sambhṛtam pṛṣadājyam \\

Sentence: b paśūṁs tāṁś cakre vāyavyān āraṇyā grāmyāś ca ye \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a tasmād yajñāt sarvahuta ṛcaḥ sāmāni jajñire \\

Sentence: b chandāṁsi jajñire tasmād yajus tasmād ajāyata \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a tasmād aśvā ajāyanta ye ke cobhayādataḥ \\

Sentence: b gāvo ha jajñire tasmāt tasmāj jātā ajāvayaḥ \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a tam yajñam barhiṣi praukṣan puruṣam jātam agrataḥ \\

Sentence: b tena devā ayajanta sādhyā ṛṣayaś ca ye \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a yat puruṣam vy adadhuḥ katidhā vy akalpayan \\

Sentence: b mukham kim asya kau bāhū kā ūrū pādā ucyete \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a brāhmaṇo sya mukham āsīd bāhū rājanyaḥ kṛtaḥ \\

Sentence: b ūrū tad asya yad vaiśyaḥ padbhyāṁ śūdro ajāyata \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a candramā manaso jātaś cakṣoḥ sūryo ajāyata \\

Sentence: b śrotrād vāyuś ca prāṇaś ca mukhād agnir ajāyata \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a nābhyā āsīd antarikṣaṁ śīrṣṇo dyauḥ sam avartata \\

Sentence: b padbhyām bhūmir diśaḥ śrotrāt tathā lokāṁ akalpayan \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a yat puruṣeṇa haviṣā devā yajñam atanvata \\

Sentence: b vasanto syāsīd ājyam grīṣma idhmaḥ śarad dhaviḥ \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a saptāsyāsan paridhayas triḥ sapta samidhaḥ kṛtāḥ \\

Sentence: b devā yad yajñam tanvānā abadhnan puruṣam paśum \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a yajñena yajñam ayajanta devās tāni dharmāṇi prathamāny āsan \\

Sentence: b te ha nākam mahimānaḥ sacanta yatra pūrve sādhyāḥ santi devāḥ \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a adbhyaḥ sambhṛtaḥ pṛthivyai rasāc ca viśvakarmaṇaḥ sam avartatāgre \\

Sentence: b tasya tvaṣṭā vidadhad rūpam eti tan martyasya devatvam ājānam agre \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a vedāham etam puruṣam mahāntam ādityavarṇam tamasaḥ parastāt \\

Sentence: b tam eva viditvāti mṛtyum eti nānyaḥ panthā vidyate yanāya \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a prajāpatiś carati garbhe antar ajāyamāno bahudhā vi jāyate \\

Sentence: b tasya yonim pari paśyanti dhīrās tasmin ha tasthur bhuvanāni viśvā \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a yo devebhya ātapati yo devānām purohitaḥ \\

Sentence: b pūrvo yo devebhyo jāto namo rucāya brāhmaye \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a rucam brāhmyam janayanto devā agre tad abruvan \\

Sentence: b yas tvaivam brāhmaṇo vidyāt tasya devā asan vaśe \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a śrīś ca te lakṣmīś ca patnyāv ahorātre pārśve nakṣatrāṇi rūpam aśvinau vyāttām \\

Sentence: b iṣṇann iṣāṇāmum ma iṣāṇa sarvalokam ma iṣāṇa \\\\

## Paragraph: 32

Verse: 1

Sentence: a tad evāgnis tad ādityas tad vāyus tad u candramāḥ \\

Sentence: b tad eva śukram tad brahma tā āpaḥ sa prajāpatiḥ \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a sarve nimeṣā jajñire vidyutaḥ puruṣād adhi \\

Sentence: b nainam ūrdhvam na tiryañcam na madhye pari jagrabhat \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a na tasya pratimā asti yasya nāma mahad yaśaḥ \\

Sentence: b hiraṇyagarbha ity eṣaḥ \\

Sentence: c mā mā hiṁsīd ity eṣā \\

Sentence: d yasmān na jāta ity eṣa \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a eṣo ha devaḥ pradiśo nu sarvāḥ pūrvo ha jātaḥ sa u garbhe antaḥ \\

Sentence: b sa eva jātaḥ sa janiṣyamāṇaḥ pratyaṅ janās tiṣthati sarvatomukhaḥ \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a yasmāj jātam na purā kim canaiva ya ābabhūva bhuvanāni viśvā \\

Sentence: b prajāpatiḥ prajayā saṁrarāṇas trīṇi jyotīṁṣi sacate sa aiḍaśī \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a yena dyaur ugrā pṛthivī ca dṛḍhā yena sva stabhitam yena nākaḥ \\

Sentence: b yo antarikṣe rajaso vimānaḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a yam krandasī avasā tastabhāne abhy aikṣetām manasā rejamāne \\

Sentence: b yatrādhi sūra udito vibhāti kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema \\

Sentence: c āpo ha yad bṛhatīḥ \\

Sentence: d yaś cid āpaḥ \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a venas tat paśyan nihitam guhā sad yatra viśvam bhavaty ekanīḍam \\

Sentence: b tasminn idaṁ sam ca vi caiti sarvaṁ sa otaḥ protaś ca vibhūḥ prajāsu \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a pra tad voced amṛtam nu vidvān gandharvo dhāma vibhṛtam guhā sat \\

Sentence: b trīṇi padāni nihitā guhāsya yas tāni veda sa pituḥ pitāsat \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a sa no bandhur janitā sa vidhātā dhāmāni veda bhuvanāni viśvā \\

Sentence: b yatra devā amṛtam ānaśānās tṛtīye dhāmann adhy airayanta \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a parītya bhūtāni parītya lokān parītya sarvāḥ pradiśo diśaś ca \\

Sentence: b upasthāya prathamajām ṛtasyātmanātmānam abhi sam viveśa \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a pari dyāvāpṛthivī sadya itvā pari lokān pari diśaḥ pari svaḥ \\

Sentence: b ṛtasya tantum vitatam vicṛtya tad apaśyat tad abhavat tad āsīt \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a sadasas patim adbhutam priyam indrasya kāmyam \\

Sentence: b sanim medhām ayāsiṣaṁm svāhā \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a yām medhām devagaṇāḥ pitaraś copāsate \\

Sentence: b tayā mām adya medhayāgne medhāvinam kuru svāhā \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a medhām me varuṇo dadātu medhām agniḥ prajāpatiḥ \\

Sentence: b medhām indras ca vāyuś ca medhām dhātā dadātu me svāhā \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a idam me brahma ca kṣatram cobhe śriyam aśnutām \\

Sentence: b mayi devā dadhatu śriyam uttamām tasyai te svāhā \\\\

## Paragraph: 33

Verse: 1

Sentence: a asyājarāso damām aritrā arcaddhūmāso agnayaḥ pāvakāḥ \\

Sentence: b śvitīcayaḥ śvātrāso bhuraṇyavo vanarṣado vāyavo na somāḥ \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a harayo dhumaketavo vātajūtā upa dyavi \\

Sentence: b yatante vṛthag agnayaḥ \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a yajā no mitrāvaruṇā yajā devāṁ ṛtam bṛhat \\

Sentence: b agne yakṣi svam damam \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a yukṣvā hi devahūtamāṁ aśvāṁ agne rathīr iva \\

Sentence: b ni hotā pūrvyaḥ sadaḥ \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a dve virūpe carataḥ svarthe anyānyā vatsam upa dhāpayete \\

Sentence: b harir anyasyām bhavati svadhāvāñ chukro anyasyām dadṛśe suvarcāḥ \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a ayam iha prathamo dhāyi dhātṛbhir hotā yajiṣṭho adhvareṣv īḍyaḥ \\

Sentence: b yam apnavāno bhṛgavo virurucur vaneṣu citram vibhvam viśe-viśe \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a trīṇi śatā trī sahasrāṇy agnim triṁśac ca devā nava cāsaparyan \\

Sentence: b aukṣan ghṛtair astṛṇan barhir asmā ād id dhotāram ny asādayanta \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a mūrdhānam divo aratim pṛthivyā vaiśvānaram ṛta ā jātam agnim \\

Sentence: b kaviṁ samrājam atithim janānām āsann ā pātram janayanta devāḥ \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a agnir vṛtrāṇi jaṅghanad draviṇasyur vipanyayā \\

Sentence: b samiddhaḥ śukra āhutaḥ \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a viśvebhiḥ somyam madhv agna indreṇa vāyunā \\

Sentence: b pibā mitrasya dhāmabhiḥ \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a ā yad iṣe nṛpatim teja ānaṭ śuci reto niṣiktam dyaur abhīke \\

Sentence: b agniḥ śardham anavadyam yuvānaṁ svādhyam janayat sūdayac ca \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a agne śardha mahate saubhagāya tava dyumnāny uttamāni santu \\

Sentence: b sam jāspatyaṁ suyamam ā kṛṇuṣva śatrūyatām abhi tiṣṭhā mahāṁsi \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a tvāṁ hi mandratamam arkaśokair vavṛmahe mahi naḥ śroṣy agne \\

Sentence: b indram na tvā śavasā devatā vāyum pṛṇanti rādhasā nṛtamāḥ \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a tve agne svāhuta priyāsaḥ santu sūrayaḥ \\

Sentence: b yantāro ye maghavāno janānām ūrvān dayanta gonām \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a śrudhi śrutkarṇa vahnibhir devair agne sayāvabhiḥ \\

Sentence: b ā sīdantu barhiṣi mitro aryamā prātaryāvāṇo adhvaram \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a viśveṣām aditir yajñiyānām viśveṣām atithir mānuṣāṇām \\

Sentence: b agnir devānām ava āvṛṇānaḥ sumṛḍīko bhavatu jātavedāḥ \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a maho agneḥ samidhānasya śarmaṇy anāgā mitre varuṇe svastaye \\

Sentence: b śreṣṭhe syāma savituḥ savīmani tad devānām avo adyā vṛṇīmahe \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a āpaś cit pipyu staryo na gāvo nakṣann ṛtam jaritāras ta indra \\

Sentence: b yāhi vāyur na niyuto no acchā tvaṁ hi dhībhir dayase vi vājān \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a gāva upāvatāvatam mahī yajñasya rapsudā \\

Sentence: b ubhā karṇā hiraṇyayā \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a yad adya sūra udite nāgā mitro aryamā \\

Sentence: b suvāti savitā bhagaḥ \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a ā sute siñcata śriyaṁ rodasyor abhiśriyam \\

Sentence: b rasā dadhīta vṛṣabham \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a ātiṣṭhantam pari viśve abhūṣañ chriyo vasānaś carati svarociḥ \\

Sentence: b mahat tad vṛṣṇo asurasya nāmā viśvarūpo amṛtāni tasthau \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a pra vo mahe mandamānāyāndhaso rcā viśvānarāya viśvābhuve \\

Sentence: b indrasya yasya sumakhaṁ saho mahi śravo nṛmṇam ca rodasī saparyataḥ \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a bṛhann id idhma eṣām bhūri śastam pṛthuḥ svaruḥ \\

Sentence: b yeṣām indro yuvā sakhā \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a indrehi matsy andhaso viśvebhiḥ somaparvabhiḥ \\

Sentence: b mahāṁ abhiṣṭir ojasā \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a indro vṛtram avṛṇoc chardhanītiḥ pra māyinām aminād varpaṇītiḥ \\

Sentence: b ahan vyaṁsam uśadhag vaneṣv āvir dhenā akṛṇod rāmyāṇām \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a kutas tvam indra māhinaḥ sann eko yāsi satpate kim ta itthā \\

Sentence: b sam pṛcchase samarāṇaḥ śubhānair voces tan no harivo yat te asme \\

Sentence: c mahāṁ indro ya ojasā \\

Sentence: d kadā cana starīr asi \\

Sentence: e kadā cana pra yucchasi \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a ā tat ta indrāyavaḥ panantābhi ya ūrvam gomantam titṛtsān \\

Sentence: b sakṛtsvam ye puruputrām mahīṁ sahasradhārām bṛhatīm dudukṣan \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a imām te dhiyam pra bhare maho mahīm asya stotre dhiṣaṇā yat ta ānaje \\

Sentence: b tam utsave ca prasave ca sāsahim indram devāsaḥ śavasāmadann anu \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a vibhrāḍ bṛhat pibatu somyam madhv āyur dadhad yajñapatāv avihrutam \\

Sentence: b vātajūto yo abhirakṣati tmanā prajāḥ pupoṣa purudhā vi rājati \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a ud u tyam jātavedasam devam vahanti ketavaḥ \\

Sentence: b dṛśe viśvāya sūryaṁ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a yenā pāvaka cakṣasā bhuraṇyantam janāṁ anu \\

Sentence: b tvam varuṇa paśyasi \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a daivyāv adhvaryū ā gataṁ rathena sūryatvacā \\

Sentence: b madhvā yajñaṁ sam añjāthe \\

Sentence: c tam pratnathā \\

Sentence: d ayam venaḥ \\

Sentence: e citram devānām \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a ā na iḍābhir vidathe suśasti viśvānaraḥ savitā deva etu \\

Sentence: b api yathā yuvāno matsathā no viśvam jagad abhipitve manīṣā \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a yad adya kac ca vṛtrahann udagā abhi sūrya \\

Sentence: b sarvam tad indra te vaśe \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a taraṇir viśvadarśato jyotiṣkṛd asi sūrya \\

Sentence: b viśvam ā bhāsi rocanam \\\\

Verse: 37
