Sentence: a tat sūryasya devatvam tan mahitvam madhyā kartor vitataṁ sam jabhāra \\

Sentence: b yaded ayukta haritaḥ sadhasthād ād rātrī vāsas tanute simasmai \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a tan mitrasya varuṇasyābhicakṣe sūryo rūpam kṛṇute dyor upasthe \\

Sentence: b anantam anyad ruśad asya pājaḥ kṛṣṇam anyad dharitaḥ sam bharanti \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a baṇ mahāṁ asi sūrya baḍ āditya mahāṁ asi \\

Sentence: b mahas te sato mahimā panasyate ddhā deva mahāṁ asi \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a baṭ sūrya śravasā mahāṁ asi satrā deva mahāṁ asi \\

Sentence: b mahnā devānām asuryaḥ purohito vibhu jyotir adābhyam \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a śrāyanta iva sūryam viśved indrasya bhakṣata \\

Sentence: b vasūni jāte janamāna ojasā prati bhāgam na dīdhima \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a adyā devā uditā sūryasya nir aṁhasaḥ pipṛtā nir avadyāt \\

Sentence: b tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a ā kṛṣṇena rajasā vartamāno niveśayann amṛtam martyam ca \\

Sentence: b hiraṇyayena savitā rathenā devo yāti bhuvanāni paśyan \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a pra vāvṛje suprayā barhir eṣām ā viśpatīva bīriṭa iyāte \\

Sentence: b viśām aktor uṣasaḥ pūrvahūtau vāyuḥ pūṣā svastaye niyutvān \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a indravāyū bṛhaspatim mitrāgnim pūṣaṇam bhagam \\

Sentence: b ādityān mārutam gaṇam \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a varuṇaḥ prāvitā bhuvan mitro viśvābhir ūtibhiḥ \\

Sentence: b karatām naḥ surādhasaḥ \\\\

Verse: 47

Sentence: a adhi na indreṣām viṣṇo sajātyānām \\

Sentence: b itā maruto aśvinā \\

Sentence: c tam pratnathā \\

Sentence: d ayam venaḥ \\

Sentence: e ye devāsaḥ \\

Sentence: f ā na iḍābhiḥ \\

Sentence: g viśvebhiḥ somyam madhu \\

Sentence: h omāsaś carṣaṇīdhṛtaḥ \\\\

Verse: 48

Sentence: a agna indra varuṇa mitra devāḥ śardhaḥ pra yanta mārutota viṣṇo \\

Sentence: b ubhā nāsatyā rudro adha gnāḥ pūṣā bhagaḥ sarasvatī juṣanta \\\\

Verse: 49

Sentence: a indrāgnī mitrāvaruṇāditiṁ svaḥ pṛthivīm dyām marutaḥ parvatāṁ apaḥ \\

Sentence: b huve viṣṇum pūṣaṇam brahmaṇaspatim bhagam nu śaṁsaṁ savitāram ūtaye \\\\

Verse: 50

Sentence: a asme rudrā mehanā parvatāso vṛtrahatye bharahūtau sajoṣāḥ \\

Sentence: b yaḥ śaṁsate stuvate dhāyi pajra indrajyeṣṭhā asmāṁ avantu devāḥ \\\\

Verse: 51

Sentence: a arvāñco adyā bhavatā yajatrā ā vo hārdi bhayamāno vyayeyam \\

Sentence: b trādhvam no devā nijuro vṛkasya trādhvam kartād avapado yajatrāḥ \\\\

Verse: 52

Sentence: a viśve adya maruto viśva ūtī viśve bhavantv agnayaḥ samiddhāḥ \\

Sentence: b viśve no devā avasā gamantu viśvam astu draviṇam vājo asme \\\\

Verse: 53

Sentence: a viśve devāḥ śṛṇutemaṁ havam me ye antarikṣe ya upa dyavi ṣṭha \\

Sentence: b ye agnijihvā uta vā yajatrā āsadyāsmin barhiṣi mādayadhvam \\\\

Verse: 54

Sentence: a devebhyo hi prathamam yajñiyebhyo mṛtatvaṁ suvasi bhāgam uttamam \\

Sentence: b ād id dāmānaṁ savitar vy ūrṇuṣe nūcīnā jīvitā mānuṣebhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 55

Sentence: a pra vāyum acchā bṛhatī manīṣā bṛhadrayim viśvavāraṁ rathaprām \\

Sentence: b dyutadyāmā niyutaḥ patyamānaḥ kaviḥ kavim iyakṣasi prayajyo \\\\

Verse: 56

Sentence: a indravāyū ime sutā upa prayobhir āgatam \\

Sentence: b +indavo vām uśanti hi \\\\

Verse: 57

Sentence: a mitraṁ huve pūtadakṣam varuṇam ca riśādasam \\

Sentence: b dhiyam ghṛtācīṁ sādhantā \\\\

Verse: 58

Sentence: a dasrā yuvākavaḥ sutā nāsatyā vṛktabarhiṣaḥ \\

Sentence: b ā yātaṁ rudravartanī \\

Sentence: c tam pratnathā \\

Sentence: d ayam venaḥ \\\\

Verse: 59

Sentence: a vidad yadī saramā rugṇam adrer mahi pāthaḥ pūrvyaṁ sadhryak kaḥ \\

Sentence: b agram nayat supady akṣarāṇām acchā ravam prathamā jānatī gāt \\\\

Verse: 60

Sentence: a nahi spaśam avidann anyam asmād vaiśvānarāt puraetāram agneḥ \\

Sentence: b em enam avṛdhann amṛtā amartyam vaiśvānaram kṣaitrajityāya devāḥ \\\\

Verse: 61

Sentence: a ugrā vighaninā mṛdha indrāgnī havāmahe \\

Sentence: b tā no mṛḍāta īdṛśe \\\\

Verse: 62

Sentence: a upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ pavamānāyendave \\

Sentence: b abhi devāṁ iyakṣate \\\\

Verse: 63

Sentence: a ye tvāhihatye maghavann avardhan ye śāmbare harivo ye gaviṣṭau \\

Sentence: b ye tvā nūnam anumadanti viprāḥ pibendra somaṁ sagaṇo marudbhiḥ \\\\

Verse: 64

Sentence: a janiṣṭhā ugraḥ sahase turāya mandra ojiṣṭho bahulābhimānaḥ \\

Sentence: b avardhann indram marutaś cid atra mātā yad vīram dadhanad dhaniṣṭhā \\\\

Verse: 65

Sentence: a ā tū na indra vṛtrahann asmākam ardham ā gahi \\

Sentence: b mahān mahībhir ūtibhiḥ \\\\

Verse: 66

Sentence: a tvam indra pratūrtiṣv abhi viśvā asi spṛdhaḥ \\

Sentence: b aśastihā janitā viśvatūr asi tvam tūrya taruṣyataḥ \\\\

Verse: 67

Sentence: a anu te śuṣmam turayantam īyatuḥ kṣoṇī śiśum na mātarā \\

Sentence: b viśvās te spṛdhaḥ śnathayanta manyave vṛtram yad indra tūrvasi \\\\

Verse: 68

Sentence: a yajño devānām praty eti sumnam ādityāso bhavatā mṛḍayantaḥ \\

Sentence: b ā vo rvācī sumatir vavṛtyād aṁhoś cid yā varivovittarāsat \\\\

Verse: 69

Sentence: a adabdhebhiḥ savitaḥ pāyubhiṣ ṭvaṁ śivebhir adya pari pāhi no gayam \\

Sentence: b hiraṇyajihvaḥ suvitāya navyase rakṣā mākir no aghaśaṁsa īśata \\\\

Verse: 70

Sentence: a pra vīrayā śucayo dadrire vām adhvaryubhir madhumantaḥ sutāsaḥ \\

Sentence: b vaha vāyo niyuto yāhy acchā pibā sutasyāndhaso madāya \\\\

Verse: 71

Sentence: a gāva upāvatāvatam mahī yajñasya rapsudā \\

Sentence: b ubhā karṇā hiraṇyayā \\\\

Verse: 72

Sentence: a kāvyayor ājāneṣu kratvā dakṣasya duroṇe \\

Sentence: b riśādasā sadhastha ā \\\\

Verse: 73

Sentence: a daivyāv adhvaryū ā gataṁ rathena sūryatvacā \\

Sentence: b madhvā yajñaṁ sam añjāthe \\

Sentence: c tam pratnathā \\

Sentence: d ayam venaḥ \\\\

Verse: 74

Sentence: a tiraścīno vitato raśmir eṣām adhaḥ svid āsī3d upari svid āsī3t \\

Sentence: b retodhā āsan mahimāna āsant svadhā avastāt prayatiḥ parastāt \\\\

Verse: 75

Sentence: a ā rodasī apṛṇad ā svar mahaj jātam yad enam apaso adhārayan \\

Sentence: b so adhvarāya pari ṇīyate kavir atyo na vājasātaye canohitaḥ \\\\

Verse: 76

Sentence: a ukthebhir vṛtrahantamā yā mandānā cid ā girā \\

Sentence: b āṅgūṣair āvivāsataḥ \\\\

Verse: 77

Sentence: a upa naḥ sūnavo giraḥ śṛṇvantv amṛtasya ye \\

Sentence: b sumṛḍīkā bhavantu naḥ \\\\

Verse: 78

Sentence: a brahmāṇi me matayaḥ śaṁ sutāsaḥ śuṣma iyarti prabhṛto me adriḥ \\

Sentence: b ā śāsate prati haryanty ukthemā harī vahatas tā no accha \\\\

Verse: 79

Sentence: a anuttam ā te maghavan nakir nu na tvāvāṁ asti devatā vidānaḥ \\

Sentence: b na jāyamāno naśate na jāto yāni kariṣyā kṛṇuhi pravṛddha \\\\

Verse: 80

Sentence: a tad id āsa bhuvaneṣu jyeṣṭham yato jajña ugras tveṣanṛmṇaḥ \\

Sentence: b sadyo jajñāno ni riṇāti śatrūn anu yam viśve madanty ūmāḥ \\\\

Verse: 81

Sentence: a imā u tvā purūvaso giro vardhantu yā mama \\

Sentence: b pāvakavarṇāḥ śucayo vipaścito bhi stomair anūṣata \\\\

Verse: 82

Sentence: a yasyāyam viśva āryo dāsaḥ śevadhipā ariḥ \\

Sentence: b tiraś cid arye ruśame parīravi tubhyet so ajyate rayiḥ \\\\

Verse: 83

Sentence: a ayaṁ sahasram ṛṣibhiḥ sahaskṛtaḥ samudra iva paprathe \\

Sentence: b satyaḥ so asya mahimā gṛṇe śavo yajñeṣu viprarājye \\\\

Verse: 84

Sentence: a adabdhebhiḥ savitaḥ pāyubhiṣ ṭvaṁ śivebhir adya pari pāhi no gayam \\

Sentence: b hiraṇyajihvaḥ suvitāya navyase rakṣā mākir no aghaśaṁsa īśata \\\\

Verse: 85

Sentence: a ā no yajñam divispṛśam vāyo yāhi sumanmabhiḥ \\

Sentence: b antaḥ pavitra upari śrīṇāno yaṁ śukro ayāmi te \\\\

Verse: 86

Sentence: a indravāyū susamdṛśā suhaveha havāmahe \\

Sentence: b yathā naḥ sarva ij jano namīvaḥ samgame sumanā asat \\\\

Verse: 87

Sentence: a ṛdhag itthā sa martyaḥ śaśame devatātaye \\

Sentence: b yo nūnam mitrāvaruṇāv abhiṣṭaya ācakre havyadātaye \\\\

Verse: 88

Sentence: a ā yātam upa bhūṣatam madhvaḥ pibatam aśvinā \\

Sentence: b dugdham payo vṛṣaṇā jenyāvasū mā no mardhiṣṭam ā gatam \\\\

Verse: 89

Sentence: a praitu brahmaṇas patiḥ pra devy ṝtu sūnṛtā \\

Sentence: b acchā vīram naryam paṅktirādhasam devā yajñam nayantu naḥ \\\\

Verse: 90

Sentence: a candramā apsv antar ā suparṇo dhāvate divi \\

Sentence: b rayim piśamgam bahulam puruspṛhaṁ harir eti kanikradat \\\\

Verse: 91

Sentence: a devam-devam vo vase devam-devam abhiṣṭaye \\

Sentence: b devam-devaṁ huvema vājasātaye gṛṇanto devyā dhiyā \\\\

Verse: 92

Sentence: a divi pṛṣṭo arocatāgnir vaiśvānaro bṛhan \\

Sentence: b kṣmayā vṛdhāna ojasā canohito jyotiṣā bādhate tamaḥ \\\\

Verse: 93

Sentence: a indrāgnī apād iyam pūrvāgāt padvatībhyaḥ \\

Sentence: b hitvī śiro jihvayā vāvadac carat triṁśat padā ny akramīt \\\\

Verse: 94

Sentence: a devāso hi ṣmā manave samanyavo viśve sākaṁ sarātayaḥ \\

Sentence: b te no adya te aparam tuce tu no bhavantu varivovidaḥ \\\\

Verse: 95

Sentence: a apādhamad abhiśastīr aśastihāthendro dyumny ābhavat \\

Sentence: b devās ta indra sakhyāya yemire bṛhadbhāno marudgaṇa \\\\

Verse: 96

Sentence: a pra va indrāya bṛhate maruto brahmārcata \\

Sentence: b vṛtraṁ hanati vṛtrahā śatakratur vajreṇa śataparvaṇā \\\\

Verse: 97

Sentence: a asyed indro vāvṛdhe vṛṣṇyaṁ śavo made sutasya viṣṇavi \\

Sentence: b adyā tam asya mahimānam āyavo nu ṣṭuvanti pūrvathā \\

Sentence: c imā u tvā \\

Sentence: d yasyāyam \\

Sentence: e ayaṁ sahasram \\

Sentence: f ūrdhva ū ṣu ṇaḥ \\\\

## Paragraph: 34

Verse: 1

Sentence: a yaj jāgrato dūram udaiti daivam tad u suptasya tathaivaiti \\

Sentence: b dūramgamam jyotiṣām jyotir ekam tan me manaḥ śivasamkalpam astu \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a yena karmāṇy apaso manīṣiṇo yajñe kṛṇvanti vidatheṣu dhīrāḥ \\

Sentence: b yad apūrvam yakṣam antaḥ prajānām tan me manaḥ śivasamkalpam astu \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a yat prajñānam uta ceto dhṛtiś ca yaj jyotir antar amṛtam prajāsu \\

Sentence: b yasmān na ṛte kim cana karma kriyate tan me manaḥ śivasamkalpam astu \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a yenedam bhūtam bhuvanam bhaviṣyat parigṛhītam amṛtena sarvam \\

Sentence: b yena yajñas tāyate saptahotā tan me manaḥ śivasamkalpam astu \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a yasminn ṛcaḥ sāma yajūṁṣi yasmin pratiṣṭhitā rathanābhāv ivārāḥ \\

Sentence: b yasmiṁś cittaṁ sarvam otam prajānam tan me manaḥ śivasamkalpam astu \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a suṣārathir aśvān iva yan manuṣyān nenīyate bhīśubhir vājina iva \\

Sentence: b hṛtpratiṣṭham yad ajiram javiṣṭham tan me manaḥ śivasamkalpam astu \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a pitum nu stoṣam maho dharmāṇam taviṣīm \\

Sentence: b yasya trito vy ojasā vṛtram viparvam ardayat \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a anv id anumate tvam manyāsai śam ca nas kṛdhi \\

Sentence: b kratve dakṣāya no hinu pra ṇa āyūṁṣi tāriṣaḥ \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a anu no dyānumatir yajñam deveṣu manyatām \\

Sentence: b agniś ca havyavāhano bhavatam dāśuṣe mayaḥ \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a sinīvāli pṛthuṣṭuke yā devānām asi svasā \\

Sentence: b juṣasva havyam āhutam prajām devi didiḍḍhi naḥ \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a pañca nadyaḥ sarasvatīm api yanti sasrotasaḥ \\

Sentence: b sarasvatī tu pañcadhā so deśe bhavat sarit \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a tvam agne prathamo aṅgirā ṛṣir devo devānām abhavaḥ śivaḥ sakhā \\

Sentence: b tava vrate kavayo vidmanāpaso jāyanta maruto bhrājadṛṣṭayaḥ \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a tvam no agne tave deva pāyubhir maghono rakṣa tanvaś ca vandya \\

Sentence: b trātā tokasya tanaye gavām asy animeṣaṁ rakṣaṇas tava vrate \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a uttānāyām ava bharā cikitvānt sadyaḥ pravītā vṛṣaṇam jajāna \\

Sentence: b aruṣastūpo ruśad asya pāja iḍāyās putro vayune janiṣṭa \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a iḍāyās tvā pade vayam nābhā pṛthivyā adhi \\

Sentence: b jātavedo ni dhīmahy agne havyāya voḍhave \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a pra manmahe śavasānāya śūṣam āṅgūṣam girvaṇase aṅgirasvat \\

Sentence: b suvṛktibhi stuvata ṛgmiyāyārcāmārkam nare viśrutāya \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a pra vo mahe mahi namo bharadhvam āṅgūṣyaṁ śavasānāya sāma \\

Sentence: b yenā naḥ pūrve pitaraḥ padajñā arcanto aṅgiraso gā avindan \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a icchanti tvā somyāsaḥ sakhāyaḥ sunvanti somam dadhati prayāṁsi \\

Sentence: b titikṣante abhiśastim janānām indra tvad ā kaś cana hi praketaḥ \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a na te dūre paramā cid rajāṁsy asyā tu pra yāhi harivo haribhyām \\

Sentence: b sthirāya vṛṣṇe savanā kṛtemā yuktā grāvāṇaḥ samidhāne agnau \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a aṣāḍham yutsu pṛtanāsu papriṁ svarṣām apsām vṛjanasya gopām \\

Sentence: b bhareṣujāṁ sukṣitiṁ suśravasam jayantam tvām anu madema soma \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a somo dhenuṁ somo arvantam āśuṁ somo vīram karmaṇyam dadāti \\

Sentence: b sādanyam vidathyaṁ sabheyam pitṛśravaṇam yo dadāśad asmai \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a tvam imā oṣadhīḥ soma viśvās tvam apo ajanayas tvam gāḥ \\

Sentence: b tvam ā tatanthorv antarikṣam tvam jyotiṣā vi tamo vavartha \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a devena no manasā deva soma rāyo bhāgaṁ sahasāvann abhi yudhya \\

Sentence: b mā tvā tanad īśiṣe vīryasyobhayebhyaḥ pra cikitsā gaviṣṭau \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a aṣṭau vy akhyat kakubhaḥ pṛthivyās trī dhanva yojanā sapta sindhūn \\

Sentence: b hiraṇyākṣaḥ savitā deva āgād dadhad ratnā dāśuṣe vāryāṇi \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a hiraṇyapāṇiḥ savitā vicarṣaṇir ubhe dyāvāpṛthivī antar īyate \\

Sentence: b apāmīvām bādhate veti sūryam abhi kṛṣṇena rajasā dyām ṛṇoti \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a hiraṇyahasto asuraḥ sunīthaḥ sumṛḍīkaḥ svavā yātv arvāṅ \\

Sentence: b apasedhan rakṣaso yātudhānān asthād devaḥ pratidoṣam gṛṇānaḥ \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a ye te panthāḥ savitaḥ pūrvyāso reṇavaḥ sukṛtā antarikṣe \\

Sentence: b tebhir no adya pathibhiḥ sugebhī rakṣā ca no adhi ca brūhi deva \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a ubhā pibatam aśvinobhā naḥ śarma yacchatam \\

Sentence: b avidriyābhir ūtibhiḥ \\\\

Verse: 29

Sentence: a apnasvatīm aśvinā vācam asme kṛtam no dasrā vṛṣaṇā manīṣām \\

Sentence: b adyūtye vase ni hvaye vām vṛdhe ca no bhavatam vājasātau \\\\

Verse: 30

Sentence: a dyubhir aktubhiḥ pari pātam asmān ariṣṭebhir aśvinā saubhagebhiḥ \\

Sentence: b tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ \\\\

Verse: 31

Sentence: a ā kṛṣṇena rajasā vartamāno niveśayann amṛtam martyam ca \\

Sentence: b hiraṇyayena savitā rathenā devo yāti bhuvanāni paśyan \\\\

Verse: 32

Sentence: a ā rātri parthivaṁ rajaḥ pitur aprāyi dhamabhiḥ \\

Sentence: b divaḥ sadāṁsi bṛhatī vi tiṣṭhasa ā tveṣam vartate tamaḥ \\\\

Verse: 33

Sentence: a uṣas tac citram ā bharāsmabhyam vājinīvati \\

Sentence: b yena tokam ca tanayam ca dhāmahe \\\\

Verse: 34

Sentence: a prātar agnim prātar indraṁ havāmahe prātar mitrāvaruṇā prātar aśvinā \\

Sentence: b prātar bhagam pūṣaṇam brahmaṇaspatim prātaḥ somam uta rudraṁ huvema \\\\

Verse: 35

Sentence: a prātarjitam bhagam ugraṁ huvema vayam putram aditer yo vidhartā \\

Sentence: b ādhraś cid yam manyamānas turaś cid rājā cid yam bhagam bhakṣīty āha \\\\

Verse: 36

Sentence: a bhaga praṇetar bhaga satyarādho bhagemām dhiyam ud avā dadan naḥ \\

Sentence: b bhaga pra ṇo janaya gobhir aśvair bhaga pra nṛbhir nṛvantaḥ syāma \\\\

Verse: 37

Sentence: a utedānīm bhagavantaḥ syāmota prapitva uta madhye ahnām \\

Sentence: b utoditā maghavan sūryasya vayam devānāṁ sumatau syāma \\\\

Verse: 38

Sentence: a bhaga eva bhagavāṁ astu devās tena vayam bhagavantaḥ syāma \\

Sentence: b tam tvā bhaga sarva ij johavīti sa no bhaga puraetā bhaveha \\\\

Verse: 39

Sentence: a sam adhvarāyoṣaso namanta dadhikrāveva śucaye padāya \\

Sentence: b arvācīnam vasuvidam bhagam no ratham ivāśvā vājina ā vahantu \\\\

Verse: 40

Sentence: a aśvāvatīr gomatīr na uṣāso vīravatīḥ sadam ucchantu bhadrāḥ \\

Sentence: b ghṛtam duhānā viśvataḥ prapītā yūyam pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ \\\\

Verse: 41

Sentence: a pūṣan tava vrate vayam na riṣyema kadā cana \\

Sentence: b stotāras ta iha smasi \\\\

Verse: 42

Sentence: a pathas-pathaḥ paripatim vacasyā kāmena kṛto abhy ānaḍ arkam \\

Sentence: b sa no rāsac churudhaś candrāgrā dhiyam-dhiyaṁ sīṣadhāti pra pūṣā \\\\

Verse: 43

Sentence: a trīṇi padā vi cakrame viṣṇur gopā adābhyaḥ \\

Sentence: b ato dharmāṇi dhārayan \\\\

Verse: 44

Sentence: a tad viprāso vipanyavo jāgṛvāṁsaḥ sam indhate \\

Sentence: b viṣṇor yat paramam padam \\\\

Verse: 45

Sentence: a ghṛtavatī bhuvanānām abhiśriyorvī pṛthvī madhudughe supeśasā \\

Sentence: b dyāvāpṛthivī varuṇasya dharmaṇā viṣkabhite ajare bhūriretasā \\\\

Verse: 46

Sentence: a ye naḥ sapatnā apa te bhavantv indrāgnibhyām ava bādhāmahe tān \\

Sentence: b vasavo rudrā ādityā uparispṛśam mogram cettāram adhirājam akran \\\\

Verse: 47

Sentence: a ā nāsatyā tribhir ekādaśair iha devebhir yātam madhupeyam aśvinā \\

Sentence: b prāyus tāriṣṭam nī rapāṁsi mṛkṣataṁ sedhatam dveṣo bhavataṁ sacābhuvā \\\\

Verse: 48

Sentence: a eṣa va stomo maruta iyam gīr māndāryasya mānyasya kāroḥ \\

Sentence: b eṣā yāsīṣṭa tanvṝ vayām vidyāmeṣam vṛjanam jīradānum \\\\

Verse: 49

Sentence: a sahastomāḥ sahacchandasa āvṛtaḥ sahapramā ṛṣayaḥ sapta daivyāḥ \\

Sentence: b pūrveṣām panthām anudṛśya dhīrā anvālebhire rathyo na raśmīn \\\\

Verse: 50

Sentence: a āyuṣyam varcasyaṁ rāyaspoṣam audbhidam \\

Sentence: b idaṁ hiraṇyam varcasvaj jaitrāyā viśatād u mām \\\\

Verse: 51

Sentence: a na tad rakṣāṁsi na piśācās taranti devānām ojaḥ prathamajaṁ hy ṝtat \\

Sentence: b yo bibharti dākṣāyaṇaṁ hiraṇyaṁ sa deveṣu kṛṇute dīrgham āyuḥ sa manuṣyṝṣu kṛṇute dīrgham āyuḥ \\\\

Verse: 52

Sentence: a yad ābadhnan dākṣāyaṇā hiraṇyaṁ śatānīkāya sumanasyamānāḥ \\

Sentence: b tan ma ā badhnāmi śataśāradāyāyuṣmān jaradaṣṭir yathāsam \\\\

Verse: 53

Sentence: a uta no hir budhnyaḥ śṛṇotv aja ekapāt pṛthivī samudraḥ \\

Sentence: b viśve devā ṛtāvṛdho huvānā stutā mantrāḥ kaviśastā avantu \\\\

Verse: 54

Sentence: a imā gira ādityebhyo ghṛtasnūḥ sanād rājabhyo juhvā juhomi \\

Sentence: b śṛṇotu mitro aryamā bhago nas tuvijāto varuṇo dakṣo aṁśaḥ \\\\

Verse: 55

Sentence: a sapta ṛṣayaḥ pratihitāḥ śarīre sapta rakṣanti sadam apramādam \\

Sentence: b saptāpaḥ svapato lokam īyus tatra jāgṛto asvapnajau satradsadau ca devau \\\\

Verse: 56

Sentence: a ut tiṣṭha brahmaṇaspate devayantas tvemahe \\

Sentence: b upa pra yantu marutaḥ sudānava indra prāśūr bhavā sacā \\\\

Verse: 57

Sentence: a pra nūnam brahmaṇaspatir mantram vadaty ukthyam \\

Sentence: b yasminn indro varuṇo mitro aryamā devā okāṁsi cakrire \\\\

Verse: 58

Sentence: a brahmaṇaspate tvam asya yantā sūktasya bodhi tanayam ca jinva \\

Sentence: b viśvam tad bhadram yad avanti devā bṛhad vadema vidathe suvīrāḥ \\

Sentence: c ya imā viśvā \\

Sentence: d viśvakarmā \\

Sentence: e yo naḥ pitā \\

Sentence: f annapate nnasya no dehi \\\\

## Paragraph: 35

Verse: 1

Sentence: a apeto yantu paṇayo sumnā devapīyavaḥ \\

Sentence: b asya lokaḥ sutāvataḥ \\

Sentence: c dyubhir ahobhir aktubhir vyaktam yamo dadātv avasānam asmai \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a savitā te śarīrebhyaḥ pṛthivyām lokam icchatu \\

Sentence: b tasmai yujyantām usriyāḥ \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a vāyuḥ punātu \\

Sentence: b savitā punātu \\

Sentence: c agner bhrājasā \\

Sentence: d sūryasya varcasā \\

Sentence: e vi mucyantām usriyāḥ \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a aśvatthe vo niṣadanam parṇe vo vasatiṣ kṛtā \\

Sentence: b gobhāja it kilāsatha yat sanavatha pūruṣam \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a savitā te śarīrāṇi mātur upastha ā vapatu \\

Sentence: b tasmai pṛthivi śam bhava \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a prajāpatau tvā devatāyām upodake loke ni dadhāmy asau \\

Sentence: b apa naḥ śośucad agham \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a param mṛtyo anu parehi panthām yas te anya itaro devayānāt \\

Sentence: b cakṣuṣmate śṛṇvate te bravīmi mā naḥ prajāṁ rīriṣo mota vīrān \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a śam vātaḥ śaṁ hi te ghṛṇiḥ śam te bhavantv iṣṭakāḥ \\

Sentence: b śam te bhavantv agnayaḥ pārthivāso mā tvābhi śūśucan \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a kalpantām te diśas tubhyam āpaḥ śivatamās tubhyam bhavantu sindhavaḥ \\

Sentence: b antarikṣaṁ śivam tubhyam kalpantām te diśaḥ sarvāḥ \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a aśmanvatī rīyate saṁ rabhadhvam ut tiṣṭhata pra taratā sakhāyaḥ \\

Sentence: b atra jahīmo śivā ye asañchivān vayam uttaremābhi vājān \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a apāgham apa kilviṣam apa kṛtyām apo rapaḥ \\

Sentence: b apāmārga tvam asmad apa duḥṣvapnyaṁ suva \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a sumitriyā na āpa oṣadhayaḥ santu durmitriyās tasmai santu yo smān dveṣṭi yam ca vayam dviṣmaḥ \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a anaḍvāham ā rabhāmahe saurabheyaṁ svastaye \\

Sentence: b sa na indra iva devebhyo vahniḥ samtaraṇo bhava \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a ud vayam tamasas pari svaḥ paśyanta uttaram \\

Sentence: b devam devatrā sūryam aganma jyotir uttamam \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a imam jīvebhyaḥ paridhim dadhāmi maiṣām nu gād aparo artham etam \\

Sentence: b śatam jīvantu śaradaḥ purūcīr antar mṛtyum dadhatām parvatena \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a agna āyūṁṣi pavasva ā suvorjam iṣam ca naḥ \\

Sentence: b āre bādhasva ducchunām \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a āyuṣmān agne haviṣā vṛdhāno ghṛtaprsatīko ghṛtayonir edhi \\

Sentence: b ghṛtam pītvā madhu cāru gavyam piteva putram abhi rakṣatād imānt svāhā \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a parīme gām aneṣata pary agnim ahṛṣata \\

Sentence: b deveṣv akrata śravaḥ ka imāṁ ā dadharṣati \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a kravyādam agnim pra hiṇomi dūram yamarājyam gacchatu ripravāhaḥ \\

Sentence: b ihaivāyam itaro jātavedā devebhyo havyam vahatu prajānan \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a vaha vapām jātavedaḥ pitṛbhyo yatrainān vettha nihitān parāke \\

Sentence: b medasaḥ kulyā upa tānt sravantu satyā eṣām āśiṣaḥ sam namantāṁ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a syonā pṛthivi no bhavānṛkṣarā niveśanī \\

Sentence: b yacchā naḥ śarma saprathāḥ \\

Sentence: c apa naḥ śośucad agham \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a asmāt tvam adhi jāto si tvad ayam jāyatām punaḥ \\

Sentence: b asau svargāya lokāya svāhā \\\\

## Paragraph: 36

Verse: 1

Sentence: a ṛcam vācam pra padye mano yajuḥ pra padye sāma prāṇam pra padye cakṣuḥ śrotram pra padye \\

Sentence: b vāg ojaḥ sahaujo mayi prāṇāpānau \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a yan me chidram cakṣuṣo hṛdayasya manaso vātitṛṇam bṛhaspatir me tad dadhātu \\

Sentence: b śam no bhavatu bhuvanasya yas patiḥ \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a bhūr bhuvaḥ svaḥ \\

Sentence: b tat savitur vareṇyam bhargo devasya dhīmahi \\

Sentence: c dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayat \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a kayā naś citra ā bhuvad ūtī sadāvṛdhaḥ sakhā \\

Sentence: b kayā śaciṣṭhayā vṛtā \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a kas tvā satyo madānām maṁhiṣṭho matsad andhasaḥ \\

Sentence: b dṛḍhā cid āruje vasu \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a abhī ṣu ṇaḥ sakhīnām avitā jaritṝṇām \\

Sentence: b śatam bhavāsy ūtaye \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a kayā tvam na ūtyābhi pra mandase vṛṣan \\

Sentence: b kayā stotṛbhya ā bhara \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a indro viśvasya rājati \\

Sentence: b śam no astu dvipade śam catuṣpade \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a śam no mitraṁ śam varuṇaḥ śam no bhavatv aryamā \\

Sentence: b śam na indro bṛhaspatiḥ śam no viṣṇur urukramaḥ \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a śam no vātaḥ pavatāṁ śam nas tapatu sūryaḥ \\

Sentence: b śam naḥ kanikradad devaḥ parjanyo abhi varṣatu \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a ahāni śam bhavantu naḥ śaṁ rātrīḥ prati dhīyatām \\

Sentence: b śam na indrāgnī bhavatām avobhiḥ śam na indrāvaruṇā rātahavyā \\

Sentence: c śam na indrāpūṣaṇā vājasātau śam indrāsomā suvitāya śam yoḥ \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a śam no devīr abhiṣṭaya āpo bhavantu pītaye \\

Sentence: b śam yor abhi sravantu naḥ \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a syonā pṛthivi no bhavānṛkṣarā niveśanī \\

Sentence: b yacchā naḥ śarma saprathāḥ \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a āpo hi ṣṭhā mayobhuvas tā na ūrje dadhātana \\

Sentence: b mahe raṇāya cakṣase \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a yo vaḥ śivatamo rasas tasya bhājayateha naḥ \\

Sentence: b uśatīr iva mātaraḥ \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a tasmā aram gamāma vo yasya kṣayāya jinvatha \\

Sentence: b āpo janayathā ca naḥ \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a dyauḥ śāntir antarikṣaṁ śāntiḥ pṛthivī śāntir āpaḥ śāntir oṣadhayaḥ śāntiḥ \\

Sentence: b vanaspatayaḥ śāntir viśve devāḥ śāntir brahma śāntiḥ sarvaṁ śāntiḥ śāntir eva śāntiḥ sā mā śāntir edhi \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a dṛte dṛṁha mā mitrasya mā cakṣuṣā sarvāṇi bhūtāni sam īkṣantām \\

Sentence: b mitrasyāham cakṣuṣā sarvāṇi bhūtāni samīkṣe \\

Sentence: c mitrasya cakṣuṣā sam īkṣāmahe \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a dṛte dṛṁha mā \\

Sentence: b jyok te samdṛśi jīvyāsam jyok te samdṛśi jīvyāsam \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a namas te harase śociṣe namas te astv arciṣe \\

Sentence: b anyāṁs te asmat tapantu hetayaḥ pāvako asmabhyaṁ śivo bhava \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a namas te astu vidyute namas te stanayitnave \\

Sentence: b namas te bhagavann astu yataḥ svaḥ samīhase \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a yato-yataḥ samīhase tato no abhayam kuru \\

Sentence: b śam naḥ kuru prajābhyo bhayam naḥ paśubhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a sumitriyā na āpa oṣadhayaḥ santu +durmitriyās tasmai santu yo smān dveṣṭi yam ca vayam dviṣmaḥ \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a tac cakṣur devahitam purastāc chukram uc carat \\

Sentence: b paśyema śaradaḥ śatam jīvema śaradaḥ śataṁ śṛṇuyāma śaradaḥ śatam pra bravāma śaradaḥ śatam adīnāḥ syāma śaradaḥ śatam bhūyaś ca śaradaḥ śatāt \\\\

## Paragraph: 37

Verse: 1

Sentence: a devasya tvā savituḥ prasavṝ śvinor bāhubhyām pūṣṇo hastābhyām \\

Sentence: b ā dade nārir asi \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a yuñjate mana uta yuñjate dhiyo viprā viprasya bṛhato vipaścitaḥ \\

Sentence: b vi hotrā dadhe vayunāvid eka in mahī devasya savituḥ pariṣṭutiḥ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a devī dyāvāpṛthivī makhasya vām adya śiro rādhyāsam devayajane pṛthivyāḥ \\

Sentence: b makhāya tvā makhasya tvā śīrṣṇe \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a devyo vamryo bhūtasya prathamajā makhasya vo dya śiro rādhyāsam devayajane pṛthivyāḥ \\

Sentence: b makhāya tvā makhasya tvā śīrṣṇe \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a iyaty agre āsīn makhasya te dya śiro rādhyāsam devayajane pṛthivyāḥ \\

Sentence: b makhāya tvā makhasya tvā śīrṣṇe \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a indrasyauja stha makhasya vo dya śiro rādhyāsam devayajane pṛthivyāḥ \\

Sentence: b makhāya tvā makhasya tvā śīrṣṇe makhāya tvā makhasya tvā śīrṣṇe makhāya tvā makhasya tvā śīrṣṇe \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a praitu brahmaṇas patiḥ pra devy ṝtu sūnṛtā \\

Sentence: b acchā vīram naryam paṅktirādhasam devā yajñam nayantu naḥ \\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a yamāya tvā \\

Sentence: b makhāya tvā \\

Sentence: c sūryasya tvā tapase \\

Sentence: d devas tvā savitā madhvānaktu \\

Sentence: e pṛthivyāḥ saṁspṛśas pāhi \\

Sentence: f arcir asi śocir asi tapo si \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a anādhṛṣṭā purastād agner ādhipatya āyur me dāḥ \\

Sentence: b putravato dakṣiṇata indrasyādhipatye prajām me dāḥ \\

Sentence: c suṣadā paścād devasya savitur ādhipatye cakṣur me dāḥ \\

Sentence: d āsrutir uttarato dhātur ādhipatye rāyaspoṣam me dāḥ \\

Sentence: e vidhṛtir upariṣṭād bṛhaspater ādhipatye ojo me dāḥ \\

Sentence: f viśvābhyo mā nāṣṭrābhyas pāhi \\

Sentence: g manor aśvāsi \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a svāhā marudbhiḥ pari śrīyasva \\

Sentence: b divaḥ saṁspṛśas pāhi \\

Sentence: c madhu madhu madhu \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a g devānām pitā matīnām patiḥ prajānām \\

Sentence: b sam devo devena savitrā gata saṁ sūryeṇa rocate \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a sam agnir agninā gata sam daivena savitrā saṁ sūryeṇārociṣṭa \\

Sentence: b svāhā sam agnis tapasā gata sam daivyena savitrā saṁ sūryeṇārūrucata \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a dhartā divo vi bhāti tapasas pṛthivyām dhartā devo devānām amartyas tapojāḥ \\

Sentence: b vācam asme ni yaccha devāyuvam \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a apaśyam gopām anipadyamānam ā ca parā ca pathibhiś carantam \\

Sentence: b sa sadhrīcīḥ sa viṣūcīr vasāna ā varīvartti bhuvaneṣv antaḥ \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a viśvāsām bhuvām pate viśvasya manasas pate viśvasya vacasas pate sarvasya vacasas pate \\

Sentence: b devaśrut tvam deva gharma devo devān pāhi \\

Sentence: c atra prāvīr anu vām devavītaye \\

Sentence: d madhu mādhvībhyām madhu mādhūcībhyām \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a hṛde tvā manase tvā dive tvā sūryāya tvā \\

Sentence: b ūrdhvo adhvaram divi deveṣu dhehi \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a pitā no si pitā no bodhi namas te astu mā mā hiṁsīḥ \\

Sentence: b tvaṣṭṛmantas tvā sapema putrān paśūn mayi dhehi prajām asmāsu dhehy ariṣṭāhaṁ sahapatyā bhūyāsam \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a ahaḥ ketunā juṣatāṁ sujyotir jyotiṣā svāhā \\

Sentence: b rātriḥ ketunā juṣatāṁ sujyotir jyotiṣā svāhā \\\\

## Paragraph: 38

Verse: 1

Sentence: a devasya tvā savituḥ prasavṝ śvinor bāhubhyām pūṣṇo hastābhyām \\

Sentence: b ā dade dityai rāsnāsi \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a iḍa ehi \\

Sentence: b adita ehi \\

Sentence: c sarasvatty ehi \\

Sentence: d asāv ehi \\

Sentence: e asāv ehi \\

Sentence: f asāv ehi \\\\

Sentence: b pūṣāsi \\

Sentence: c gharmāya dīṣva \\\\

Verse: 4

Sentence: a aśvibhyām pinvasva \\

Sentence: b sarasvat pinvasva \\

Sentence: c indrāya pinvasva \\

Sentence: d svāhendravat \\

Sentence: e svāhendravat \\

Sentence: f svāhendravat \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a yas te stanaḥ śaśayo yo mayobhūr yo ratnadhā vasuvid yaḥ sudatraḥ \\

Sentence: b yena viśvā puṣyasi vāryāṇi sarasvat tam iha dhātave kaḥ \\

Sentence: c urv antarikṣam anv emi \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a gāyatram chando si \\

Sentence: b traiṣṭubham chando si \\

Sentence: c dyāvāpṛthivībhyām tvā pari gṛhṇāmi \\

Sentence: d antarikṣeṇopa yacchāmi \\

Sentence: e indrāśvinā \\

Sentence: f madhunaḥ sāraghasya gharmam pāta vasavo yajata vāṭ \\

Sentence: g svāhā sūryasya raśmaye vṛṣṭivanaye \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a samudrāya tvā vātāya svāhā \\

Sentence: b sarirāya tvā vātāya svāhā \\

Sentence: c anādhṛṣyāya tvā vātāya svāhā \\

Sentence: d apratidhṛṣyāya tvā vātāya svāhā \\

Sentence: e avasyave tvā vātāya svāhā \\

Sentence: f aśimidāya tvā vātāya svāhā \\\\

Sentence: a indrāya tvā vasumate rudravate svāhā \\

Sentence: b indrāya tvādityavate svāhā \\

Sentence: c indrāya tvābhimātighne svāhā \\

Sentence: d savitre tva ṛbhumate vibhumate vājavate svāhā \\

Sentence: e bṛhaspataye tvā viśvadevyāvate svāhā \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a yamāya tvāṅgirasvate pitṛmate svāhā \\

Sentence: b svāhā gharmāya \\

Sentence: c svāhā gharmaḥ pitre \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a viśvā āśā dakṣiṇasad viśvān devān ayāḍ iha \\

Sentence: b svāhākṛtasya gharmasya madhoḥ pibatam aśvinā \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a divi dhā imam yajñam imam yajñam divi dhāḥ \\

Sentence: b svāhāgnaye yajñiyāya śam yajurbhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a aśvinā gharmam pātaṁ hārdvānam ahar divābhir ūtibhiḥ \\

Sentence: b tantrāyiṇo namo dyāvāpṛthivībhyām \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a apātām aśvinā gharmam anu dyāvāpṛthivī amaṁsātām \\

Sentence: b ihaiva rātayaḥ santu \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a iṣe pinvasva \\

Sentence: b ūrje pinvasva \\

Sentence: c brahmaṇe pinvasva \\

Sentence: d kṣatrāya pinvasva \\

Sentence: e dyāvāpṛthivībhyām pinvasva \\

Sentence: f dharmāsi sudharma \\

Sentence: g ameny asme nṛmṇāni dhāraya brahma dhāraya kṣatram dhāraya viṣam dhāraya \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a svāhā pūṣṇe śarase \\

Sentence: b svāhā grāvabhyaḥ \\

Sentence: c svāhā pratiravebhyaḥ \\

Sentence: d svāhā pitṛbhya ūrdhvabarhirbhyo gharmapāvabhyaḥ \\

Sentence: e svāhā dyāvāpṛthivībhyām \\

Sentence: f svāhā viśvebhyaḥ devebhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a svāhā rudrāya rudrahūtaye \\

Sentence: b svāhā sam jyotiṣā jyotiḥ \\

Sentence: c ahaḥ ketunā juṣatāṁ sujyotir jyotiṣā svāhā \\

Sentence: d rātriḥ ketunā juṣatāṁ sujyotir jyotiṣā svāhā \\

Sentence: e madhu hutam indratame agnāv aśyāma te deva gharma namas te astu mā mā hiṁsīḥ \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a abhīmam mahimā divam vipro babhūva saprathāḥ \\

Sentence: b uta śravasā pṛthivīṁ saṁ sīdasva mahāṁ asi rocasva devavītamaḥ \\\\

Verse: 18

Sentence: a yā te gharma divyā śug yā gāyatryāṁ havirdhāne \\

Sentence: b sā ta ā pyāyatām niṣ pyāyatām tasyai te svāhā \\

Sentence: c yā te gharmāntarikṣe śug yā triṣṭubhy āgnīdhre \\

Sentence: d sā ta ā pyāyatām niṣ pyāyatām tasyai te svāhā \\

Sentence: e yā te gharma pṛthivyāṁ śug yā jagatyāṁ sadasyā \\

Sentence: f sā ta ā pyāyatām niṣ pyāyatām tasyai te svāhā \\\\

Verse: 19

Sentence: a kṣatrasya tvā paraspāya brahmaṇas tanvam pāhi \\

Sentence: b viśas tvā dharmaṇā vayam anu krāmāma suvitāya navyase \\\\

Verse: 20

Sentence: a catuḥsraktir nābhir ṛtasya saprathāḥ sa no viśvāyuḥ saprathāḥ sa naḥ sarvāyuḥ saprathāḥ \\

Sentence: b apa dveṣo apa hvaro nyavratasya saścima \\\\

Verse: 21

Sentence: a gharmaitat te purīṣam tena vardhasva cā ca pyāyasva \\

Sentence: b vardhiṣīmahi ca vayam ā ca pyāsiṣīmahi \\\\

Verse: 22

Sentence: a acikradad vṛṣā harir mahān mitro na darśataḥ \\

Sentence: b saṁ sūryeṇa didyutad udadhir nidhiḥ \\\\

Verse: 23

Sentence: a sumitriyā na āpa oṣadhayaḥ santu +durmitriyās tasmai santu yo smān dveṣṭi yam ca vayam dviṣmaḥ \\\\

Verse: 24

Sentence: a ud vayam tamasas pari svaḥ paśyanta uttaram \\

Sentence: b devam devatrā sūryam aganma jyotir uttamam \\\\

Verse: 25

Sentence: a edho sy edhiṣīmahi \\

Sentence: b samid asi tejo si tejo mayi dhehi \\\\

Verse: 26

Sentence: a yāvato dyāvāpṛthivī yāvac ca sapta sindhavo vitasthire \\

Sentence: b tavantam indra te graham ūrjā gṛhṇāmy akṣitam mayi gṛhṇāmy akṣitam \\\\

Verse: 27

Sentence: a mayi tyad indriyam bṛhan mayi dakṣo mayi kratuḥ \\

Sentence: b gharmas triśug vi rājati virājā jyotiṣā saha brahmaṇā tejasā saha \\\\

Verse: 28

Sentence: a payaso reta ābhṛtam tasya doham aśīmahy uttarām-uttarāṁ samām \\

Sentence: b tviṣaḥ samvṛk kratve dakṣasya te suṣuvāṇasya te suṣumṇāgnihutaḥ \\

Sentence: c indrapītasya prajāpatibhakṣitasya madhumata upahūta upahūtasya bhakṣayāmi \\\\

Sentence: a prajāpatiḥ sambhriyamāṇaḥ samrāṭ sambhṛto vaiśvadevaḥ saṁsanno gharmaḥ pravṛktas teja udyata āśvinaḥ payasyānīyamāne pauṣṇo viṣpandamāne mārutaḥ klathan \\

Sentence: b maitraḥ śarasi samtāyyamāne vāyavyo hriyamāṇa āgneyo hūyamāno vāg ghutaḥ \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a savitā prathame hann agnir dvitīye vāyus tṛtīya ādityaś caturthe candramāḥ pañcama ṛtuḥ ṣaṣṭhe marutaḥ saptame bṛhaspatir aṣṭame \\

Verse: 7

Sentence: b sāsahvāṁś cābhiyugvā ca vikṣipaḥ svāhā \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a agniṁ hṛdayenāśaniṁ hṛdayāgreṇa paśupatim kṛtsnahṛdayena bhavam yaknā \\

Sentence: b śarvam matasnābhyām īśānam manyunā mahādevam antaḥparśavyenogram devam vaniṣṭhunā vasiṣṭhahanuḥ śiṅgīni kośyābhyām \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a ugram lohitena mitraṁ sauvratyena rudram daurvratyenendram prakrīḍena maruto balena sādhyān pramudā \\

Sentence: b bhavasya kaṇṭhaṁ rudrasyāntaḥpārśvam mahādevasya yakṛc charvasya vaniṣṭhuḥ paśupateḥ purītat \\\\

Sentence: b māṁsebhyaḥ svāhā māṁsebhyaḥ svāhā snāvabhyaḥ svāhā snāvabhyaḥ svāhāsthabhyaḥ svāhāsthabhyaḥ svāhā majjabhyaḥ svāhā majjabhyaḥ svāhā \\

Sentence: c retase svāhā pāyave svāhā \\\\

Sentence: b śuce svāhā śocate svāhā śocamānāya svāhā śokāya svāhā \\\\

Verse: 12

## Paragraph: 40

Verse: 1

Sentence: a īśā vāsyam idaṁ sarvam yat kim ca jagatyām jagat \\

Sentence: b tena tyaktena bhuñjīthā mā gṛdhaḥ kasya svid dhanam \\\\

Verse: 2

Sentence: a kurvann eveha karmāṇi jijīviṣec chataṁ samāḥ \\

Sentence: b evam tvayi nānyatheto sti na karma lipyate nare \\\\

Verse: 3

Sentence: a asuryā nāma te lokā andhena tamasāvṛtāḥ \\

Sentence: b tāṁs te pretyāpi gacchanti ye ke cātmahano janāḥ \\\\

\*

Verse: 4

Sentence: a anejad ekam manaso javīyo nainad devā āpnuvan pūrvam arśat \\

Sentence: b tad dhāvato nyān aty eti tiṣṭhat tasminn apo mātariśvā dadhāti \\\\

Verse: 5

Sentence: a tad ejati tan naijati tad dūre tad v antike \\

Sentence: b tad antar asya sarvasya tad u saravasyāsya bāhyataḥ \\\\

Verse: 6

Sentence: a yas tu sarvāṇi bhūtāny ātmann evānupaśyati \\

Sentence: b sarvabhūteṣu cātmānam tato na vi cikitsati \\\\

Verse: 7

Sentence: a yasmint sarvāṇi bhūtāny ātmaivābhūd vijānataḥ \\

Sentence: b tatra ko mohaḥ kaḥ śoka ekatvam anupaśyataḥ \\\\

Verse: 8

Sentence: a sa pary agāc chukram akāyam avraṇam asnāviraṁ śuddham apāpaviddham \\

Sentence: b kavir manīṣī paribhūḥ svayambhūr yāthātathyato rthān vy adadhāc chāśvatībhyaḥ samābhyaḥ \\\\

Verse: 9

Sentence: a andham tamaḥ pra viśanti ye sambhūtim upāsate \\

Sentence: b tato bhūya iva te tamo ya u sambhūtyāṁ ratāḥ \\\\

Verse: 10

Sentence: a anyad evāhuḥ sambhavād anyad āhur śambhavāt \\

Sentence: b iti śuśruma dhīrāṇām ye nas tad vicacakṣire \\\\

Verse: 11

Sentence: a sambhūtim ca vināśam ca yas tad vedobhayaṁ saha \\

Sentence: b vināśena mṛtyum tīrtvā sambhūtyāmṛtam aśnute \\\\

Verse: 12

Sentence: a andham tamaḥ pra viśanti ye vidyām upāsate \\

Sentence: b tato bhūya iva te tamo ya u vidyāyāṁ ratāḥ \\\\

Verse: 13

Sentence: a anyad evāhur vidyāyā anyad āhur avidyāyāḥ \\

Sentence: b iti śuśruma dhīrāṇām ye nas tad vicacakṣire \\\\

Verse: 14

Sentence: a vidyām cāvidyām ca yas tad vedobhayaṁ saha \\

Sentence: b avidyayā mṛtyum tīrtvā vidyayāmṛtam aśnute \\\\

Verse: 15

Sentence: a vāyur anilam amṛtam athedam bhasmāntaṁ śarīram \\

Sentence: b o3m krato smara klibe smara kṛtaṁ smara \\\\

Verse: 16

Sentence: a agne naya supathā rāye asmān viśvāni deva vayunāni vidvān \\

Sentence: b yuyodhy asmaj juhurāṇam eno bhuyiṣṭhām te namaūktim vidhema \\\\

Verse: 17

Sentence: a hiraṇmayena pātreṇa satyasyāpihitam mukham \\

Sentence: b yo sāv āditye puruṣaḥ so sāv aham \\

Sentence: c o3m kham brahma \\\\
